'backend\app\agent\catalog_refs.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import difflib
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel

from .contracts.catalog import Catalog, split_column_ref
from .contracts.tools import CATALOG_KEY
from .llm.structured import model_schema


def catalog_fields(args_model: type[BaseModel]) -> dict[str, str]:
    props = args_model.model_json_schema().get("properties", {})
    return {name: prop[CATALOG_KEY] for name, prop in props.items() if CATALOG_KEY in prop}


def _inject_enum(node: dict[str, Any], values: list[str]) -> None:
    if node.get("type") == "string":
        node["enum"] = values
    elif node.get("type") == "array" and isinstance(node.get("items"), dict):
        _inject_enum(node["items"], values)
    for branch in node.get("anyOf", []):
        _inject_enum(branch, values)


def build_args_schema(args_model: type[BaseModel], tables: list[str], columns: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    schema = model_schema(args_model)

    def walk(node: Any) -> None:
        if isinstance(node, list):
            for item in node:
                walk(item)
            return
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            return
        kind = node.pop(CATALOG_KEY, None)
        if kind == "table" and tables:
            _inject_enum(node, tables)
        elif kind == "column" and columns:
            _inject_enum(node, columns)
        for value in node.values():
            walk(value)

    walk(schema)
    return schema


def _closest(value: str, allowed: list[str]) -> str | None:
    lowered = {a.lower(): a for a in allowed}
    if value.lower() in lowered:
        return lowered[value.lower()]
    match = difflib.get_close_matches(value.lower(), list(lowered), n=1, cutoff=0.75)
    return lowered[match[0]] if match else None


def _fix_column(value: str, allowed: list[str]) -> str:
    if value in allowed:
        return value
    by_column = [ref for ref in allowed if split_column_ref(ref)[1].lower() == value.lower()]
    if len(by_column) == 1:
        return by_column[0]
    return _closest(value, allowed) or value


def fix_catalog_refs(data: Any, fields: dict[str, str], tables: list[str], columns: list[str]) -> Any:
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return data
    fixed = dict(data)
    for name, kind in fields.items():
        value = fixed.get(name)
        allowed = tables if kind == "table" else columns
        fix = (lambda v: _closest(v, allowed) or v) if kind == "table" else (lambda v: _fix_column(v, allowed))
        if isinstance(value, str):
            fixed[name] = fix(value)
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            fixed[name] = [fix(v) if isinstance(v, str) else v for v in value]
    return fixed


def check_catalog_refs(args: BaseModel, fields: dict[str, str], catalog: Catalog) -> list[str]:
    tables = {t.name for t in catalog.tables}
    errors = []
    for name, kind in fields.items():
        value = getattr(args, name, None)
        values = value if isinstance(value, list) else [value] if isinstance(value, str) else []
        for v in values:
            ok = v in tables if kind == "table" else catalog.resolve_column(v) is not None
            if not ok:
                allowed = sorted(tables) if kind == "table" else catalog.column_refs()
                hints = difflib.get_close_matches(v, allowed, n=3, cutoff=0.4) or allowed[:8]
                errors.append(f"{name}: «{v}» нет в данных, допустимые варианты: {', '.join(hints)}")
    return errors


'backend\app\agent\chart_planner.py':
"""Chart-first planning: LLM proposes charts before query generation."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from .contracts.catalog import Catalog
from .contracts.response import ChartType
from .llm.client import LLMClient, system_msg, user_msg
from .llm.structured import generate_structured, model_schema
from .schema_linking import render_schema

CHART_PLANNER_SYSTEM = """Ты планировщик визуализаций в BI-системе.
По вопросу пользователя и схеме данных определи, какие графики лучше всего отвечают.
Правила:
- Верни от 1 до 3 графиков.
- Для каждого укажи chart_type, title (краткий, на русском) и data_requirement —
  что должно быть в результате: оси, метрики, фильтры, единицы. На русском.
- Не придумывай колонки, которых нет в схеме.
- Одно число (итог, сумма) → kpi. Динамика во времени → line. Сравнение категорий → bar.
  Связь двух чисел → scatter. Точные значения/список → table.
- Если вопрос простой и требует одного графика — верни один график.

Схема данных:
{schema}
"""


class ChartPlanItem(BaseModel):
    chart_type: ChartType
    title: str = Field(description="Короткий заголовок на русском")
    data_requirement: str = Field(
        description="Что должно быть в результате: оси, метрики, фильтры. На русском."
    )


class ChartPlan(BaseModel):
    charts: list[ChartPlanItem] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=3)


async def plan_charts(
    llm: LLMClient,
    question: str,
    catalog: Catalog,
    *,
    allowed_types: set[ChartType],
    max_attempts: int = 3,
    max_tables: int = 3,
    max_columns: int = 60,
) -> ChartPlan:
    table_names = [t.name for t in catalog.tables][:max_tables]
    schema_doc = render_schema(catalog, table_names, max_columns=max_columns)
    prompt = CHART_PLANNER_SYSTEM.format(schema=schema_doc)

    schema = model_schema(ChartPlan)
    # Ограничиваем enum тем, что реально умеет рендерер
    schema["properties"]["charts"]["items"]["properties"]["chart_type"] = {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": sorted(t.value for t in allowed_types),
    }
    return await generate_structured(
        llm,
        [system_msg(prompt), user_msg(question)],
        ChartPlan,
        schema=schema,
        schema_name="chart_plan",
        max_attempts=max_attempts,
        validate=lambda p: [
            f"chart_type {c.chart_type.value!r} не поддерживается"
            for c in p.charts if c.chart_type not in allowed_types
        ],
    )

'backend\app\agent\config.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any


class StructuredMode(StrEnum):
    JSON_SCHEMA = "json_schema"
    JSON_OBJECT = "json_object"
    PROMPT = "prompt"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LLMConfig:
    base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434/v1"
    model: str = "qwen2.5:7b-instruct"
    api_key: str = "not-needed"
    structured_mode: StructuredMode = StructuredMode.JSON_SCHEMA
    temperature: float = 0.0
    timeout_s: float = 120.0
    max_retries: int = 2
    backoff_base_s: float = 1.0

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls, prefix: str = "LLM_") -> LLMConfig:
        env = os.environ
        default = cls()
        return cls(
            base_url=env.get(f"{prefix}BASE_URL", default.base_url),
            model=env.get(f"{prefix}MODEL", default.model),
            api_key=env.get(f"{prefix}API_KEY", default.api_key),
            structured_mode=StructuredMode(env.get(f"{prefix}STRUCTURED_MODE", default.structured_mode)),
            temperature=float(env.get(f"{prefix}TEMPERATURE", default.temperature)),
            timeout_s=float(env.get(f"{prefix}TIMEOUT_S", default.timeout_s)),
            max_retries=int(env.get(f"{prefix}MAX_RETRIES", default.max_retries)),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentConfig:
    llm: LLMConfig = field(default_factory=LLMConfig)
    sql_llm: LLMConfig | None = None
    max_structured_attempts: int = 3
    max_tool_repairs: int = 2
    clarify_threshold: float = 0.45
    history_turns: int = 4
    max_schema_tables: int = 3
    max_schema_columns: int = 60
    tool_timeout_s: float = 60.0
    preview_rows: int = 20
    stored_rows: int = 500
    max_narrative_payload_chars: int = 6000
    chart_types: frozenset[Any] | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls) -> AgentConfig:
        sql = LLMConfig.from_env("SQL_LLM_") if os.environ.get("SQL_LLM_MODEL") else None
        return cls(llm=LLMConfig.from_env(), sql_llm=sql)

'backend\app\agent\contracts\catalog.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator


class SemanticType(StrEnum):
    ID = "id"
    CATEGORY = "category"
    NUMERIC = "numeric"
    DATETIME = "datetime"
    DATE = "date"
    BOOLEAN = "boolean"
    TEXT = "text"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


TIME_TYPES = frozenset({SemanticType.DATETIME, SemanticType.DATE})


class ValueCount(BaseModel):
    value: str
    count: int


class ColumnInfo(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(description="Физическое имя колонки в БД, именно оно идёт в SQL")
    display_name: str | None = Field(None, description="Исходный заголовок из CSV, если отличается от name")
    db_type: str = Field(description="Тип в СУБД: text, bigint, numeric, timestamp ...")
    semantic_type: SemanticType = SemanticType.UNKNOWN
    description: str | None = Field(None, description="Описание простым языком (автоматическое или ручное)")
    unit: str | None = Field(None, description="Единица измерения: руб., шт., %, ч")
    null_share: float | None = Field(None, ge=0, le=1)
    distinct_count: int | None = None
    min_value: str | None = None
    max_value: str | None = None
    top_values: list[ValueCount] = Field(default_factory=list, description="До 10 самых частых значений")
    sample_values: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="До 5 примеров значений")

    @property
    def label(self) -> str:
        return self.display_name or self.name


class TableInfo(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(description="Физическое имя таблицы в БД, без точек")
    display_name: str | None = Field(None, description="Исходное имя файла или человекочитаемое название")
    description: str | None = None
    row_count: int
    columns: list[ColumnInfo]
    primary_key: str | None = None
    time_column: str | None = Field(None, description="Основная колонка даты, если есть")
    period_start: str | None = None
    period_end: str | None = None

    @field_validator("name")
    @classmethod
    def _no_dots(cls, v: str) -> str:
        if "." in v:
            raise ValueError("имя таблицы не должно содержать точку: агент адресует колонки как «таблица.колонка»")
        return v

    @property
    def label(self) -> str:
        return self.display_name or self.name

    def column(self, name: str) -> ColumnInfo | None:
        return next((c for c in self.columns if c.name == name), None)


class Relation(BaseModel):
    left_table: str
    left_column: str
    right_table: str
    right_column: str
    overlap_share: float = Field(ge=0, le=1, description="Доля значений left, найденных в right")
    cardinality: Literal["one_to_one", "one_to_many", "many_to_one", "many_to_many", "unknown"] = "unknown"


def column_ref(table: str, column: str) -> str:
    return f"{table}.{column}"


def split_column_ref(ref: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    table, _, column = ref.partition(".")
    return table, column


class Catalog(BaseModel):
    tables: list[TableInfo] = Field(default_factory=list)
    relations: list[Relation] = Field(default_factory=list)
    version: str = Field("", description="Меняется при каждой загрузке или изменении данных")

    def table(self, name: str) -> TableInfo | None:
        return next((t for t in self.tables if t.name == name), None)

    def column_refs(self, tables: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str]:
        return [
            column_ref(t.name, c.name) for t in self.tables if tables is None or t.name in tables for c in t.columns
        ]

    def resolve_column(self, ref: str) -> tuple[TableInfo, ColumnInfo] | None:
        table_name, column_name = split_column_ref(ref)
        table = self.table(table_name)
        column = table.column(column_name) if table else None
        return (table, column) if table and column else None


class CatalogProvider(Protocol):
    async def get_catalog(self) -> Catalog: ...


class QueryResult(BaseModel):
    columns: list[str]
    rows: list[list[Any]]
    row_count: int
    truncated: bool = False
    elapsed_ms: int = 0


class QueryError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, sqlstate: str | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.sqlstate = sqlstate


class QueryExecutor(Protocol):
    async def execute(
        self,
        sql: str,
        *,
        params: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
        max_rows: int = 10_000,
        timeout_s: float = 30.0,
    ) -> QueryResult: ...


'backend\app\agent\contracts\dashboard.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any, Protocol

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from ..intents import Intent
from .response import ChartSpec, ResultTable


class DashboardWidget(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    question: str
    intent: Intent
    tool: str
    args: dict[str, Any]
    chart: ChartSpec | None = None
    data: ResultTable | None = None
    summary: str = ""


class Dashboard(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    session_id: str
    request: str
    widgets: list[DashboardWidget]
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC))
    refreshed_at: datetime | None = None


class DashboardStore(Protocol):
    async def save(self, dashboard: Dashboard) -> None: ...

    async def get(self, dashboard_id: str) -> Dashboard | None: ...

    async def list(self, session_id: str | None = None) -> list[Dashboard]: ...


class InMemoryDashboardStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._items: dict[str, Dashboard] = {}

    async def save(self, dashboard: Dashboard) -> None:
        self._items[dashboard.id] = dashboard.model_copy(deep=True)

    async def get(self, dashboard_id: str) -> Dashboard | None:
        d = self._items.get(dashboard_id)
        return d.model_copy(deep=True) if d else None

    async def list(self, session_id: str | None = None) -> list[Dashboard]:
        return [d for d in self._items.values() if session_id is None or d.session_id == session_id]


'backend\app\agent\contracts\response.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Annotated, Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from ..intents import Intent
from .catalog import SemanticType


class ChartType(StrEnum):
    BAR = "bar"
    LINE = "line"
    AREA = "area"
    PIE = "pie"
    SCATTER = "scatter"
    HISTOGRAM = "histogram"
    HEATMAP = "heatmap"
    KPI = "kpi"
    TABLE = "table"


CHART_LABELS: dict[ChartType, str] = {
    ChartType.BAR: "столбчатая диаграмма",
    ChartType.LINE: "линейный график",
    ChartType.AREA: "диаграмма с областями",
    ChartType.PIE: "круговая диаграмма",
    ChartType.SCATTER: "точечная диаграмма",
    ChartType.HISTOGRAM: "гистограмма",
    ChartType.HEATMAP: "тепловая карта",
    ChartType.KPI: "карточка с числом",
    ChartType.TABLE: "таблица",
}


class TableColumn(BaseModel):
    name: str
    label: str | None = None
    semantic_type: SemanticType = SemanticType.UNKNOWN
    unit: str | None = None


class ResultTable(BaseModel):
    columns: list[TableColumn]
    rows: list[list[Any]]
    total_rows: int | None = None
    truncated: bool = False

    def column(self, name: str) -> TableColumn | None:
        return next((c for c in self.columns if c.name == name), None)

    def head(self, n: int) -> ResultTable:
        return self.model_copy(update={"rows": self.rows[:n], "truncated": self.truncated or len(self.rows) > n})


class ChartSpec(BaseModel):
    chart_type: ChartType
    title: str = ""
    x: str | None = Field(None, description="Колонка ResultTable по оси X (или категории для pie)")
    y: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Колонки-значения")
    series: str | None = Field(None, description="Колонка, разбивающая данные на серии (для heatmap это ось Y)")
    x_label: str | None = None
    y_label: str | None = None
    stacked: bool = False
    sort: Literal["none", "asc", "desc"] = "none"


class TextBlock(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["text"] = "text"
    markdown: str
    points: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Нумерованные пункты; на них ссылаются «пункт 2»")


class TableBlock(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["table"] = "table"
    id: str
    title: str = ""
    data: ResultTable


class ChartBlock(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["chart"] = "chart"
    id: str
    spec: ChartSpec
    data: ResultTable
    note: str | None = Field(None, description="Пояснение, например почему выбран другой тип графика")
    engine_spec: dict[str, Any] | None = Field(
        None, description="Та же спецификация в формате движка графиков команды (app.chart_system), уже провалидированная"
    )


class DashboardBlock(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["dashboard"] = "dashboard"
    dashboard_id: str
    title: str
    widget_count: int


class ClarificationBlock(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["clarification"] = "clarification"
    question: str
    options: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Кнопки; нажатие отправляет текст как новое сообщение")


class ErrorBlock(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["error"] = "error"
    message: str
    retryable: bool = False


Block = Annotated[
    TextBlock | TableBlock | ChartBlock | DashboardBlock | ClarificationBlock | ErrorBlock,
    Field(discriminator="type"),
]


class LLMCallTrace(BaseModel):
    purpose: str
    latency_ms: int
    prompt_tokens: int | None = None
    completion_tokens: int | None = None


class ToolCallTrace(BaseModel):
    tool: str
    args: dict[str, Any]
    status: str
    elapsed_ms: int
    sql: str | None = None
    error: str | None = None


class AgentTrace(BaseModel):
    intent: Intent | None = None
    confidence: float | None = None
    standalone_question: str | None = None
    routed_by: Literal["rules", "llm"] | None = None
    tool_calls: list[ToolCallTrace] = Field(default_factory=list)
    llm_calls: list[LLMCallTrace] = Field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    elapsed_ms: int = 0


class AgentResponse(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    turn_id: str
    status: Literal["ok", "clarification", "refused", "error"]
    blocks: list[Block]
    follow_ups: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Подсказки следующих вопросов")
    trace: AgentTrace = Field(default_factory=AgentTrace)


class StatusEvent(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["status"] = "status"
    stage: Literal["routing", "planning", "running_tool", "writing", "dashboard"]
    message: str


class DoneEvent(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["done"] = "done"
    response: AgentResponse


StreamEvent = Annotated[StatusEvent | DoneEvent, Field(discriminator="type")]


'backend\app\agent\contracts\tools.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, ClassVar, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from ..intents import Intent
from ..llm.client import LLMClient
from .catalog import Catalog, QueryExecutor
from .response import ChartType, ResultTable

CATALOG_KEY = "x-catalog"


def TableField(description: str, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:  # noqa: N802
    return Field(description=description, json_schema_extra={CATALOG_KEY: "table"}, **kwargs)


def ColumnField(description: str, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:  # noqa: N802
    return Field(description=description, json_schema_extra={CATALOG_KEY: "column"}, **kwargs)


class ToolStatus(StrEnum):
    OK = "ok"
    EMPTY = "empty"
    RETRYABLE_ERROR = "retryable_error"
    ERROR = "error"


class ChartHint(BaseModel):
    kind: Literal["trend", "comparison", "distribution", "share", "correlation", "single_value"] | None = None
    x: str | None = None
    y: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    series: str | None = None
    preferred: ChartType | None = None


class ToolResult(BaseModel):
    status: ToolStatus = ToolStatus.OK
    facts: dict[str, Any] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="Ключевые числа и выводы в готовом виде (уже округлённые). Модель пересказывает только их.",
    )
    table: ResultTable | None = None
    chart_hint: ChartHint | None = None
    summary_text: str | None = Field(None, description="Шаблонный текст на русском на случай, если модель не справится")
    sql: str | None = Field(None, description="Выполненный SQL: для прозрачности и пересчёта дашбордов")
    warnings: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    error: str | None = None


@dataclass
class ToolContext:
    catalog: Catalog
    executor: QueryExecutor | None
    session_id: str
    question: str
    llm: LLMClient | None = None


class BaseTool(ABC):
    name: ClassVar[str]
    description: ClassVar[str]
    intents: ClassVar[frozenset[Intent]]
    args_model: ClassVar[type[BaseModel]]
    args_hint: ClassVar[str] = ""
    llm_role: ClassVar[Literal["default", "sql"]] = "default"

    @abstractmethod
    async def run(self, args: BaseModel, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult: ...


class ToolRegistry:
    def __init__(self, tools: list[BaseTool] | None = None):
        self._tools: dict[str, BaseTool] = {}
        for tool in tools or []:
            self.register(tool)

    def register(self, tool: BaseTool) -> None:
        if tool.name in self._tools:
            raise ValueError(f"инструмент {tool.name} уже зарегистрирован")
        self._tools[tool.name] = tool

    def get(self, name: str) -> BaseTool | None:
        return self._tools.get(name)

    def for_intent(self, intent: Intent) -> list[BaseTool]:
        return [t for t in self._tools.values() if intent in t.intents]

    def all(self) -> list[BaseTool]:
        return list(self._tools.values())


'backend\app\agent\factory.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from .integrations.postgres_store import PostgresCatalogProvider, PostgresQueryExecutor
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path

from .config import AgentConfig
from .contracts.dashboard import InMemoryDashboardStore
from .contracts.tools import ToolRegistry
from .integrations.basic_tools import DescribeColumnTool, OverviewTool, RelationsTool, TopValuesTool
from .integrations.charts import SUPPORTED_CHART_TYPES, export_chart
from .integrations.csv_store import InMemoryCsvStore
from .integrations.query_dsl import QueryDslTool
from .llm.client import LLMClient, OpenAICompatibleClient
from .memory import InMemoryMemoryStore
from .orchestrator import Agent


@dataclass
class AgentBundle:
    agent: Agent
    store: InMemoryCsvStore
    dashboards: InMemoryDashboardStore


def build_tools(store: InMemoryCsvStore) -> ToolRegistry:
    return ToolRegistry(
        [
            OverviewTool(),
            DescribeColumnTool(),
            TopValuesTool(store.schema, store.sources),
            RelationsTool(),
            QueryDslTool(store.schema, store.sources),
        ]
    )


def build_agent(*, config=None, llm=None, sql_llm=None, data_dir=None):
    config = config or AgentConfig.from_env()
    config = replace(config, chart_types=config.chart_types or SUPPORTED_CHART_TYPES)

    catalog_provider = None
    executor = None
    store = None

    if os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        from app.db import engine  # SQLAlchemy engine
        catalog_provider = PostgresCatalogProvider(engine)
        executor = PostgresQueryExecutor(engine)
        # для загрузки CSV через /api/datasets
        from .integrations.csv_store import InMemoryCsvStore
        store = InMemoryCsvStore()  # fallback, но каталог берётся из PG
    else:
        data_dir = data_dir if data_dir is not None else os.environ.get("DATA_DIR")
        store = InMemoryCsvStore.from_directory(data_dir) if data_dir and Path(data_dir).is_dir() else InMemoryCsvStore()
        catalog_provider = store

    llm = llm or OpenAICompatibleClient(config.llm)
    if sql_llm is None and config.sql_llm is not None:
        sql_llm = OpenAICompatibleClient(config.sql_llm)

    dashboards = InMemoryDashboardStore()
    tools = build_tools(store) if store is not None else build_tools_no_store()
    agent = Agent(
        llm=llm,
        sql_llm=sql_llm,
        catalog_provider=catalog_provider,
        executor=executor,
        tools=tools,
        memory=InMemoryMemoryStore(),
        dashboards=dashboards,
        config=config,
        chart_exporter=export_chart,
    )
    return AgentBundle(agent=agent, store=store, dashboards=dashboards)


def build_tools_no_store() -> ToolRegistry:
    """Когда работаем на Postgres, store не нужен — tools сами пойдут через executor."""
    return ToolRegistry([
        OverviewTool(), DescribeColumnTool(),
        TopValuesTool(lambda: {}, lambda: {}),
        RelationsTool(), QueryDslTool(lambda: {}, lambda: {}),
    ])

'backend\app\agent\integrations\__init__.py':


'backend\app\agent\integrations\basic_tools.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.query_system import QueryEngine

from ..contracts.catalog import TIME_TYPES, Catalog, SemanticType, split_column_ref
from ..contracts.response import ChartType, ResultTable, TableColumn
from ..contracts.tools import BaseTool, ChartHint, ColumnField, TableField, ToolContext, ToolResult, ToolStatus
from ..intents import Intent
from .query_dsl import to_result_table

_TYPE_LABELS = {
    SemanticType.ID: "идентификатор",
    SemanticType.CATEGORY: "категория",
    SemanticType.NUMERIC: "число",
    SemanticType.DATETIME: "дата и время",
    SemanticType.DATE: "дата",
    SemanticType.BOOLEAN: "да/нет",
    SemanticType.TEXT: "текст",
    SemanticType.UNKNOWN: "неизвестно",
}


class NoArgs(BaseModel):
    pass


class OverviewTool(BaseTool):
    name = "dataset_overview"
    description = "Обзор всех загруженных таблиц: объём, период, состав и типы колонок, связи."
    intents = frozenset({Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW})
    args_model = NoArgs

    async def run(self, args: BaseModel, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        catalog = ctx.catalog
        tables = []
        rows = []
        for t in catalog.tables:
            by_type: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
            for c in t.columns:
                if c.description and c.description.startswith("производная"):
                    continue
                by_type.setdefault(_TYPE_LABELS[c.semantic_type], []).append(c.label)
            period = f"{t.period_start} — {t.period_end}" if t.period_start else None
            tables.append(
                {"таблица": t.label, "строк": t.row_count, "колонок": len(t.columns), "период": period, "колонки_по_типам": by_type}
            )
            rows.append([t.label, t.row_count, sum(len(v) for v in by_type.values()), period or "—"])
        relations = [
            f"{r.left_table}.{r.left_column} → {r.right_table}.{r.right_column}" for r in catalog.relations
        ]
        facts: dict[str, Any] = {"таблиц": len(catalog.tables), "таблицы": tables}
        if relations:
            facts["связи"] = relations
        total = sum(t.row_count for t in catalog.tables)
        summary = f"Загружено таблиц: {len(catalog.tables)}, всего строк: {total}."
        table = ResultTable(
            columns=[
                TableColumn(name="Таблица", semantic_type=SemanticType.CATEGORY),
                TableColumn(name="Строк", semantic_type=SemanticType.NUMERIC),
                TableColumn(name="Колонок", semantic_type=SemanticType.NUMERIC),
                TableColumn(name="Период", semantic_type=SemanticType.TEXT),
            ],
            rows=rows,
        )
        return ToolResult(facts=facts, table=table, summary_text=summary, chart_hint=ChartHint(kind="comparison", x="Таблица", y=["Строк"]))


class ColumnArgs(BaseModel):
    column: str = ColumnField("Колонка в формате «таблица.колонка»")


def _column_facts(catalog: Catalog, ref: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    resolved = catalog.resolve_column(ref)
    if resolved is None:
        return None
    table, col = resolved
    facts: dict[str, Any] = {
        "таблица": table.label,
        "поле": col.label,
        "тип": _TYPE_LABELS[col.semantic_type],
        "разных_значений": col.distinct_count,
        "доля_пропусков": col.null_share,
        "всего_строк": table.row_count,
    }
    if col.description:
        facts["описание"] = col.description
    if col.unit:
        facts["единица"] = col.unit
    if col.min_value is not None:
        facts["минимум"], facts["максимум"] = col.min_value, col.max_value
    if col.top_values:
        facts["частые_значения"] = {v.value: v.count for v in col.top_values[:10]}
    elif col.sample_values:
        facts["примеры"] = col.sample_values
    return facts


class DescribeColumnTool(BaseTool):
    name = "describe_column"
    description = "Описывает одно поле: тип, число разных значений, пропуски, диапазон, частые значения."
    intents = frozenset({Intent.DESCRIBE_COLUMN})
    args_model = ColumnArgs

    async def run(self, args: ColumnArgs, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        facts = _column_facts(ctx.catalog, args.column)
        if facts is None:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR, error=f"колонки {args.column} нет в каталоге")
        summary = (
            f"Поле «{facts['поле']}» ({facts['тип']}): {facts['разных_значений']} разных значений, "
            f"пропусков {round((facts['доля_пропусков'] or 0) * 100, 1)}%."
        )
        return ToolResult(facts=facts, summary_text=summary)


class TopValuesArgs(BaseModel):
    column: str = ColumnField("Колонка в формате «таблица.колонка»")
    limit: int = Field(10, ge=1, le=50)


class TopValuesTool(BaseTool):
    name = "top_values"
    description = "Частые значения одной колонки с количеством и долей; для числовых и дат — распределение по значениям."
    intents = frozenset({Intent.DISTRIBUTION})
    args_model = TopValuesArgs

    def __init__(self, schema: Callable[[], dict[str, dict[str, str]]], sources: Callable[[], dict[str, Any]]):
        self._schema = schema
        self._sources = sources
        self.engine = QueryEngine()

    async def run(self, args: TopValuesArgs, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        resolved = ctx.catalog.resolve_column(args.column)
        if resolved is None:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR, error=f"колонки {args.column} нет в каталоге")
        table_info, col = resolved
        table_name, column = split_column_ref(args.column)
        is_time = col.semantic_type in TIME_TYPES
        steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            {"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": table_name},
            {"var": "F", "op": "filter", "input": "T", "condition": {"column": column, "cmp": "is_not_null"}},
            {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "F", "group_by": [column],
             "aggregates": [{"func": "count", "column": "*", "alias": "Количество"}]},
            {"var": "S", "op": "sort", "input": "A",
             "by": [{"column": column, "dir": "asc"}] if is_time else [{"column": "Количество", "dir": "desc"}]},
        ]
        if not is_time:
            steps.append({"var": "L", "op": "limit", "input": "S", "n": args.limit})
        payload = {"steps": steps, "output": steps[-1]["var"]}
        query = self.engine.from_dict(payload)
        validation = self.engine.validate(query, self._schema())
        if not validation.ok:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.ERROR, error="Не удалось посчитать распределение для этого поля.")
        rows = (await asyncio.to_thread(self.engine.execute, query, self._sources())).to_dicts()
        if not rows:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.EMPTY, summary_text=f"В поле «{col.label}» нет заполненных значений.")
        non_null = round(table_info.row_count * (1 - (col.null_share or 0)))
        for r in rows:
            r["Доля, %"] = round(r["Количество"] / non_null * 100, 1) if non_null else None
        schema = {**(validation.output_schema or {}), "Доля, %": "float"}
        table = to_result_table(rows, schema, 500)
        top = rows[0] if not is_time else max(rows, key=lambda r: r["Количество"])
        top_value = table.rows[rows.index(top)][0]
        facts = {
            "поле": col.label,
            "разных_значений": col.distinct_count,
            "самое_частое": top_value,
            "его_количество": top["Количество"],
            "его_доля_процентов": top["Доля, %"],
            "заполненных_строк": non_null,
        }
        summary = f"Чаще всего в поле «{col.label}» встречается «{top_value}»: {top['Количество']} раз ({top['Доля, %']}%)."
        return ToolResult(
            facts=facts,
            table=table,
            chart_hint=ChartHint(kind="trend" if is_time else "distribution", x=column, y=["Количество"]),
            sql=str(payload),
            summary_text=summary,
        )


class RelationsArgs(BaseModel):
    table: str | None = TableField("Таблица, связи которой интересуют; пусто — все связи", default=None)


class RelationsTool(BaseTool):
    name = "relationships"
    description = "Показывает, как таблицы и записи связаны через общие идентификаторы."
    intents = frozenset({Intent.RELATIONSHIPS})
    args_model = RelationsArgs

    async def run(self, args: RelationsArgs, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        rels = [
            r for r in ctx.catalog.relations if args.table is None or args.table in (r.left_table, r.right_table)
        ]
        if not rels:
            return ToolResult(
                status=ToolStatus.EMPTY,
                summary_text="Явных связей между таблицами по общим идентификаторам не найдено.",
            )
        rows = [[f"{r.left_table}.{r.left_column}", f"{r.right_table}.{r.right_column}", round(r.overlap_share * 100, 1), r.cardinality] for r in rels]
        table = ResultTable(
            columns=[
                TableColumn(name="Откуда", semantic_type=SemanticType.TEXT),
                TableColumn(name="Куда", semantic_type=SemanticType.TEXT),
                TableColumn(name="Совпадение, %", semantic_type=SemanticType.NUMERIC),
                TableColumn(name="Тип связи", semantic_type=SemanticType.CATEGORY),
            ],
            rows=rows,
        )
        facts = {"связей": len(rels), "связи": [{"откуда": r[0], "куда": r[1], "совпадение_процентов": r[2]} for r in rows]}
        return ToolResult(
            facts=facts,
            table=table,
            chart_hint=ChartHint(preferred=ChartType.TABLE),
            summary_text=f"Найдено связей по идентификаторам: {len(rels)}.",
        )


'backend\app\agent\integrations\charts.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from app.chart_system import BAR_MAX_LIMIT, LINE_MAX_LIMIT, SCATTER_MAX_LIMIT, TABLE_MAX_LIMIT, ChartEngine

from ..contracts.catalog import SemanticType
from ..contracts.response import ChartSpec, ChartType, ResultTable

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

SUPPORTED_CHART_TYPES: frozenset[ChartType] = frozenset(
    {ChartType.BAR, ChartType.LINE, ChartType.SCATTER, ChartType.KPI, ChartType.TABLE}
)

_engine = ChartEngine()


def engine_schema(table: ResultTable) -> dict[str, str]:
    schema: dict[str, str] = {}
    for i, col in enumerate(table.columns):
        values = [r[i] for r in table.rows if r[i] is not None]
        if col.semantic_type in (SemanticType.DATE, SemanticType.DATETIME) and all(_is_iso_date(v) for v in values):
            schema[col.name] = "date"
        elif values and all(isinstance(v, bool) for v in values):
            schema[col.name] = "bool"
        elif values and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) for v in values):
            schema[col.name] = "int"
        elif values and all(isinstance(v, int | float) and not isinstance(v, bool) for v in values):
            schema[col.name] = "float"
        else:
            schema[col.name] = "str"
    return schema


def _is_iso_date(value: Any) -> bool:
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return True
    try:
        date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
        return True
    except ValueError:
        return False


def _ref(name: str, label: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"name": name, "description": label or name}


def to_engine_dict(spec: ChartSpec, table: ResultTable) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    rows = len(table.rows)
    title = spec.title or "График"
    match spec.chart_type:
        case ChartType.BAR if spec.x and spec.y:
            return {
                "type": "bar",
                "title": title,
                "x": _ref(spec.x, spec.x_label),
                "y": _ref(spec.y[0], spec.y_label),
                "sort": [{"by": "y", "dir": "desc"}],
                "limit": max(1, min(rows, BAR_MAX_LIMIT)),
            }
        case ChartType.LINE if spec.x and spec.y:
            return {
                "type": "line",
                "title": title,
                "x": _ref(spec.x, spec.x_label),
                "y": _ref(spec.y[0], spec.y_label),
                "limit": max(1, min(rows, LINE_MAX_LIMIT)),
            }
        case ChartType.SCATTER if spec.x and spec.y:
            return {
                "type": "scatter",
                "title": title,
                "x": _ref(spec.x, spec.x_label),
                "y": _ref(spec.y[0], spec.y_label),
                "limit": max(1, min(rows, SCATTER_MAX_LIMIT)),
            }
        case ChartType.KPI if spec.y:
            return {"type": "card", "title": title, "value": _ref(spec.y[0], spec.y_label)}
        case ChartType.TABLE:
            return {
                "type": "table",
                "title": title,
                "columns": [_ref(c.name, c.label) for c in table.columns],
                "limit": max(1, min(rows, TABLE_MAX_LIMIT)),
            }
    return None


def export_chart(spec: ChartSpec, table: ResultTable) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    payload = to_engine_dict(spec, table)
    if payload is None:
        return None
    validation = _engine.validate(_engine.from_dict(payload), engine_schema(table), row_count=len(table.rows))
    if not validation.ok:
        log.info("chart spec rejected by chart engine: %s", [(e.reason, e.field) for e in validation.errors])
        return None
    return payload


def render_png(engine_spec: dict[str, Any], table: ResultTable) -> bytes:
    names = [c.name for c in table.columns]
    data = [dict(zip(names, row, strict=False)) for row in table.rows]
    schema = engine_schema(table)
    for row in data:
        for name, kind in schema.items():
            if kind == "date" and row[name] is not None and not isinstance(row[name], date):
                row[name] = date.fromisoformat(str(row[name])[:10])
    chart = _engine.from_dict(engine_spec)
    validation = _engine.validate(chart, schema, row_count=len(data))
    if not validation.ok:
        raise ValueError(f"спецификация графика не прошла проверку: {[(e.reason, e.field) for e in validation.errors]}")
    return _engine.matplotlib_out(chart, data)


'backend\app\agent\integrations\csv_store.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import hashlib
import io
import re
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from ..contracts.catalog import Catalog, ColumnInfo, Relation, SemanticType, TableInfo, ValueCount

_INT_RE = re.compile(r"^-?\d+$")
_FLOAT_RE = re.compile(r"^-?\d{1,3}(?:[  ]\d{3})*(?:[.,]\d+)?$|^-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$")
_DATE_FORMATS = ("%Y-%m-%d", "%d.%m.%Y", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S", "%d.%m.%Y %H:%M:%S", "%d.%m.%Y %H:%M")
_BOOL_VALUES = {"true": True, "false": False, "да": True, "нет": False}
_ID_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(^|\W)(код|id|идентификатор|номер|артикул)(\W|$)", re.I)
_NULLS = {"", "null", "none", "nan", "n/a", "-"}
_SAMPLE = 2000


def _parse_date(value: str) -> tuple[date, bool] | None:
    for fmt in _DATE_FORMATS:
        try:
            parsed = datetime.strptime(value, fmt)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        return parsed.date(), ("%H" in fmt)
    return None


def _to_float(value: str) -> float:
    return float(value.replace(" ", "").replace(" ", "").replace(",", "."))


def infer_type(values: list[str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    sample = values[:_SAMPLE]
    if not sample:
        return "str", False
    if all(v.lower() in _BOOL_VALUES for v in sample):
        return "bool", False
    if all(_INT_RE.match(v) for v in sample):
        if any(len(v.lstrip("-")) > 1 and v.lstrip("-").startswith("0") for v in sample):
            return "str", False
        return "int", False
    if all(_FLOAT_RE.match(v) for v in sample):
        return "float", False
    dates = [_parse_date(v) for v in sample]
    if all(dates):
        return "date", any(d[1] for d in dates if d)
    return "str", False


def _convert(value: str, kind: str) -> Any:
    if value.strip().lower() in _NULLS:
        return None
    v = value.strip()
    try:
        match kind:
            case "int":
                return int(v)
            case "float":
                return _to_float(v)
            case "bool":
                return _BOOL_VALUES.get(v.lower())
            case "date":
                parsed = _parse_date(v)
                return parsed[0] if parsed else None
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return v


def _sniff(text: str) -> csv.Dialect | type[csv.Dialect]:
    try:
        return csv.Sniffer().sniff(text[:20000], delimiters=",;\t|")
    except csv.Error:
        return csv.excel


def _decode(raw: bytes) -> str:
    for encoding in ("utf-8-sig", "cp1251"):
        try:
            return raw.decode(encoding)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
    return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")


def table_name_from(filename: str) -> str:
    stem = Path(filename).stem.strip()
    return re.sub(r"[.\s]+", "_", stem) or "table"


@dataclass
class LoadedTable:
    name: str
    display_name: str
    schema: dict[str, str]
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]]
    with_time: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    derived: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)


def parse_csv(raw: bytes, filename: str) -> LoadedTable:
    text = _decode(raw)
    reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(text), _sniff(text))
    header = next(reader, None)
    if not header:
        raise ValueError(f"файл {filename} пуст")
    columns = [h.strip() or f"колонка_{i + 1}" for i, h in enumerate(header)]
    raw_rows = [r + [""] * (len(columns) - len(r)) for r in reader if any(cell.strip() for cell in r)]

    schema: dict[str, str] = {}
    with_time: set[str] = set()
    for i, col in enumerate(columns):
        values = [r[i].strip() for r in raw_rows if r[i].strip().lower() not in _NULLS]
        kind, has_time = infer_type(values)
        schema[col] = kind
        if has_time:
            with_time.add(col)

    rows = [{col: _convert(r[i], schema[col]) for i, col in enumerate(columns)} for r in raw_rows]
    derived: dict[str, str] = {}
    for col in [c for c, k in schema.items() if k == "date"]:
        month_col, year_col = f"{col} (месяц)", f"{col} (год)"
        if month_col in schema or year_col in schema:
            continue
        schema[month_col], schema[year_col] = "date", "int"
        derived[month_col] = derived[year_col] = col
        for row in rows:
            d = row[col]
            row[month_col] = date(d.year, d.month, 1) if d else None
            row[year_col] = d.year if d else None
    return LoadedTable(table_name_from(filename), Path(filename).stem, schema, rows, with_time, derived)


def _semantic(name: str, kind: str, distinct: int, non_null: int, has_time: bool) -> SemanticType:
    if kind == "date":
        return SemanticType.DATETIME if has_time else SemanticType.DATE
    if kind == "bool":
        return SemanticType.BOOLEAN
    if _ID_NAME_RE.search(name) and (kind in ("int", "str")):
        return SemanticType.ID
    if kind in ("int", "float"):
        return SemanticType.NUMERIC
    if non_null and distinct / non_null > 0.9 and distinct > 50:
        return SemanticType.ID
    if distinct <= max(30, non_null // 20):
        return SemanticType.CATEGORY
    return SemanticType.TEXT


def _fmt(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return f"{value:.2f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
    return str(value)


class InMemoryCsvStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._tables: dict[str, LoadedTable] = {}
        self._catalog: Catalog | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_directory(cls, directory: str | Path) -> InMemoryCsvStore:
        store = cls()
        for path in sorted(Path(directory).glob("*.csv")):
            store.add_csv(path.read_bytes(), path.name)
        return store

    def add_csv(self, raw: bytes, filename: str) -> TableInfo:
        table = parse_csv(raw, filename)
        self._tables[table.name] = table
        self._catalog = None
        catalog = self.catalog()
        info = catalog.table(table.name)
        assert info is not None
        return info

    def schema(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
        return {name: dict(t.schema) for name, t in self._tables.items()}

    def sources(self) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
        return {name: t.rows for name, t in self._tables.items()}

    def rows(self, table: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self._tables[table].rows

    async def get_catalog(self) -> Catalog:
        return self.catalog()

    def catalog(self) -> Catalog:
        if self._catalog is None:
            self._catalog = self._build_catalog()
        return self._catalog

    def _build_catalog(self) -> Catalog:
        tables = [self._profile(t) for t in self._tables.values()]
        version = hashlib.sha1("|".join(f"{t.name}:{t.row_count}" for t in tables).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
        return Catalog(tables=tables, relations=self._relations(tables), version=version)

    def _profile(self, t: LoadedTable) -> TableInfo:
        columns: list[ColumnInfo] = []
        total = len(t.rows)
        for col, kind in t.schema.items():
            values = [r[col] for r in t.rows if r[col] is not None]
            counts = Counter(values)
            semantic = _semantic(col, kind, len(counts), len(values), col in t.with_time)
            info = ColumnInfo(
                name=col,
                db_type=kind,
                semantic_type=semantic,
                null_share=round(1 - len(values) / total, 4) if total else None,
                distinct_count=len(counts),
                sample_values=[_fmt(v) for v in list(dict.fromkeys(values))[:5]],
                description=f"производная от «{t.derived[col]}»" if col in t.derived else None,
            )
            if kind in ("int", "float", "date") and values and semantic is not SemanticType.ID:
                info.min_value, info.max_value = _fmt(min(values)), _fmt(max(values))
            if semantic in (SemanticType.CATEGORY, SemanticType.BOOLEAN, SemanticType.TEXT):
                info.top_values = [ValueCount(value=_fmt(v), count=n) for v, n in counts.most_common(10)]
            columns.append(info)
        time_col = next((c for c in columns if c.semantic_type in (SemanticType.DATE, SemanticType.DATETIME)), None)
        return TableInfo(
            name=t.name,
            display_name=t.display_name if t.display_name != t.name else None,
            row_count=total,
            columns=columns,
            primary_key=next((c.name for c in columns if c.semantic_type is SemanticType.ID and c.distinct_count == total), None),
            time_column=time_col.name if time_col else None,
            period_start=time_col.min_value if time_col else None,
            period_end=time_col.max_value if time_col else None,
        )

    def _relations(self, tables: list[TableInfo]) -> list[Relation]:
        id_columns = [
            (t, c) for t in tables for c in t.columns if c.semantic_type is SemanticType.ID and (c.distinct_count or 0) > 1
        ]
        value_sets = {
            (t.name, c.name): {r[c.name] for r in self._tables[t.name].rows if r[c.name] is not None} for t, c in id_columns
        }
        relations: list[Relation] = []
        for lt, lc in id_columns:
            for rt, rc in id_columns:
                if (lt.name, lc.name) == (rt.name, rc.name) or lc.db_type != rc.db_type:
                    continue
                left, right = value_sets[(lt.name, lc.name)], value_sets[(rt.name, rc.name)]
                if not left:
                    continue
                share = len(left & right) / len(left)
                if share < 0.5 or rc.distinct_count != rt.row_count:
                    continue
                left_unique = lc.distinct_count == lt.row_count
                relations.append(
                    Relation(
                        left_table=lt.name,
                        left_column=lc.name,
                        right_table=rt.name,
                        right_column=rc.name,
                        overlap_share=round(share, 3),
                        cardinality="one_to_one" if left_unique else "many_to_one",
                    )
                )
        return relations


'backend\app\agent\integrations\dsl_to_sql.py':
"""Compile the agent DSL to PostgreSQL CTE SQL."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from .query_dsl import (
    AggregateStepArg, AndCondition, Condition, DistinctArg, FilterArg, JoinArg,
    Leaf, LimitArg, NotCondition, OrCondition, QueryArgs, SelectArg, SortArg, SourceArg,
)


class DslCompileError(ValueError):
    pass


def _q(name: str) -> str:
    return '"' + name.replace('"', '""') + '"'


def _lit(v: Any) -> str:
    if v is None:
        return "NULL"
    if isinstance(v, bool):
        return "TRUE" if v else "FALSE"
    if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
        return repr(v)
    return "'" + str(v).replace("'", "''") + "'"


def _cond(c: Condition) -> str:
    if isinstance(c, Leaf):
        col = _q(c.column)
        op = {"eq": "=", "ne": "<>", "gt": ">", "lt": "<", "ge": ">=", "le": "<="}
        if c.cmp in op:
            return f"{col} {op[c.cmp]} {_lit(c.value)}"
        if c.cmp in ("in", "not_in"):
            vals = ", ".join(_lit(v) for v in (c.values or []))
            return f"{col} {'IN' if c.cmp == 'in' else 'NOT IN'} ({vals})"
        if c.cmp == "between":
            return f"{col} BETWEEN {_lit(c.min)} AND {_lit(c.max)}"
        if c.cmp == "like":
            return f"{col} LIKE {_lit(c.pattern)}"
        if c.cmp == "is_null":
            return f"{col} IS NULL"
        if c.cmp == "is_not_null":
            return f"{col} IS NOT NULL"
        raise DslCompileError(f"unknown cmp {c.cmp}")
    if isinstance(c, AndCondition):
        return "(" + " AND ".join(_cond(x) for x in c.all_of) + ")"
    if isinstance(c, OrCondition):
        return "(" + " OR ".join(_cond(x) for x in c.any_of) + ")"
    if isinstance(c, NotCondition):
        return "NOT (" + _cond(c.negated) + ")"
    raise DslCompileError(f"unknown condition {type(c).__name__}")


def _agg(a) -> str:
    col = "*" if a.column == "*" else _q(a.column)
    return f"{a.func.upper()}({col}) AS {_q(a.alias)}"


def compile_dsl_to_sql(
    args: QueryArgs, *, table_map: dict[str, str], schema: str = "data",
) -> str:
    ctes: list[str] = []
    for step in args.steps:
        if isinstance(step, SourceArg):
            phys = table_map.get(step.table, step.table)
            ctes.append(f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT * FROM {_q(schema)}.{_q(phys)})")
        elif isinstance(step, SelectArg):
            cols = ", ".join(_q(c) for c in step.columns)
            ctes.append(f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT {cols} FROM {_q(step.input)})")
        elif isinstance(step, FilterArg):
            ctes.append(
                f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT * FROM {_q(step.input)} WHERE {_cond(step.condition)})"
            )
        elif isinstance(step, SortArg):
            order = ", ".join(f"{_q(k.column)} {k.dir.upper()}" for k in step.by)
            ctes.append(f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT * FROM {_q(step.input)} ORDER BY {order})")
        elif isinstance(step, LimitArg):
            ctes.append(f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT * FROM {_q(step.input)} LIMIT {int(step.n)})")
        elif isinstance(step, AggregateStepArg):
            gb = ", ".join(_q(c) for c in step.group_by)
            aggs = ", ".join(_agg(a) for a in step.aggregates)
            sel = ", ".join(filter(None, [gb, aggs]))
            grp = f" GROUP BY {gb}" if gb else ""
            hav = f" HAVING {_cond(step.having)}" if step.having else ""
            ctes.append(
                f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT {sel} FROM {_q(step.input)}{grp}{hav})"
            )
        elif isinstance(step, DistinctArg):
            ctes.append(f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT DISTINCT * FROM {_q(step.input)})")
        elif isinstance(step, JoinArg):
            how = {"inner": "INNER", "left": "LEFT", "right": "RIGHT"}[step.how]
            on = " AND ".join(
                f"L.{_q(k.left_col)} = R.{_q(k.right_col)}" for k in step.on
            )
            # как в QueryEngine: коллизии получают суффиксы _left/_right
            ctes.append(
                f"{_q(step.var)} AS (SELECT L.*, R.* FROM {_q(step.left)} L "
                f"{how} JOIN {_q(step.right)} R ON {on})"
            )
        else:
            raise DslCompileError(f"unsupported step {type(step).__name__}")
    return "WITH " + ",\n".join(ctes) + f"\nSELECT * FROM {_q(args.output)}"


'backend\app\agent\integrations\postgres_store.py':
"""Postgres-backed Catalog + QueryExecutor for the agent."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import re
from collections import Counter
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

from ..contracts.catalog import (
    Catalog, ColumnInfo, QueryResult, Relation, SemanticType, TableInfo, ValueCount,
)

_PG_TO_DSL = {
    "integer": "int", "bigint": "int", "smallint": "int", "int4": "int", "int8": "int",
    "numeric": "float", "double precision": "float", "real": "float", "float8": "float",
    "boolean": "bool", "bool": "bool",
    "date": "date", "timestamp without time zone": "date",
    "timestamp with time zone": "date", "timestamptz": "date",
    "text": "str", "character varying": "str", "varchar": "str", "character": "str",
    "uuid": "str", "jsonb": "str",
}
_ID_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"(^|\W)(код|id|идентификатор|номер|артикул)(\W|$)", re.I)


def _dsl_type(pg_type: str) -> str:
    return _PG_TO_DSL.get(pg_type.lower(), "str")


def _semantic(name: str, kind: str, distinct: int, non_null: int) -> SemanticType:
    if kind == "date":
        return SemanticType.DATE
    if kind == "bool":
        return SemanticType.BOOLEAN
    if _ID_NAME_RE.search(name) and kind in ("int", "str"):
        return SemanticType.ID
    if kind in ("int", "float"):
        return SemanticType.NUMERIC
    if non_null and distinct / non_null > 0.9 and distinct > 50:
        return SemanticType.ID
    if distinct <= max(30, non_null // 20):
        return SemanticType.CATEGORY
    return SemanticType.TEXT


class PostgresCatalogProvider:
    """Build a Catalog from information_schema. Human names come from app.datasets."""

    def __init__(self, engine: Engine, *, schema: str = "data", profile_schema: str = "app"):
        self.engine = engine
        self.schema = schema
        self.profile_schema = profile_schema
        self._cache: Catalog | None = None

    async def get_catalog(self) -> Catalog:
        return self.catalog()

    def catalog(self) -> Catalog:
        if self._cache is None:
            self._cache = self._build()
        return self._cache

    def invalidate(self) -> None:
        self._cache = None

    # map logical catalog name -> physical table name (для DSL->SQL)
    def table_map(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {t.name: t.name for t in self.catalog().tables}

    def _build(self) -> Catalog:
        # human names from metadata
        display: dict[str, str] = {}
        try:
            with self.engine.connect() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute(text(
                    f'SELECT table_name, original_filename FROM "{self.profile_schema}".datasets'
                ))
                for tname, fname in rows:
                    display[tname] = (fname or tname).rsplit(".", 1)[0]
        except Exception:
            pass

        tables: list[TableInfo] = []
        with self.engine.connect() as conn:
            names = [r[0] for r in conn.execute(text(
                "SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables "
                "WHERE table_schema = :s AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE' ORDER BY table_name"
            ), {"s": self.schema})]
            for tname in names:
                cols = conn.execute(text(
                    "SELECT column_name, data_type FROM information_schema.columns "
                    "WHERE table_schema = :s AND table_name = :t ORDER BY ordinal_position"
                ), {"s": self.schema, "t": tname}).fetchall()
                total = int(conn.execute(text(
                    f'SELECT count(*) FROM "{self.schema}"."{tname}"'
                )).scalar() or 0)

                columns: list[ColumnInfo] = []
                for cname, ctype in cols:
                    kind = _dsl_type(ctype)
                    stat = conn.execute(text(
                        f'SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE "{cname}" IS NOT NULL), '
                        f'count(DISTINCT "{cname}"), min("{cname}"), max("{cname}") '
                        f'FROM "{self.schema}"."{tname}"'
                    )).one()
                    non_null, distinct, cmin, cmax = stat
                    info = ColumnInfo(
                        name=cname,
                        db_type=kind,
                        semantic_type=_semantic(cname, kind, int(distinct or 0), int(non_null or 0)),
                        null_share=round(1 - (non_null or 0) / total, 4) if total else None,
                        distinct_count=int(distinct or 0),
                        min_value=str(cmin) if cmin is not None else None,
                        max_value=str(cmax) if cmax is not None else None,
                    )
                    columns.append(info)

                tables.append(TableInfo(
                    name=tname,
                    display_name=display.get(tname),
                    row_count=total,
                    columns=columns,
                ))

        version = hashlib.sha1(
            "|".join(f"{t.name}:{t.row_count}" for t in tables).encode()
        ).hexdigest()[:12]
        return Catalog(tables=tables, relations=[], version=version)


class PostgresQueryExecutor:
    """Execute raw SQL produced by dsl_to_sql against Postgres."""

    def __init__(self, engine: Engine, *, statement_timeout_ms: int = 30_000):
        self.engine = engine
        self.statement_timeout_ms = statement_timeout_ms

    async def execute(
        self, sql: str, *, params=None, max_rows: int = 10_000, timeout_s: float = 30.0,
    ) -> QueryResult:
        def _run() -> QueryResult:
            with self.engine.connect() as conn:
                conn.execute(text(f"SET statement_timeout = {int(timeout_s * 1000)}"))
                res = conn.execute(text(sql), params or {})
                cols = list(res.keys())
                rows = [list(r) for r in res.fetchmany(max_rows)]
                return QueryResult(
                    columns=cols, rows=rows, row_count=len(rows),
                    truncated=len(rows) == max_rows,
                )
        return await asyncio.to_thread(_run)


'backend\app\agent\integrations\query_dsl.py':
from __future__ import annotations
from .dsl_to_sql import compile_dsl_to_sql

import asyncio
import json
from collections.abc import Callable
from datetime import date
from typing import Annotated, Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from app.query_system import STEPS, QueryEngine, ValidationError

from ..contracts.catalog import SemanticType
from ..contracts.response import ResultTable, TableColumn
from ..contracts.tools import BaseTool, ChartHint, TableField, ToolContext, ToolResult, ToolStatus
from ..intents import Intent

Scalar = str | int | float | bool


class Leaf(BaseModel):
    column: str
    cmp: Literal["eq", "ne", "gt", "lt", "ge", "le", "in", "not_in", "between", "like", "is_null", "is_not_null"]
    value: Scalar | None = None
    values: list[Scalar] | None = None
    min: Scalar | None = None
    max: Scalar | None = None
    pattern: str | None = None


class AndCondition(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(populate_by_name=True)
    all_of: list[Leaf] = Field(alias="and", min_length=1)


class OrCondition(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(populate_by_name=True)
    any_of: list[Leaf] = Field(alias="or", min_length=1)


class NotCondition(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(populate_by_name=True)
    negated: Leaf = Field(alias="not")


Condition = Leaf | AndCondition | OrCondition | NotCondition


class SortKeyArg(BaseModel):
    column: str
    dir: Literal["asc", "desc"]


class AggregateArg(BaseModel):
    func: Literal["count", "sum", "avg", "min", "max"]
    column: str = Field(description="Колонка или '*' для count")
    alias: str = Field(description="Имя колонки результата, по-русски, например «Выручка»")


class JoinKeyArg(BaseModel):
    left_col: str
    right_col: str


class SourceArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["source"]
    table: str = TableField("Таблица каталога")


class SelectArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["select"]
    input: str
    columns: list[str] = Field(min_length=1)


class FilterArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["filter"]
    input: str
    condition: Condition


class SortArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["sort"]
    input: str
    by: list[SortKeyArg] = Field(min_length=1)


class LimitArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["limit"]
    input: str
    n: int = Field(ge=0)


class AggregateStepArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["aggregate"]
    input: str
    group_by: list[str]
    aggregates: list[AggregateArg] = Field(min_length=1)
    having: Condition | None = None


class DistinctArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["distinct"]
    input: str


class JoinArg(BaseModel):
    var: str
    op: Literal["join"]
    left: str
    right: str
    on: list[JoinKeyArg] = Field(min_length=1)
    how: Literal["inner", "left", "right"]


StepArg = Annotated[
    SourceArg | SelectArg | FilterArg | SortArg | LimitArg | AggregateStepArg | DistinctArg | JoinArg,
    Field(discriminator="op"),
]


class QueryArgs(BaseModel):
    steps: list[StepArg] = Field(min_length=1)
    output: str = Field(description="var последнего шага с итоговым результатом")

    def to_engine(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.model_dump(by_alias=True, exclude_none=True, mode="json")


_REASONS = {
    "column_not_found": "нет такой колонки",
    "table_not_found": "нет такой таблицы",
    "type_mismatch": "тип не подходит",
    "invalid_parameter": "неверный параметр",
    "invalid_condition": "неверное условие",
    "undefined_reference": "ссылка на неопределённую переменную",
    "duplicate_var": "повтор имени переменной",
    "output_not_found": "output не указывает на шаг",
    "unsupported_operation": "операция не поддерживается",
}


def format_errors(errors: list[ValidationError], limit: int = 6) -> str:
    parts = []
    for e in errors[:limit]:
        where = f"шаг {e.step_index + 1} ({e.var})" if e.step_index >= 0 else "запрос"
        text = f"{where}: {_REASONS.get(e.reason, e.reason)}"
        if e.field:
            text += f", поле {e.field}"
        if e.value is not None:
            text += f", значение «{e.value}»"
        if e.valid_options:
            text += f"; допустимо: {', '.join(map(str, e.valid_options[:12]))}"
        parts.append(text)
    return "; ".join(parts)


def _steps_hint() -> str:
    lines = [
        "Строй запрос как список шагов steps; каждый шаг создаёт переменную var и ссылается только на переменные выше.",
        "Колонки называй точно как в схеме, в том числе русские названия с пробелами и запятыми.",
        "Для дат по месяцам и годам используй колонки «... (месяц)» и «... (год)».",
        "Вычисляемых колонок и SQL нет. Для «топ N» используй sort + limit.",
        "Операции:",
    ]
    lines += [f"- {s.name}: {s.description} Пример: {json.dumps(s.example, ensure_ascii=False)}" for s in STEPS]
    example = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "T", "group_by": ["Город"],
             "aggregates": [{"func": "count", "column": "*", "alias": "Заказы"}]},
            {"var": "S", "op": "sort", "input": "A", "by": [{"column": "Заказы", "dir": "desc"}]},
            {"var": "L", "op": "limit", "input": "S", "n": 20},
        ],
        "output": "L",
    }
    lines.append("Полный пример (заказы по городам, топ-20): " + json.dumps(example, ensure_ascii=False))
    return "\n".join(lines)


_ENGINE_TYPES = {
    "int": SemanticType.NUMERIC,
    "float": SemanticType.NUMERIC,
    "date": SemanticType.DATE,
    "bool": SemanticType.BOOLEAN,
    "str": SemanticType.CATEGORY,
}


def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return round(value, 4)
    return value


def to_result_table(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], schema: dict[str, str], max_rows: int) -> ResultTable:
    columns = [TableColumn(name=c, semantic_type=_ENGINE_TYPES.get(t, SemanticType.UNKNOWN)) for c, t in schema.items()]
    names = list(schema)
    return ResultTable(
        columns=columns,
        rows=[[_json_value(r.get(c)) for c in names] for r in rows[:max_rows]],
        total_rows=len(rows),
        truncated=len(rows) > max_rows,
    )


class QueryDslTool(BaseTool):
    name = "data_query"
    description = "Отвечает на вопрос расчётом по данным: фильтры, группировки, суммы, средние, рейтинги, соединения таблиц."
    intents = frozenset(
        {Intent.DATA_QUESTION, Intent.SEGMENT_COMPARE, Intent.TIME_PATTERNS, Intent.ANOMALIES, Intent.CORRELATION}
    )
    args_model = QueryArgs
    args_hint = _steps_hint()
    llm_role = "sql"

    def __init__(
        self,
        schema: Callable[[], dict[str, dict[str, str]]],
        sources: Callable[[], dict[str, Any]],
        *,
        max_rows: int = 1000,
    ):
        self._schema = schema
        self._sources = sources
        self.max_rows = max_rows
        self.engine = QueryEngine()

    async def run(self, args: QueryArgs, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        payload = args.to_engine()
        query = self.engine.from_dict(payload)
        validation = self.engine.validate(query, self._schema())
        if not validation.ok:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR, error=format_errors(validation.errors))
        if ctx.executor is not None and isinstance(ctx.catalog, Catalog):
            try:
                sql = compile_dsl_to_sql(
                    args,
                    table_map={t.name: t.name for t in ctx.catalog.tables},
                )
                raw = await ctx.executor.execute(sql, max_rows=self.max_rows)
                rows = [dict(zip(raw.columns, r)) for r in raw.rows]
                schema = {c: "str" for c in raw.columns}  # типы не критичны для table
                # Приводим к семантике через output_schema движка
                schema = {**schema, **(validation.output_schema or {})}
            except Exception as e:
                return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR, error=f"SQL: {e}")
        else:
            table = await asyncio.to_thread(self.engine.execute, query, self._sources())
            rows = table.to_dicts()
            schema = validation.output_schema or {c: "str" for c in table.columns}

        query_text = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
        if not rows:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.EMPTY, sql=query_text,
                              summary_text="По этому запросу строк не нашлось.")
        result_table = to_result_table(rows, schema, self.max_rows)
        facts: dict[str, Any] = {"строк_в_результате": len(rows)}
        if len(rows) == 1:
            facts["значения"] = {k: _json_value(v) for k, v in rows[0].items()}
            summary = ", ".join(f"{k}: {_json_value(v)}" for k, v in rows[0].items())
        else:
            first = rows[0]
            facts["первая_строка"] = {k: _json_value(v) for k, v in first.items()}
            summary = f"Получено {len(rows)} строк. Первая: " + ", ".join(f"{k}: {_json_value(v)}" for k, v in first.items())
        return ToolResult(
            facts=facts,
            table=result_table,
            chart_hint=_chart_hint(args, schema),
            sql=query_text,
            summary_text=summary + ".",
        )


def _chart_hint(args: QueryArgs, schema: dict[str, str]) -> ChartHint | None:
    aggregate = next((s for s in reversed(args.steps) if isinstance(s, AggregateStepArg)), None)
    if aggregate is None:
        return None
    x = next((c for c in aggregate.group_by if c in schema), None)
    y = [a.alias for a in aggregate.aggregates if schema.get(a.alias) in ("int", "float")]
    kind = "trend" if x and schema.get(x) == "date" else "comparison" if x else "single_value"
    return ChartHint(kind=kind, x=x, y=y)
