'backend\app\agent\llm\client.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import time
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

import httpx
from pydantic import BaseModel

from ..config import LLMConfig, StructuredMode
from .errors import LLMBadRequestError, LLMUnavailableError

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    role: Literal["system", "user", "assistant"]
    content: str


def system_msg(content: str) -> ChatMessage:
    return ChatMessage(role="system", content=content)


def user_msg(content: str) -> ChatMessage:
    return ChatMessage(role="user", content=content)


def assistant_msg(content: str) -> ChatMessage:
    return ChatMessage(role="assistant", content=content)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LLMReply:
    content: str
    model: str
    finish_reason: str | None = None
    prompt_tokens: int | None = None
    completion_tokens: int | None = None
    latency_ms: int = 0


class LLMClient(Protocol):
    async def chat(
        self,
        messages: Sequence[ChatMessage],
        *,
        json_schema: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        schema_name: str = "response",
        temperature: float | None = None,
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
    ) -> LLMReply: ...


_RETRY_STATUS = {408, 409, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504}
_DOWNGRADE = {
    StructuredMode.JSON_SCHEMA: StructuredMode.JSON_OBJECT,
    StructuredMode.JSON_OBJECT: StructuredMode.PROMPT,
}


class OpenAICompatibleClient:
    def __init__(self, config: LLMConfig, http: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None):
        self.config = config
        self.structured_mode = config.structured_mode
        self._http = http or httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=config.base_url.rstrip("/") + "/",
            timeout=config.timeout_s,
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {config.api_key}"},
        )

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        await self._http.aclose()

    async def chat(
        self,
        messages: Sequence[ChatMessage],
        *,
        json_schema: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        schema_name: str = "response",
        temperature: float | None = None,
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
    ) -> LLMReply:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": self.config.model,
            "messages": [m.model_dump() for m in messages],
            "temperature": self.config.temperature if temperature is None else temperature,
            "stream": False,
        }
        if max_tokens is not None:
            payload["max_tokens"] = max_tokens
        while True:
            body = dict(payload)
            response_format = self._response_format(json_schema, schema_name)
            if response_format is not None:
                body["response_format"] = response_format
            try:
                return await self._post(body)
            except LLMBadRequestError:
                if response_format is None:
                    raise
                new_mode = _DOWNGRADE[self.structured_mode]
                log.warning("LLM server rejected response_format=%s, falling back to %s", self.structured_mode, new_mode)
                self.structured_mode = new_mode

    def _response_format(self, json_schema: dict[str, Any] | None, name: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        if json_schema is None:
            return None
        match self.structured_mode:
            case StructuredMode.JSON_SCHEMA:
                return {"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": {"name": name, "schema": json_schema, "strict": False}}
            case StructuredMode.JSON_OBJECT:
                return {"type": "json_object"}
            case StructuredMode.PROMPT:
                return None

    async def _post(self, body: dict[str, Any]) -> LLMReply:
        attempts = self.config.max_retries + 1
        last_error: Exception | None = None
        for attempt in range(attempts):
            if attempt:
                await asyncio.sleep(min(self.config.backoff_base_s * 2 ** (attempt - 1), 8.0))
            started = time.monotonic()
            try:
                resp = await self._http.post("chat/completions", json=body)
            except (httpx.TimeoutException, httpx.TransportError) as e:
                last_error = e
                log.warning("LLM request failed (attempt %d/%d): %r", attempt + 1, attempts, e)
                continue
            if resp.status_code in _RETRY_STATUS:
                last_error = LLMUnavailableError(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:300]}")
                log.warning("LLM server busy (attempt %d/%d): %s", attempt + 1, attempts, last_error)
                continue
            if resp.status_code >= 400:
                raise LLMBadRequestError(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:500]}")
            try:
                data = resp.json()
                choice = data["choices"][0]
                content = choice["message"].get("content") or ""
            except (ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError) as e:
                raise LLMUnavailableError(f"Неожиданный ответ сервера модели: {resp.text[:300]}") from e
            usage = data.get("usage") or {}
            return LLMReply(
                content=content,
                model=data.get("model", self.config.model),
                finish_reason=choice.get("finish_reason"),
                prompt_tokens=usage.get("prompt_tokens"),
                completion_tokens=usage.get("completion_tokens"),
                latency_ms=int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000),
            )
        raise LLMUnavailableError(f"Модель недоступна после {attempts} попыток: {last_error}") from last_error


'backend\app\agent\llm\errors.py':
class LLMError(Exception):
    pass


class LLMUnavailableError(LLMError):
    pass


class LLMBadRequestError(LLMError):
    pass


class StructuredOutputError(LLMError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, raw: str, errors: list[str]):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.raw = raw
        self.errors = errors


'backend\app\agent\llm\structured.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import re
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator, Sequence
from typing import Any, TypeVar

from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError

from .client import ChatMessage, LLMClient, assistant_msg, system_msg, user_msg
from .errors import StructuredOutputError

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)

SCHEMA_INSTRUCTION = (
    "Ответь одним JSON-объектом строго по JSON-схеме ниже. Без пояснений, без markdown.\nСхема: {schema}"
)
SHORT_INSTRUCTION = "Ответь одним JSON-объектом в описанном формате. Без пояснений, без markdown."
MAX_PROMPT_SCHEMA_CHARS = 4000
REPAIR_PROMPT = "Ответ не прошёл проверку:\n{errors}\nВерни только исправленный JSON-объект без пояснений."

_THINK_RE = re.compile(r"<think>.*?</think>", re.S)
_FENCE_RE = re.compile(r"```(?:json)?\s*(.*?)```", re.S)
_TRAILING_COMMA_RE = re.compile(r",\s*([}\]])")


def extract_json(text: str) -> Any:
    text = _THINK_RE.sub("", text).strip()
    candidates = [m.group(1) for m in _FENCE_RE.finditer(text)] + [text]
    for candidate in candidates:
        for chunk in _balanced_objects(candidate):
            for variant in (chunk, _TRAILING_COMMA_RE.sub(r"\1", chunk)):
                try:
                    return json.loads(variant)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
    raise ValueError("в ответе нет корректного JSON-объекта")


def _balanced_objects(text: str, limit: int = 20) -> Iterator[str]:
    found = 0
    start = text.find("{")
    while start != -1 and found < limit:
        depth, in_str, escaped = 0, False, False
        for i in range(start, len(text)):
            ch = text[i]
            if in_str:
                if escaped:
                    escaped = False
                elif ch == "\\":
                    escaped = True
                elif ch == '"':
                    in_str = False
            elif ch == '"':
                in_str = True
            elif ch == "{":
                depth += 1
            elif ch == "}":
                depth -= 1
                if depth == 0:
                    found += 1
                    yield text[start : i + 1]
                    break
        start = text.find("{", start + 1)


def compact_schema(schema: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    defs = schema.get("$defs", {})

    def walk(node: Any, is_properties: bool = False) -> Any:
        if isinstance(node, list):
            return [walk(x) for x in node]
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            return node
        if is_properties:
            return {name: walk(sub) for name, sub in node.items()}
        if "$ref" in node:
            target = walk(defs[node["$ref"].rsplit("/", 1)[-1]])
            rest = {k: walk(v, k == "properties") for k, v in node.items() if k not in ("$ref", "title")}
            return {**target, **rest}
        return {
            ("anyOf" if k == "oneOf" else k): walk(v, k == "properties")
            for k, v in node.items()
            if k not in ("$defs", "title", "discriminator")
        }

    return walk(schema)


def model_schema(model_cls: type[BaseModel]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return compact_schema(model_cls.model_json_schema())


def _format_errors(error: Exception) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(error, ValidationError):
        return [
            f"{'.'.join(str(p) for p in e['loc']) or '(корень)'}: {e['msg']}" for e in error.errors()[:6]
        ]
    return [str(error)]


async def generate_structured(
    llm: LLMClient,
    messages: Sequence[ChatMessage],
    model_cls: type[T],
    *,
    schema: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    schema_name: str | None = None,
    max_attempts: int = 3,
    temperature: float = 0.0,
    max_tokens: int | None = None,
    preprocess: Callable[[Any], Any] | None = None,
    validate: Callable[[T], list[str]] | None = None,
    schema_in_prompt: bool | None = None,
) -> T:
    schema = schema or model_schema(model_cls)
    name = schema_name or model_cls.__name__
    schema_text = json.dumps(schema, ensure_ascii=False)
    if schema_in_prompt is None:
        schema_in_prompt = len(schema_text) <= MAX_PROMPT_SCHEMA_CHARS
    instruction = SCHEMA_INSTRUCTION.format(schema=schema_text) if schema_in_prompt else SHORT_INSTRUCTION
    convo = list(messages)
    if convo and convo[0].role == "system":
        convo[0] = system_msg(convo[0].content + "\n\n" + instruction)
    else:
        convo.insert(0, system_msg(instruction))

    raw, errors = "", []
    for attempt in range(max_attempts):
        reply = await llm.chat(
            convo,
            json_schema=schema,
            schema_name=name,
            temperature=temperature if attempt == 0 else max(temperature, 0.3),
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
        )
        raw = reply.content
        try:
            data = extract_json(raw)
            if preprocess is not None:
                data = preprocess(data)
            obj = model_cls.model_validate(data)
            errors = validate(obj) if validate is not None else []
            if not errors:
                return obj
        except ValueError as e:
            errors = _format_errors(e)
        log.info("structured output %s rejected (attempt %d/%d): %s", name, attempt + 1, max_attempts, errors)
        convo = [*convo, assistant_msg(raw), user_msg(REPAIR_PROMPT.format(errors="\n".join(f"- {e}" for e in errors)))]
    raise StructuredOutputError(f"модель не вернула валидный {name}", raw=raw, errors=errors)


'backend\app\agent\memory.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any, Protocol

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from .contracts.response import ChartSpec
from .contracts.tools import ToolResult
from .intents import Intent
from .llm.client import ChatMessage, assistant_msg, user_msg

MAX_TURNS = 50
MAX_ARTIFACTS = 20


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)


class Artifact(BaseModel):
    id: str
    question: str
    intent: Intent
    tool: str
    args: dict[str, Any]
    result: ToolResult
    chart: ChartSpec | None = None
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=_now)


class Turn(BaseModel):
    id: str
    user_message: str
    standalone_question: str | None = None
    intent: Intent | None = None
    summary: str = ""
    points: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    artifact_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=_now)


class SessionState(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    turns: list[Turn] = Field(default_factory=list)
    artifacts: dict[str, Artifact] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    focus_tables: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    last_artifact_id: str | None = None

    def last_turn(self) -> Turn | None:
        return self.turns[-1] if self.turns else None

    def last_artifact(self) -> Artifact | None:
        return self.artifacts.get(self.last_artifact_id) if self.last_artifact_id else None

    def add_artifact(self, artifact: Artifact) -> None:
        self.artifacts[artifact.id] = artifact
        self.last_artifact_id = artifact.id
        while len(self.artifacts) > MAX_ARTIFACTS:
            self.artifacts.pop(next(iter(self.artifacts)))

    def add_turn(self, turn: Turn) -> None:
        self.turns.append(turn)
        del self.turns[:-MAX_TURNS]

    def set_focus(self, tables: list[str]) -> None:
        if tables:
            self.focus_tables = list(dict.fromkeys(tables))[:3]

    def history_messages(self, n: int) -> list[ChatMessage]:
        messages: list[ChatMessage] = []
        for t in self.turns[-n:]:
            messages.append(user_msg(t.standalone_question or t.user_message))
            text = t.summary
            if t.points:
                text += "\n" + "\n".join(f"{i}. {p}" for i, p in enumerate(t.points, 1))
            messages.append(assistant_msg(text or "(ответ без текста)"))
        return messages


class MemoryStore(Protocol):
    async def load(self, session_id: str) -> SessionState: ...

    async def save(self, state: SessionState) -> None: ...


class InMemoryMemoryStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._items: dict[str, SessionState] = {}

    async def load(self, session_id: str) -> SessionState:
        state = self._items.get(session_id)
        return state.model_copy(deep=True) if state else SessionState(session_id=session_id)

    async def save(self, state: SessionState) -> None:
        self._items[state.session_id] = state.model_copy(deep=True)


'backend\app\agent\narrator.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import math
import re
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from .contracts.tools import ToolResult
from .llm.client import LLMClient, assistant_msg, system_msg, user_msg
from .llm.structured import generate_structured
from .prompts import GROUNDING_FEEDBACK, NARRATOR_SYSTEM, NARRATOR_USER

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.,])-?\d{1,3}(?:[   ]\d{3})+(?:[.,]\d+)?(?![\w])|(?<![\w.,])-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?")
_SMALL_INT_LIMIT = 12
_SCALES = (1.0, 100.0, 1e-3, 1e-6, 1e-9)


class Narrative(BaseModel):
    summary: str = Field(description="1–3 предложения с главным выводом")
    points: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=5)
    follow_ups: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=3)


def _parse_number(token: str) -> tuple[float, int] | None:
    clean = re.sub(r"[   ]", "", token).replace(",", ".")
    try:
        value = float(clean)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    decimals = len(clean.split(".")[1]) if "." in clean else 0
    return value, decimals


def _collect_numbers(obj: Any, out: set[float]) -> None:
    if isinstance(obj, bool):
        return
    if isinstance(obj, int | float):
        if math.isfinite(obj):
            out.add(float(obj))
    elif isinstance(obj, str):
        for m in _NUMBER_RE.finditer(obj):
            parsed = _parse_number(m.group())
            if parsed:
                out.add(parsed[0])
    elif isinstance(obj, dict):
        for v in obj.values():
            _collect_numbers(v, out)
    elif isinstance(obj, list | tuple):
        for v in obj:
            _collect_numbers(v, out)


def ungrounded_numbers(text: str, source: Any) -> list[str]:
    allowed: set[float] = set()
    _collect_numbers(source, allowed)
    bad = []
    for m in _NUMBER_RE.finditer(text):
        parsed = _parse_number(m.group())
        if parsed is None:
            continue
        value, decimals = parsed
        if decimals == 0 and abs(value) <= _SMALL_INT_LIMIT:
            continue
        tolerance = 0.5 * 10**-decimals + 1e-9
        if not any(abs(value - a * s) <= tolerance for a in allowed for s in _SCALES):
            bad.append(m.group())
    return bad


def build_payload(result: ToolResult, preview_rows: int, max_chars: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"facts": result.facts}
    if result.table is not None:
        payload["table"] = {
            "columns": [c.label or c.name for c in result.table.columns],
            "rows": result.table.rows[:preview_rows],
            "total_rows": result.table.total_rows or len(result.table.rows),
        }
    if result.warnings:
        payload["warnings"] = result.warnings
    while len(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)) > max_chars and payload.get("table", {}).get("rows"):
        payload["table"]["rows"] = payload["table"]["rows"][: len(payload["table"]["rows"]) // 2]
    return payload


def _fallback(result: ToolResult) -> Narrative:
    return Narrative(summary=result.summary_text or "Готово, результат ниже.")


async def narrate(
    llm: LLMClient,
    question: str,
    result: ToolResult,
    *,
    preview_rows: int = 20,
    max_chars: int = 6000,
    max_attempts: int = 3,
) -> tuple[Narrative, list[str]]:
    payload = build_payload(result, preview_rows, max_chars)
    payload_text = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    messages = [system_msg(NARRATOR_SYSTEM), user_msg(NARRATOR_USER.format(question=question, payload=payload_text))]
    warnings: list[str] = []
    for attempt in range(2):
        narrative = await generate_structured(
            llm, messages, Narrative, schema_name="narrative", max_attempts=max_attempts, temperature=0.2
        )
        bad = ungrounded_numbers(" ".join([narrative.summary, *narrative.points]), payload)
        if not bad:
            return narrative, warnings
        warnings.append(f"числа без опоры на данные (попытка {attempt + 1}): {', '.join(bad)}")
        log.info("narrative has ungrounded numbers: %s", bad)
        messages = [
            *messages,
            assistant_msg(narrative.model_dump_json()),
            user_msg(GROUNDING_FEEDBACK.format(numbers=", ".join(bad))),
        ]
    if result.summary_text:
        warnings.append("показан шаблонный текст инструмента")
        return _fallback(result), warnings
    if ungrounded_numbers(narrative.summary, payload):
        return _fallback(result), warnings
    return Narrative(summary=narrative.summary), warnings


'backend\app\agent\orchestrator.py':
from __future__ import annotations

)

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EventSink = Callable[[StreamEvent], Awaitable[None]]
ResponseStatus = Literal["ok", "clarification", "refused", "error"]


class ToolChoice(BaseModel):
    tool: str


class WidgetPlan(BaseModel):
    title: str
    question: str
    intent: Intent
    chart_type: ChartType | None = None


class DashboardPlan(BaseModel):
    title: str
    widgets: list[WidgetPlan] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=6)


class _EarlyReply(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status: ResponseStatus, text: str, options: Sequence[str] = (), *, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(text)
        self.status = status
        self.text = text
        self.options = list(options)
        self.retryable = retryable


class TracingLLM:
    def __init__(self, inner: LLMClient, trace: AgentTrace):
        self.inner = inner
        self.trace = trace

    async def chat(self, messages: Sequence[ChatMessage], **kwargs: Any) -> LLMReply:
        reply = await self.inner.chat(messages, **kwargs)
        self.trace.llm_calls.append(
            LLMCallTrace(
                purpose=kwargs.get("schema_name", "response"),
                latency_ms=reply.latency_ms,
                prompt_tokens=reply.prompt_tokens,
                completion_tokens=reply.completion_tokens,
            )
        )
        return reply


@dataclass
class _Run:
    session_id: str
    turn_id: str
    state: SessionState
    trace: AgentTrace
    llm: LLMClient
    sql_llm: LLMClient
    emit: EventSink | None
    catalog: Catalog = field(default_factory=Catalog)


@dataclass
class _Execution:
    tool: BaseTool
    args: BaseModel
    result: ToolResult
    tables: list[str]


def _new_id() -> str:
    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]


def _title(question: str, limit: int = 90) -> str:
    q = question.strip().rstrip("?").strip()
    return q if len(q) <= limit else q[: limit - 1].rstrip() + "…"

_MULTI_RE = re.compile(
    r"сравн|обзор|несколько|дашборд|панел|отчёт|отчет|покажи\s+всё|покажи\s+все",
    re.I,
)

def _looks_multi_chart(text: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_MULTI_RE.search(text))

class Agent:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        llm: LLMClient,
        catalog_provider: CatalogProvider,
        executor: QueryExecutor | None = None,
        tools: ToolRegistry,
        memory: MemoryStore | None = None,
        dashboards: DashboardStore | None = None,
        config: AgentConfig | None = None,
        sql_llm: LLMClient | None = None,
        chart_exporter: Callable[[ChartSpec, ResultTable], dict[str, Any] | None] | None = None,
    ):
        self.llm = llm
        self.sql_llm = sql_llm or llm
        self.catalog_provider = catalog_provider
        self.executor = executor
        self.tools = tools
        self.memory = memory or InMemoryMemoryStore()
        self.dashboards = dashboards
        self.config = config or AgentConfig()
        self.chart_exporter = chart_exporter
        self._locks: defaultdict[str, asyncio.Lock] = defaultdict(asyncio.Lock)


    async def handle(self, session_id: str, message: str, *, emit: EventSink | None = None) -> AgentResponse:
        async with self._locks[session_id]:
            return await self._handle(session_id, message, emit)

    async def refresh_dashboard(self, dashboard_id: str) -> Dashboard | None:
        if self.dashboards is None:
            return None
        dashboard = await self.dashboards.get(dashboard_id)
        if dashboard is None:
            return None
        catalog = await self.catalog_provider.get_catalog()
        for widget in dashboard.widgets:
            tool = self.tools.get(widget.tool)
            if tool is None:
                continue
            ctx = ToolContext(catalog, self.executor, dashboard.session_id, widget.question, self.llm)
            try:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    tool.run(tool.args_model.model_validate(widget.args), ctx), self.config.tool_timeout_s
                )
            except Exception:
                log.exception("dashboard %s widget %s refresh failed", dashboard_id, widget.id)
                continue
            if result.status is ToolStatus.OK and result.table is not None:
                widget.data = result.table.head(self.config.stored_rows)
                widget.summary = result.summary_text or widget.summary
        dashboard.refreshed_at = datetime.now(UTC)
        await self.dashboards.save(dashboard)
        return dashboard


    async def _handle(self, session_id: str, message: str, emit: EventSink | None) -> AgentResponse:
        started = time.monotonic()
        trace = AgentTrace()
        state = await self.memory.load(session_id)
        run = _Run(
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_id=_new_id(),
            state=state,
            trace=trace,
            llm=TracingLLM(self.llm, trace),
            sql_llm=TracingLLM(self.sql_llm, trace),
            emit=emit,
        )
        turn = Turn(id=run.turn_id, user_message=message)
        try:
            response = await self._dispatch(run, message, turn)
        except _EarlyReply as e:
            response = self._early(run, e)
        except LLMUnavailableError as e:
            log.warning("LLM unavailable: %s", e)
            trace.warnings.append(f"llm unavailable: {e}")
            response = self._respond(run, "error", [ErrorBlock(message=LLM_DOWN_TEXT, retryable=True)])
        except StructuredOutputError as e:
            trace.warnings.append(f"structured output failed: {e.errors}")
            response = self._clarify(run, NOT_UNDERSTOOD_TEXT, self._examples(run.catalog))
        except LLMError as e:
            log.error("LLM error: %s", e)
            trace.warnings.append(f"llm error: {e}")
            response = self._respond(run, "error", [ErrorBlock(message=GENERIC_ERROR_TEXT, retryable=True)])
        except Exception:
            log.exception("agent failed on session %s", session_id)
            response = self._respond(run, "error", [ErrorBlock(message=GENERIC_ERROR_TEXT, retryable=True)])

        trace.elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000)
        if not turn.summary:
            turn.summary = next(
                (b.markdown for b in response.blocks if isinstance(b, TextBlock)),
                next((b.question for b in response.blocks if isinstance(b, ClarificationBlock)), ""),
            )
        state.add_turn(turn)
        try:
            await self.memory.save(state)
        except Exception:
            log.exception("failed to save session %s", session_id)
        if emit is not None:
            await emit(DoneEvent(response=response))
        return response

    async def _dispatch(self, run: _Run, message: str, turn: Turn) -> AgentResponse:
        await self._status(run, "routing", "Разбираю вопрос")
        run.catalog = await self.catalog_provider.get_catalog()
        router = IntentRouter(run.llm, history_turns=self.config.history_turns, max_attempts=self.config.max_structured_attempts)
        decision, routed_by = await router.route(message, run.state, run.catalog)
        run.trace.intent = decision.intent
        run.trace.confidence = decision.confidence
        run.trace.standalone_question = decision.standalone_question
        run.trace.routed_by = routed_by  # type: ignore[assignment]
        turn.intent = decision.intent
        turn.standalone_question = decision.standalone_question
        examples = self._examples(run.catalog)

        match decision.intent:
            case Intent.HELP:
                return self._respond(run, "ok", [TextBlock(markdown=HELP_TEXT)], examples)
            case Intent.OUT_OF_SCOPE:
                return self._respond(run, "refused", [TextBlock(markdown=OUT_OF_SCOPE_TEXT)], examples)
            case Intent.UNCLEAR:
                return self._clarify(run, decision.clarification_question or CLARIFY_DEFAULT, examples)
        if decision.confidence < self.config.clarify_threshold:
            question = decision.clarification_question or f"Правильно понимаю, что нужно: «{decision.standalone_question}»?"
            return self._clarify(run, question, [decision.standalone_question, *examples][:4])
        if not run.catalog.tables:
            return self._respond(run, "ok", [TextBlock(markdown=NO_DATA_TEXT)])

        match decision.intent:
            case Intent.CHANGE_VIEW:
                return await self._change_view(run, decision, turn)
            case Intent.FOLLOW_UP_DETAIL:
                return await self._follow_up(run, decision, turn, router)
            case Intent.CREATE_DASHBOARD:
                return await self._dashboard(run, decision, turn, message)
            case _:
                return await self._answer(run, decision, turn)

        match decision.intent:
            case Intent.CHANGE_VIEW:
                return await self._change_view(run, decision, turn)
            case Intent.FOLLOW_UP_DETAIL:
                return await self._follow_up(run, decision, turn, router)
            case Intent.CREATE_DASHBOARD:
                return await self._dashboard(run, decision, turn, message)
            case _ if decision.intent in ANALYTIC_INTENTS and (
                getattr(decision, "wants_chart", False)
                or _looks_multi_chart(decision.standalone_question)
            ):
                return await self._answer_chart_first(run, decision.standalone_question, turn)
            case _:
                return await self._answer(run, decision, turn)

    async def _answer_chart_first(self, run: "_Run", question: str, turn: Turn) -> AgentResponse:
        """LLM сначала планирует графики, потом под каждый генерит запрос."""
        await self._status(run, "planning", "Планирую визуализации")
        allowed = set(self.config.chart_types or SUPPORTED_CHART_TYPES)
        plan = await plan_charts(
            run.llm, question, run.catalog,
            allowed_types=allowed,
            max_attempts=self.config.max_structured_attempts,
            max_tables=self.config.max_schema_tables,
            max_columns=self.config.max_schema_columns,
        )
        blocks: list[Block] = []
        summaries: list[str] = []
        titles: list[str] = []
        for item in plan.charts:
            chart_question = (
                f"{question}\n\n"
                f"Собери данные для графика «{item.title}» ({item.chart_type.value}): "
                f"{item.data_requirement}"
            )
            try:
                execution = await self._execute(run, Intent.DATA_QUESTION, chart_question)
            except _EarlyReply as e:
                run.trace.warnings.append(f"график «{item.title}» пропущен: {e.text}")
                continue
            if execution.result.status is not ToolStatus.OK:
                continue
            chart, note = choose_chart(
                execution.result.table,
                hint=execution.result.chart_hint,
                requested=item.chart_type,
                title=item.title,
                allowed=self.config.chart_types,
            )
            if chart is None:
                chart, note = choose_chart(
                    execution.result.table,
                    hint=execution.result.chart_hint,
                    title=item.title,
                    allowed=self.config.chart_types,
                )
                note = (note or "") + " (запрошенный тип недоступен для этих данных)"
            artifact = self._store_artifact(
                run, Intent.DATA_QUESTION, chart_question, execution, chart
            )
            blocks.append(TextBlock(markdown=f"**{item.title}**"))
            blocks.extend(self._result_blocks(artifact, note))
            if execution.result.summary_text:
                summaries.append(execution.result.summary_text)
            titles.append(item.title)

        if not blocks:
            raise _EarlyReply(
                "clarification",
                "Не удалось построить ни один график. Уточните, какие показатели нужны?",
                self._examples(run.catalog),
            )
        turn.summary = " ".join(summaries)[:400] or "Готово."
        turn.points = titles
        return self._respond(run, "ok", blocks)

    async def _answer(self, run: _Run, decision: IntentDecision, turn: Turn) -> AgentResponse:
        question = decision.standalone_question
        execution = await self._execute(run, decision.intent, question)
        result = execution.result
        run.state.set_focus(execution.tables)
        if result.status is ToolStatus.EMPTY or (result.table is not None and not result.table.rows and not result.facts):
            text = result.summary_text or EMPTY_RESULT_TEXT
            turn.summary = text
            return self._respond(run, "ok", [TextBlock(markdown=text)], self._examples(run.catalog))

        await self._status(run, "writing", "Формулирую ответ")
        narrative, warnings = await narrate(
            run.llm,
            question,
            result,
            preview_rows=self.config.preview_rows,
            max_chars=self.config.max_narrative_payload_chars,
            max_attempts=self.config.max_structured_attempts,
        )
        run.trace.warnings.extend(warnings)

        chart, note = None, None
        if result.table is not None and result.table.rows:
            chart, note = choose_chart(
                result.table,
                hint=result.chart_hint,
                requested=decision.chart_type,
                title=_title(question),
                allowed=self.config.chart_types,
            )
        artifact = self._store_artifact(run, decision.intent, question, execution, chart)
        turn.summary = narrative.summary
        turn.points = narrative.points
        turn.artifact_ids = [artifact.id]
        blocks: list[Block] = [TextBlock(markdown=narrative.summary, points=narrative.points)]
        blocks += self._result_blocks(artifact, note)
        blocks += [TextBlock(markdown="Обратите внимание: " + w) for w in result.warnings[:2]]
        return self._respond(run, "ok", blocks, narrative.follow_ups)

    async def _change_view(self, run: _Run, decision: IntentDecision, turn: Turn) -> AgentResponse:
        artifact = run.state.last_artifact()
        table = artifact.result.table if artifact else None
        if artifact is None or table is None or not table.rows:
            raise _EarlyReply("clarification", "Пока нечего перерисовать: сначала задайте вопрос к данным.", self._examples(run.catalog))
        current = artifact.chart.chart_type if artifact.chart else None
        title = artifact.chart.title if artifact.chart and artifact.chart.title else _title(artifact.question)
        note = None
        if decision.chart_type is not None:
            spec, note = choose_chart(
                table, hint=artifact.result.chart_hint, requested=decision.chart_type, title=title,
                allowed=self.config.chart_types,
            )
        else:
            spec = alternative_chart(table, current, artifact.result.chart_hint, self.config.chart_types)
            spec = spec.model_copy(update={"title": title}) if spec else None
        if spec is None:
            raise _EarlyReply("clarification", "Эти данные не получается показать иначе. Какой вид графика нужен?", [])
        new_artifact = artifact.model_copy(update={"id": _new_id(), "chart": spec})
        run.state.add_artifact(new_artifact)
        text = f"Те же данные, вид: {CHART_LABELS[spec.chart_type]}."
        turn.summary = text
        turn.artifact_ids = [new_artifact.id]
        return self._respond(run, "ok", [TextBlock(markdown=text), *self._result_blocks(new_artifact, note)])

    async def _follow_up(self, run: _Run, decision: IntentDecision, turn: Turn, router: IntentRouter) -> AgentResponse:
        last = run.state.last_turn()
        n = decision.point_number
        if last is None or not last.points or n is None or n > len(last.points):
            count = len(last.points) if last else 0
            options = [f"Подробнее про пункт {i}" for i in range(1, count + 1)]
            raise _EarlyReply("clarification", "Уточните, про какой пункт рассказать подробнее?", options)
        point = last.points[n - 1]
        context = last.standalone_question or last.user_message
        message = f"Расскажи подробнее: {point} (это пункт ответа на вопрос «{context}»)"
        detail = await router.classify(message, run.state, run.catalog, allowed=sorted(ANALYTIC_INTENTS))
        run.trace.standalone_question = detail.standalone_question
        run.trace.warnings.append(f"пункт {n} переклассифицирован как {detail.intent.value}")
        turn.standalone_question = detail.standalone_question
        return await self._answer(run, detail, turn)

    async def _dashboard(self, run: _Run, decision: IntentDecision, turn: Turn, message: str) -> AgentResponse:
        await self._status(run, "dashboard", "Проектирую дашборд")
        tables = select_tables(
            decision.standalone_question, run.catalog, focus=run.state.focus_tables, max_tables=self.config.max_schema_tables
        )
        allowed = [i for i in Intent if i in ANALYTIC_INTENTS]
        schema = model_schema(DashboardPlan)
        schema["properties"]["widgets"]["items"]["properties"]["intent"]["enum"] = [i.value for i in allowed]
        prompt = DASHBOARD_SYSTEM.format(
            intents=", ".join(i.value for i in allowed),
            schema=render_schema(run.catalog, tables, max_columns=self.config.max_schema_columns),
        )
        plan = await generate_structured(
            run.llm,
            [system_msg(prompt), user_msg(decision.standalone_question)],
            DashboardPlan,
            schema=schema,
            schema_name="dashboard_plan",
            max_attempts=self.config.max_structured_attempts,
        )
        widgets: list[DashboardWidget] = []
        skipped: list[str] = []
        for wp in plan.widgets:
            intent = wp.intent if wp.intent in ANALYTIC_INTENTS else Intent.DATA_QUESTION
            try:
                ex = await self._execute(run, intent, wp.question)
            except (_EarlyReply, StructuredOutputError) as e:
                skipped.append(wp.title)
                run.trace.warnings.append(f"виджет «{wp.title}» пропущен: {e}")
                continue
            if ex.result.status is not ToolStatus.OK or ex.result.table is None or not ex.result.table.rows:
                skipped.append(wp.title)
                continue
            chart, _ = choose_chart(
                ex.result.table, hint=ex.result.chart_hint, requested=wp.chart_type, title=wp.title,
                allowed=self.config.chart_types,
            )
            widgets.append(
                DashboardWidget(
                    id=_new_id(),
                    title=wp.title,
                    question=wp.question,
                    intent=intent,
                    tool=ex.tool.name,
                    args=ex.args.model_dump(mode="json"),
                    chart=chart or ChartSpec(chart_type=ChartType.TABLE, title=wp.title),
                    data=ex.result.table.head(self.config.stored_rows),
                    summary=ex.result.summary_text or "",
                )
            )
        if not widgets:
            raise _EarlyReply(
                "clarification", "Не получилось собрать ни одного виджета. Уточните, какие показатели нужны?", self._examples(run.catalog)
            )
        dashboard = Dashboard(id=_new_id(), title=plan.title, session_id=run.session_id, request=message, widgets=widgets)
        if self.dashboards is not None:
            await self.dashboards.save(dashboard)
        text = f"Собрал дашборд «{dashboard.title}» из {len(widgets)} виджетов."
        if skipped:
            text += f" Не удалось построить: {', '.join(skipped)}."
        turn.summary = text
        turn.points = [w.title for w in widgets]
        blocks: list[Block] = [
            TextBlock(markdown=text, points=turn.points),
            DashboardBlock(dashboard_id=dashboard.id, title=dashboard.title, widget_count=len(widgets)),
        ]
        for w in widgets:
            if w.chart and w.chart.chart_type is not ChartType.TABLE:
                blocks.append(self._chart_block(f"{dashboard.id}-{w.id}", w.chart, w.data, None))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            else:
                blocks.append(TableBlock(id=f"{dashboard.id}-{w.id}", title=w.title, data=w.data))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        return self._respond(run, "ok", blocks)


    async def _execute(self, run: _Run, intent: Intent, question: str) -> _Execution:
        tools = self.tools.for_intent(intent) or self.tools.for_intent(Intent.DATA_QUESTION)
        if not tools:
            raise _EarlyReply("clarification", NOT_IMPLEMENTED_TEXT, self._examples(run.catalog))
        tool = tools[0] if len(tools) == 1 else await self._choose_tool(run, tools, question)
        tables = select_tables(
            question, run.catalog, focus=run.state.focus_tables, max_tables=self.config.max_schema_tables
        )
        await self._status(run, "planning", "Готовлю расчёт")
        feedback: tuple[BaseModel, str] | None = None
        for attempt in range(self.config.max_tool_repairs + 1):
            args = await self._fill_args(run, tool, question, tables, feedback)
            await self._status(run, "running_tool", "Считаю")
            result = await self._run_tool(run, tool, args, question)
            if result.status is ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR and attempt < self.config.max_tool_repairs:
                feedback = (args, result.error or "инструмент вернул ошибку")
                continue
            break
        if result.status is ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR:
            raise _EarlyReply(
                "clarification",
                "Не получилось построить расчёт для этого вопроса. Уточните, какие поля или период использовать?",
                self._examples(run.catalog),
            )
        if result.status is ToolStatus.ERROR:
            raise _EarlyReply("error", result.error or GENERIC_ERROR_TEXT, retryable=True)
        return _Execution(tool, args, result, self._tables_used(tool, args) or tables)

    async def _choose_tool(self, run: _Run, tools: list[BaseTool], question: str) -> BaseTool:
        names = [t.name for t in tools]
        schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {"tool": {"type": "string", "enum": names}}, "required": ["tool"]}
        prompt = TOOL_CHOICE_SYSTEM.format(tools="\n".join(f"- {t.name}: {t.description}" for t in tools))
        choice = await generate_structured(
            run.llm,
            [system_msg(prompt), user_msg(question)],
            ToolChoice,
            schema=schema,
            schema_name="tool_choice",
            max_attempts=self.config.max_structured_attempts,
            validate=lambda c: [] if c.tool in names else [f"tool должен быть одним из: {', '.join(names)}"],
        )
        return next(t for t in tools if t.name == choice.tool)

    async def _fill_args(
        self, run: _Run, tool: BaseTool, question: str, tables: list[str], feedback: tuple[BaseModel, str] | None
    ) -> BaseModel:
        if not tool.args_model.model_fields:
            return tool.args_model()
        catalog = run.catalog
        fields = catalog_fields(tool.args_model)
        all_tables = [t.name for t in catalog.tables]
        all_columns = catalog.column_refs()
        schema = build_args_schema(tool.args_model, all_tables, catalog.column_refs(tables))
        prompt = ARGS_SYSTEM.format(
            name=tool.name,
            description=tool.description,
            hint=tool.args_hint,
            schema=render_schema(catalog, tables, max_columns=self.config.max_schema_columns),
        )
        content = ARGS_USER.format(question=question)
        if feedback is not None:
            content += ARGS_FEEDBACK.format(args=feedback[0].model_dump_json(), error=feedback[1])
        llm = run.sql_llm if tool.llm_role == "sql" else run.llm
        return await generate_structured(
            llm,
            [system_msg(prompt), user_msg(content)],
            tool.args_model,
            schema=schema,
            schema_name=f"args_{tool.name}",
            max_attempts=self.config.max_structured_attempts,
            preprocess=lambda data: fix_catalog_refs(data, fields, all_tables, all_columns),
            validate=lambda args: check_catalog_refs(args, fields, catalog),
        )

    async def _run_tool(self, run: _Run, tool: BaseTool, args: BaseModel, question: str) -> ToolResult:
        ctx = ToolContext(run.catalog, self.executor, run.session_id, question, run.llm)
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            result = await asyncio.wait_for(tool.run(args, ctx), self.config.tool_timeout_s)
        except QueryError as e:
            result = ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR, error=f"ошибка базы данных: {e.message}")
        except TimeoutError:
            result = ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.ERROR, error=TOOL_TIMEOUT_TEXT)
        except Exception:
            log.exception("tool %s failed", tool.name)
            result = ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.ERROR, error=GENERIC_ERROR_TEXT)
        run.trace.tool_calls.append(
            ToolCallTrace(
                tool=tool.name,
                args=args.model_dump(mode="json"),
                status=result.status.value,
                elapsed_ms=int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000),
                sql=result.sql,
                error=result.error,
            )
        )
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _tables_used(tool: BaseTool, args: BaseModel) -> list[str]:
        used: list[str] = []
        for name, kind in catalog_fields(tool.args_model).items():
            value = getattr(args, name, None)
            for v in value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]:
                if isinstance(v, str):
                    used.append(v if kind == "table" else split_column_ref(v)[0])
        return list(dict.fromkeys(used))


    def _store_artifact(
        self, run: _Run, intent: Intent, question: str, execution: _Execution, chart: ChartSpec | None
    ) -> Artifact:
        result = execution.result
        if result.table is not None:
            result = result.model_copy(update={"table": result.table.head(self.config.stored_rows)})
        artifact = Artifact(
            id=_new_id(),
            question=question,
            intent=intent,
            tool=execution.tool.name,
            args=execution.args.model_dump(mode="json"),
            result=result,
            chart=chart,
        )
        run.state.add_artifact(artifact)
        return artifact

    def _chart_block(self, block_id: str, spec: ChartSpec, data: ResultTable, note: str | None) -> ChartBlock:
        engine_spec = None
        if self.chart_exporter is not None:
            try:
                engine_spec = self.chart_exporter(spec, data)
            except Exception:
                log.exception("chart exporter failed for %s", spec.chart_type)
        return ChartBlock(id=block_id, spec=spec, data=data, note=note, engine_spec=engine_spec)

    def _result_blocks(self, artifact: Artifact, note: str | None) -> list[Block]:
        table: ResultTable | None = artifact.result.table
        if table is None or not table.rows:
            return []
        chart = artifact.chart
        blocks: list[Block] = []
        if chart is not None and chart.chart_type is not ChartType.TABLE:
            blocks.append(self._chart_block(f"{artifact.id}-chart", chart, table, note))
        if chart is None or chart.chart_type is not ChartType.KPI:
            blocks.append(TableBlock(id=f"{artifact.id}-table", title=chart.title if chart else "", data=table))
        return blocks

    def _respond(
        self, run: _Run, status: ResponseStatus, blocks: list[Block], follow_ups: Sequence[str] = ()
    ) -> AgentResponse:
        return AgentResponse(
            session_id=run.session_id,
            turn_id=run.turn_id,
            status=status,
            blocks=blocks,
            follow_ups=list(follow_ups)[:3],
            trace=run.trace,
        )

    def _clarify(self, run: _Run, question: str, options: Sequence[str]) -> AgentResponse:
        return self._respond(run, "clarification", [ClarificationBlock(question=question, options=list(options)[:4])])

    def _early(self, run: _Run, e: _EarlyReply) -> AgentResponse:
        if e.status == "clarification":
            return self._clarify(run, e.text, e.options)
        if e.status == "error":
            return self._respond(run, "error", [ErrorBlock(message=e.text, retryable=e.retryable)])
        return self._respond(run, e.status, [TextBlock(markdown=e.text)], e.options)

    @staticmethod
    def _examples(catalog: Catalog) -> list[str]:
        if not catalog.tables:
            return []
        examples = ["Что есть в этих данных?"]
        table = max(catalog.tables, key=lambda t: t.row_count)
        category = next((c for c in table.columns if c.semantic_type is SemanticType.CATEGORY), None)
        if category is not None:
            examples.append(f"Какие значения «{category.label}» встречаются чаще всего?")
        time_col = next((c for c in table.columns if c.semantic_type in TIME_TYPES), None)
        if time_col is not None:
            examples.append(f"Как менялось число записей в «{table.label}» по месяцам?")
        return examples

    @staticmethod
    async def _status(run: _Run, stage: str, message: str) -> None:
        if run.emit is not None:
            await run.emit(StatusEvent(stage=stage, message=message))  # type: ignore[arg-type]


'backend\app\agent\prompts.py':
from .intents import INTENT_DESCRIPTIONS, Intent

ROUTER_SYSTEM = """Ты модуль маршрутизации в BI-ассистенте. Пользователь задаёт вопросы о своих табличных данных.
Сделай три вещи:
1) standalone_question: перепиши последнее сообщение в самостоятельный вопрос, подставив нужное из диалога;
2) intent: выбери одно намерение из списка;
3) confidence: уверенность от 0 до 1.

Намерения:
{intents}

Таблицы: {tables}

Правила:
- Вопрос про данные, но непонятно, что посчитать: intent=unclear и вопрос пользователю в clarification_question.
- Просьба изменить, удалить, загрузить данные или вопрос не про данные: out_of_scope.
- wants_chart=true, если просят показать, нарисовать, визуализировать.
- chart_type заполняй, только если пользователь сам назвал вид графика.
- point_number заполняй, если пользователь ссылается на пункт предыдущего ответа.

Примеры:
{examples}"""

ROUTER_EXAMPLES: list[tuple[str, Intent]] = [
    ("Что передо мной? За какой период эти данные?", Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW),
    ("Что означает поле «Скидка, %»?", Intent.DESCRIBE_COLUMN),
    ("Какие значения статуса встречаются чаще всего?", Intent.DISTRIBUTION),

]


def render_intents(allowed: list[Intent]) -> str:
    return "\n".join(f"- {i.value}: {INTENT_DESCRIPTIONS[i]}" for i in allowed)


def render_examples(allowed: list[Intent]) -> str:
    return "\n".join(f"«{q}» -> {i.value}" for q, i in ROUTER_EXAMPLES if i in allowed)

'backend\app\agent\router.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from .contracts.catalog import Catalog
from .contracts.response import ChartType
from .intents import Intent
from .llm.client import LLMClient, system_msg, user_msg
from .llm.structured import generate_structured, model_schema
from .memory import SessionState
from .prompts import ROUTER_SYSTEM, render_examples, render_intents
from .schema_linking import render_tables_brief


class IntentDecision(BaseModel):
    standalone_question: str = Field(description="Самостоятельная формулировка вопроса с учётом диалога")
    intent: Intent
    confidence: float = Field(ge=0, le=1)
    wants_chart: bool = False
    chart_type: ChartType | None = None
    point_number: int | None = Field(None, ge=1)
    clarification_question: str | None = None


_CHART_WORDS: list[tuple[re.Pattern[str], ChartType]] = [
    (re.compile(r"кругов|пирог|пончик", re.I), ChartType.PIE),
    (re.compile(r"столбчат|столбик", re.I), ChartType.BAR),
    (re.compile(r"гистограмм", re.I), ChartType.HISTOGRAM),
    (re.compile(r"линейн|линией|линии|\bлиния", re.I), ChartType.LINE),
    (re.compile(r"с\s+областями|площад", re.I), ChartType.AREA),
    (re.compile(r"точечн|рассеян|скаттер", re.I), ChartType.SCATTER),
    (re.compile(r"теплов|хитмап|heatmap", re.I), ChartType.HEATMAP),
    (re.compile(r"(в\s+виде|как)\s+таблиц|таблицей|табличк", re.I), ChartType.TABLE),
]
_CHANGE_VIEW_RE = re.compile(r"друг\w*\s+(вид|форм|тип|график|диаграмм)|по-?другому|\bиначе\b", re.I)
_SHORT_VIEW_WORDS = 4
_FOLLOW_UP_RE = re.compile(r"подробн|детальн|больше|ещё|еще|раскрой|поясни|объясни|расскажи", re.I)
_POINT_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?:пункт\w*|п\.)\s*(?:№\s*)?(?P<after>\d+|[а-яё]+)|(?P<before>\d+|[а-яё]+)(?:-?[а-яё]{1,3})?\s+пункт", re.I
)
_ORDINALS = {"перв": 1, "втор": 2, "трет": 3, "четв": 4, "пят": 5, "шест": 6}
_HELP_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(привет|здравству|добр\w+\s+(день|утро|вечер)|hello|hi\b)|что\s+ты\s+(умеешь|можешь)|^\s*помощь\s*$", re.I)
_WRITE_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(удали\w*|удалить|сотри|очисти|перезапиши|измени\s+данн\w*|обнови\s+данн\w*|drop|truncate|delete|update|insert|alter)\b",
    re.I,
)


def detect_chart_type(text: str) -> ChartType | None:
    return next((t for pattern, t in _CHART_WORDS if pattern.search(text)), None)


def _point_number(text: str) -> int | None:
    for match in _POINT_RE.finditer(text):
        token = (match.group("after") or match.group("before")).lower()
        if token.isdigit():
            return int(token)
        number = next((n for stem, n in _ORDINALS.items() if token.startswith(stem)), None)
        if number is not None:
            return number
    return None


def route_by_rules(message: str, state: SessionState) -> IntentDecision | None:
    text = message.strip()

    def decision(intent: Intent, question: str | None = None, **kw: object) -> IntentDecision:
        return IntentDecision(standalone_question=question or text, intent=intent, confidence=0.95, **kw)

    if not text:
        return decision(Intent.UNCLEAR)
    if _WRITE_RE.search(text):
        return decision(Intent.OUT_OF_SCOPE)
    if _HELP_RE.search(text) and len(text.split()) <= 6:
        return decision(Intent.HELP)

    last_turn = state.last_turn()
    if last_turn and last_turn.points and _FOLLOW_UP_RE.search(text):
        number = _point_number(text)
        if number is not None:
            return decision(Intent.FOLLOW_UP_DETAIL, point_number=number)

    artifact = state.last_artifact()
    if artifact is not None:
        chart_type = detect_chart_type(text)
        short_request = chart_type is not None and len(text.split()) <= _SHORT_VIEW_WORDS
        if _CHANGE_VIEW_RE.search(text) or short_request:
            return decision(
                Intent.CHANGE_VIEW, artifact.question, wants_chart=chart_type is not ChartType.TABLE, chart_type=chart_type
            )
    return None


class IntentRouter:
    def __init__(self, llm: LLMClient, *, history_turns: int = 4, max_attempts: int = 3):
        self.llm = llm
        self.history_turns = history_turns
        self.max_attempts = max_attempts

    async def route(self, message: str, state: SessionState, catalog: Catalog) -> tuple[IntentDecision, str]:
        by_rules = route_by_rules(message, state)
        if by_rules is not None:
            return by_rules, "rules"
        return await self.classify(message, state, catalog), "llm"

    async def classify(
        self, message: str, state: SessionState, catalog: Catalog, *, allowed: list[Intent] | None = None
    ) -> IntentDecision:
        allowed = allowed or list(Intent)
        if state.last_artifact() is None:
            allowed = [i for i in allowed if i is not Intent.CHANGE_VIEW]
        schema = model_schema(IntentDecision)
        schema["properties"]["intent"] = {"type": "string", "enum": [i.value for i in allowed]}
        prompt = ROUTER_SYSTEM.format(
            intents=render_intents(allowed), tables=render_tables_brief(catalog), examples=render_examples(allowed)
        )
        messages = [system_msg(prompt), *state.history_messages(self.history_turns), user_msg(message)]
        decision = await generate_structured(
            self.llm,
            messages,
            IntentDecision,
            schema=schema,
            schema_name="intent_decision",
            max_attempts=self.max_attempts,
            validate=lambda d: [] if d.intent in allowed else [f"intent должен быть одним из: {', '.join(allowed)}"],
        )
        explicit = detect_chart_type(message)
        if explicit is not None:
            decision = decision.model_copy(update={"chart_type": explicit, "wants_chart": True})
        return decision


'backend\app\agent\schema_linking.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from .contracts.catalog import TIME_TYPES, Catalog, ColumnInfo, SemanticType, TableInfo

_WORD_RE = re.compile(r"\w+", re.U)


def _stems(text: str | None) -> set[str]:
    if not text:
        return set()
    return {w[:5] for w in _WORD_RE.findall(text.lower()) if len(w) > 2}


def _table_score(question: set[str], table: TableInfo) -> float:
    score = 3 * len(question & (_stems(table.name) | _stems(table.display_name) | _stems(table.description)))
    for c in table.columns:
        score += 2 * len(question & (_stems(c.label) | _stems(c.description)))
        values = " ".join(v.value for v in c.top_values) + " " + " ".join(c.sample_values)
        score += len(question & _stems(values))
    return score


def select_tables(question: str, catalog: Catalog, *, focus: list[str] | None = None, max_tables: int = 3) -> list[str]:
    known = {t.name for t in catalog.tables}
    focus = [t for t in (focus or []) if t in known]
    if len(catalog.tables) <= max_tables:
        return [t.name for t in catalog.tables]
    q = _stems(question)
    scored = sorted(((_table_score(q, t), t.name) for t in catalog.tables), reverse=True)
    picked = [name for score, name in scored if score > 0][:max_tables]
    for name in focus:
        if name not in picked:
            picked.append(name)
    if not picked:
        picked = [t.name for t in sorted(catalog.tables, key=lambda t: t.row_count, reverse=True)[:max_tables]]
    return picked


def _column_line(c: ColumnInfo) -> str:
    parts = [c.semantic_type.value]
    if c.unit:
        parts.append(c.unit)
    if c.semantic_type in TIME_TYPES or c.semantic_type is SemanticType.NUMERIC:
        if c.min_value is not None and c.max_value is not None:
            parts.append(f"от {c.min_value} до {c.max_value}")
    elif c.top_values:
        parts.append("напр.: " + ", ".join(v.value for v in c.top_values[:4]))
    elif c.sample_values:
        parts.append("напр.: " + ", ".join(c.sample_values[:3]))
    line = f'  - "{c.name}" ({"; ".join(parts)})'
    if c.display_name and c.display_name != c.name:
        line += f" — в файле «{c.display_name}»"
    if c.description:
        line += f" — {c.description}"
    return line


def render_schema(catalog: Catalog, tables: list[str], *, max_columns: int = 60) -> str:
    lines: list[str] = []
    budget = max_columns
    for name in tables:
        t = catalog.table(name)
        if t is None:
            continue
        header = f'Таблица "{t.name}"'
        if t.display_name and t.display_name != t.name:
            header += f" («{t.display_name}»)"
        header += f", {t.row_count} строк"
        if t.period_start and t.period_end:
            header += f", период {t.period_start} — {t.period_end}"
        lines.append(header + (f". {t.description}" if t.description else ""))
        shown = t.columns[: max(budget, 0)]
        lines.extend(_column_line(c) for c in shown)
        if len(shown) < len(t.columns):
            lines.append(f"  ... ещё {len(t.columns) - len(shown)} колонок")
        budget -= len(shown)
    rels = [r for r in catalog.relations if r.left_table in tables and r.right_table in tables]
    if rels:
        lines.append("Связи:")
        lines.extend(
            f'  - "{r.left_table}"."{r.left_column}" = "{r.right_table}"."{r.right_column}" ({r.cardinality})'
            for r in rels
        )
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_tables_brief(catalog: Catalog) -> str:
    if not catalog.tables:
        return "нет загруженных таблиц"
    return "; ".join(f"{t.name} ({t.label}, {t.row_count} строк)" for t in catalog.tables)


'backend\app\agent\viz.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Collection

from .contracts.catalog import TIME_TYPES, SemanticType
from .contracts.response import CHART_LABELS, ChartSpec, ChartType, ResultTable, TableColumn
from .contracts.tools import ChartHint

MAX_PIE_SLICES = 8


def _distinct(table: ResultTable, column: str) -> int:
    idx = [c.name for c in table.columns].index(column)
    return len({row[idx] for row in table.rows})


def _non_negative(table: ResultTable, column: str) -> bool:
    idx = [c.name for c in table.columns].index(column)
    return all(isinstance(r[idx], int | float) and r[idx] >= 0 for r in table.rows)


def _label(c: TableColumn | None) -> str | None:
    return (c.label or c.name) if c else None


def candidates(
    table: ResultTable, hint: ChartHint | None = None, allowed: Collection[ChartType] | None = None
) -> list[ChartSpec]:
    names = {c.name for c in table.columns}
    num = [c.name for c in table.columns if c.semantic_type is SemanticType.NUMERIC]
    time = [c.name for c in table.columns if c.semantic_type in TIME_TYPES]
    cat = [c.name for c in table.columns if c.semantic_type not in TIME_TYPES and c.semantic_type is not SemanticType.NUMERIC]
    if hint:
        hint_y = [y for y in hint.y if y in names]
        num = hint_y + [n for n in num if n not in hint_y] if hint_y else num
    rows = len(table.rows)
    if rows == 0:
        return []
    specs: list[ChartSpec] = []

    def add(chart_type: ChartType, x: str | None, y: list[str], series: str | None = None) -> None:
        specs.append(ChartSpec(chart_type=chart_type, x=x, y=y, series=series))

    x_hint = hint.x if hint and hint.x in names else None
    if rows == 1 and num and not time:
        add(ChartType.KPI, None, num[:1])
    elif time and num:
        x = x_hint if x_hint in time else time[0]
        series = cat[0] if cat and _distinct(table, cat[0]) <= 10 else None
        add(ChartType.LINE, x, num[:3], series)
        add(ChartType.AREA, x, num[:1], series)
        add(ChartType.BAR, x, num[:1], series)
    elif len(cat) >= 2 and num:
        add(ChartType.HEATMAP, cat[0], num[:1], cat[1])
        add(ChartType.BAR, cat[0], num[:1], cat[1])
    elif cat and num:
        x = x_hint if x_hint in cat else cat[0]
        add(ChartType.BAR, x, num[:3])
        if _distinct(table, x) <= MAX_PIE_SLICES and _non_negative(table, num[0]):
            add(ChartType.PIE, x, num[:1])
        add(ChartType.LINE, x, num[:1])
    elif len(num) >= 2:
        add(ChartType.SCATTER, num[0], [num[1]])
    elif len(num) == 1 and rows > 1:
        add(ChartType.HISTOGRAM, num[0], [])
    add(ChartType.TABLE, None, [])

    if allowed is not None:
        specs = [s for s in specs if s.chart_type in allowed or s.chart_type is ChartType.TABLE]
    if hint and hint.preferred:
        specs.sort(key=lambda s: s.chart_type is not hint.preferred)
    for s in specs:
        s.x_label = _label(table.column(s.x)) if s.x else None
        s.y_label = _label(table.column(s.y[0])) if s.y else None
    return specs


def choose_chart(
    table: ResultTable,
    *,
    hint: ChartHint | None = None,
    requested: ChartType | None = None,
    title: str = "",
    allowed: Collection[ChartType] | None = None,
) -> tuple[ChartSpec | None, str | None]:
    options = candidates(table, hint, allowed)
    if not options:
        return None, None
    note = None
    if requested is not None:
        match = next((s for s in options if s.chart_type is requested), None)
        if match is None:
            match = options[0]
            unsupported = allowed is not None and requested not in allowed and requested is not ChartType.TABLE
            reason = "пока не поддерживается" if unsupported else "не подходит для этих данных"
            note = (
                f"{CHART_LABELS[requested].capitalize()} {reason}, "
                f"поэтому показываю: {CHART_LABELS[match.chart_type]}."
            )
        chosen = match
    else:
        chosen = options[0]
        if chosen.chart_type is ChartType.TABLE:
            return None, None
    return chosen.model_copy(update={"title": title}), note


def alternative_chart(
    table: ResultTable,
    current: ChartType | None,
    hint: ChartHint | None = None,
    allowed: Collection[ChartType] | None = None,
) -> ChartSpec | None:
    options = candidates(table, hint, allowed)
    if not options:
        return None
    types = [s.chart_type for s in options]
    if current in types:
        return options[(types.index(current) + 1) % len(options)]
    return options[0]

'backend\app\api\server.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
import uuid
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, UploadFile
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import Response, StreamingResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.agent.contracts import AgentResponse, Catalog, ChartType, Dashboard, ResultTable, TableInfo
from app.agent.factory import AgentBundle, build_agent
from app.agent.integrations.charts import SUPPORTED_CHART_TYPES, render_png
from app.agent.intents import Intent

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 300 * 1024 * 1024


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4000)
    session_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Пусто — начать новый диалог")


class TurnView(BaseModel):
    id: str
    user_message: str
    intent: Intent | None
    summary: str
    points: list[str]
    created_at: datetime


class SessionView(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    turns: list[TurnView]


class DashboardSummary(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    session_id: str
    widget_count: int
    created_at: datetime


class ChartPngRequest(BaseModel):
    engine_spec: dict[str, Any] = Field(description="ChartBlock.engine_spec из ответа агента")
    data: ResultTable


class HealthView(BaseModel):
    status: str
    model: str | None
    structured_mode: str | None
    tables: int


def create_app(bundle: AgentBundle | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    bundle = bundle or build_agent()
    agent = bundle.agent

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):
        yield
        close = getattr(agent.llm, "aclose", None)
        if close is not None:
            await close()

    app = FastAPI(title="AI-Native BI", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=os.environ.get("CORS_ORIGINS", "*").split(","),
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    @app.get("/api/health", response_model=HealthView)
    async def health() -> HealthView:
        config = getattr(agent.llm, "config", None)
        mode = getattr(agent.llm, "structured_mode", None)
        return HealthView(
            status="ok",
            model=getattr(config, "model", None),
            structured_mode=str(mode) if mode else None,
            tables = len((await agent.catalog_provider.get_catalog()).tables),
        )

    @app.post("/api/chat", response_model=AgentResponse)
    async def chat(request: ChatRequest) -> AgentResponse:
        return await agent.handle(request.session_id or uuid.uuid4().hex, request.message)

    @app.post("/api/chat/stream")
    async def chat_stream(request: ChatRequest) -> StreamingResponse:
        session_id = request.session_id or uuid.uuid4().hex
        queue: asyncio.Queue[str | None] = asyncio.Queue()

        async def emit(event: Any) -> None:
            await queue.put(f"event: {event.type}\ndata: {event.model_dump_json()}\n\n")

        async def run() -> None:
            try:
                await agent.handle(session_id, request.message, emit=emit)
            finally:
                await queue.put(None)

        async def stream():
            task = asyncio.create_task(run())
            try:
                while (chunk := await queue.get()) is not None:
                    yield chunk
            finally:
                await task

        return StreamingResponse(stream(), media_type="text/event-stream", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})

    @app.get("/api/sessions/{session_id}", response_model=SessionView)
    async def session(session_id: str) -> SessionView:
        state = await agent.memory.load(session_id)
        turns = [
            TurnView(id=t.id, user_message=t.user_message, intent=t.intent, summary=t.summary, points=t.points, created_at=t.created_at)
            for t in state.turns
        ]
        return SessionView(session_id=session_id, turns=turns)

    @app.get("/api/catalog", response_model=Catalog)
    async def catalog() -> Catalog:
        return await agent.catalog_provider.get_catalog()

    @app.post("/api/datasets", response_model=TableInfo)
    async def upload_dataset(file: UploadFile) -> TableInfo:
        raw = await file.read(MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1)
        if len(raw) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
            raise HTTPException(413, "Файл слишком большой")
        try:
            return await asyncio.to_thread(bundle.store.add_csv, raw, file.filename or "data.csv")
        except ValueError as e:
            raise HTTPException(422, str(e)) from e

    @app.get("/api/dashboards", response_model=list[DashboardSummary])
    async def dashboards(session_id: str | None = None) -> list[DashboardSummary]:
        items = await bundle.dashboards.list(session_id)
        return [
            DashboardSummary(id=d.id, title=d.title, session_id=d.session_id, widget_count=len(d.widgets), created_at=d.created_at)
            for d in sorted(items, key=lambda d: d.created_at, reverse=True)
        ]

    @app.get("/api/dashboards/{dashboard_id}", response_model=Dashboard)
    async def dashboard(dashboard_id: str) -> Dashboard:
        found = await bundle.dashboards.get(dashboard_id)
        if found is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Дашборд не найден")
        return found

    @app.post("/api/dashboards/{dashboard_id}/refresh", response_model=Dashboard)
    async def refresh_dashboard(dashboard_id: str) -> Dashboard:
        refreshed = await agent.refresh_dashboard(dashboard_id)
        if refreshed is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Дашборд не найден")
        return refreshed

    @app.get("/api/charts/types", response_model=list[ChartType])
    async def chart_types() -> list[ChartType]:
        return sorted(SUPPORTED_CHART_TYPES)

    @app.post("/api/charts/png", response_class=Response, responses={200: {"content": {"image/png": {}}}})
    async def chart_png(request: ChartPngRequest) -> Response:
        try:
            png = await asyncio.to_thread(render_png, request.engine_spec, request.data)
        except ValueError as e:
            raise HTTPException(422, str(e)) from e
        return Response(content=png, media_type="image/png")

    @app.post("/api/datasets/import-db")
    async def import_db(payload: dict):
        from sqlalchemy import create_engine
        from app.agent.integrations.postgres_store import (
            PostgresCatalogProvider,
            PostgresQueryExecutor,
        )
        url = payload["url"]
        schema = payload.get("schema", "public")
        pg_engine = create_engine(
            url, pool_pre_ping=True, connect_args={"connect_timeout": 5}
        )
        provider = PostgresCatalogProvider(pg_engine, schema=schema)
        agent.catalog_provider = provider
        agent.executor = PostgresQueryExecutor(pg_engine)
        catalog = await provider.get_catalog()
        return {"status": "ok", "tables": len(catalog.tables)}

    return app


app = create_app()
