'backend\app\chart_system\base.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from io import BytesIO
from typing import Any

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.figure import Figure

from app.query_system.conditions import ValidationError

VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"int", "float", "str", "date", "bool"})
NUMERIC_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"int", "float"})
BAR_X_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"str", "int", "bool"})
LINE_X_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"date", "int", "float"})
VALID_SORT_DIRS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"asc", "desc"})
BAR_SORT_TARGETS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"x", "y"})

FIGURE_SIZE: tuple[int, int] = (8, 5)
FIGURE_DPI: int = 100


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParamSpec:
    """Specification of a single chart parameter for catalog documentation."""

    name: str
    type: str
    required: bool
    description: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChartTypeSpec:
    """Catalog specification for a chart type."""

    name: str
    description: str
    when_to_use: str
    fields: list[ParamSpec]
    example: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColumnRef:
    """Reference to an output column produced by the query engine."""

    name: str
    description: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """Serialize the column reference to a dictionary."""
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": self.description,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SortSpec:
    """Single sorting rule for chart data."""

    by: str
    dir: str

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """Serialize the sorting rule to a dictionary."""
        return {
            "by": self.by,
            "dir": self.dir,
        }


class ChartBase(ABC):
    """Abstract base class for a chart specification and renderer."""

    name: str = ""

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ChartBase:
        """Dispatch by data['type'] via CHART_REGISTRY and return InvalidChart on error."""
        from app.chart_system.charts import CHART_REGISTRY

        if not isinstance(data, Mapping):
            return InvalidChart(
                raw_data={},
                parse_errors=(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=0,
                        var="",
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=None,
                        value=str(data),
                        valid_options=None,
                    ),
                ),
            )

        parse_errors: list[ValidationError] = []
        raw_type = data.get("type")

        if cls is ChartBase:
            if not isinstance(raw_type, str) or raw_type not in CHART_REGISTRY:
                parse_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=0,
                        var=raw_type if isinstance(raw_type, str) else "",
                        reason="unsupported_operation",
                        field="type",
                        value=str(raw_type) if raw_type is not None else None,
                        valid_options=sorted(CHART_REGISTRY.keys()),
                    )
                )
                return InvalidChart(
                    raw_data=dict(data),
                    parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
                )
            target_cls = CHART_REGISTRY[raw_type]
        else:
            expected_type = next(
                (k for k, v in CHART_REGISTRY.items() if v is cls),
                None,
            )
            if expected_type is not None and raw_type != expected_type:
                parse_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=0,
                        var=str(raw_type) if raw_type is not None else "",
                        reason="unsupported_operation",
                        field="type",
                        value=str(raw_type) if raw_type is not None else None,
                        valid_options=[expected_type],
                    )
                )
                return InvalidChart(
                    raw_data=dict(data),
                    parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
                )
            target_cls = cls

        return target_cls._parse(data, parse_errors)

    @classmethod
    @abstractmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> ChartBase:
        """Build a chart instance while recording structural parse errors."""

    @abstractmethod
    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize the chart specification back into an identical dictionary."""

    @abstractmethod
    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        """Validate the chart specification against the output table schema."""

    @abstractmethod
    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        """Render the chart into PNG bytes using matplotlib."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InvalidChart(ChartBase):
    """Fallback representation for a chart specification with unrecoverable parse errors."""

    name: str = ""
    raw_data: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = ()

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> ChartBase:
        return cls(
            raw_data=dict(data),
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return the original raw dictionary."""
        return dict(self.raw_data)

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        """Return all recorded structural parse errors."""
        return list(self.parse_errors)

    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        """Raise RuntimeError because an invalid chart cannot be rendered."""
        raise RuntimeError("Cannot render an invalid chart specification.")


def parse_title(
    chart_name: str,
    data: Mapping[str, Any],
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> str:
    """Parse and validate the mandatory chart title."""
    raw_title = data.get("title")
    if not isinstance(raw_title, str) or not raw_title.strip():
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field="title",
                value=str(raw_title) if raw_title is not None else None,
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )
        return ""
    return raw_title


def parse_column_ref(
    chart_name: str,
    data: Mapping[str, Any],
    field_name: str,
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> ColumnRef:
    """Parse a ColumnRef object from the given field in data."""
    raw_ref = data.get(field_name)
    return parse_column_ref_item(chart_name, raw_ref, field_name, parse_errors)


def parse_column_ref_item(
    chart_name: str,
    raw_ref: Any,
    field_path: str,
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> ColumnRef:
    """Parse a single ColumnRef mapping at the specified field path."""
    if not isinstance(raw_ref, Mapping):
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field=field_path,
                value=str(raw_ref) if raw_ref is not None else None,
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )
        return ColumnRef(name="", description="")

    raw_name = raw_ref.get("name")
    if not isinstance(raw_name, str) or not raw_name.strip():
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field=f"{field_path}.name",
                value=str(raw_name) if raw_name is not None else None,
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )
        name_val = ""
    else:
        name_val = raw_name

    raw_desc = raw_ref.get("description")
    desc_val = raw_desc if isinstance(raw_desc, str) else ""
    return ColumnRef(name=name_val, description=desc_val)


def parse_sort_specs(
    chart_name: str,
    data: Mapping[str, Any],
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> tuple[SortSpec, ...] | None:
    """Parse an optional list of SortSpec entries."""
    if "sort" not in data or data["sort"] is None:
        return None

    raw_sort = data["sort"]
    if not isinstance(raw_sort, list):
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field="sort",
                value=str(raw_sort),
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )
        return ()

    specs: list[SortSpec] = []
    for idx, item in enumerate(raw_sort):
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=chart_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field=f"sort[{idx}]",
                    value=str(item),
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
            continue
        raw_by = item.get("by")
        raw_dir = item.get("dir")
        by_str = raw_by if isinstance(raw_by, str) else str(raw_by)
        dir_str = raw_dir if isinstance(raw_dir, str) else str(raw_dir)
        specs.append(SortSpec(by=by_str, dir=dir_str))

    return tuple(specs)


def parse_limit(
    chart_name: str,
    data: Mapping[str, Any],
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> int | None:
    """Parse an optional integer limit parameter."""
    if "limit" not in data or data["limit"] is None:
        return None

    raw_limit = data["limit"]
    if isinstance(raw_limit, bool) or not isinstance(raw_limit, int):
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field="limit",
                value=str(raw_limit),
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )
        return None

    return raw_limit


def check_unsupported_field(
    chart_name: str,
    data: Mapping[str, Any],
    field_name: str,
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> None:
    """Record an unsupported_operation error if field_name is present in data."""
    if field_name in data and data[field_name] is not None:
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="unsupported_operation",
                field=field_name,
                value=str(data[field_name]),
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )


def validate_title_value(
    chart_name: str,
    title: str,
    errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> None:
    """Ensure title is non-empty during validation."""
    if not title or not title.strip():
        if not any(e.field == "title" for e in errors):
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=chart_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="title",
                    value=title,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )


def validate_column_ref(
    chart_name: str,
    col_ref: ColumnRef,
    field_path: str,
    schema: Mapping[str, str],
    allowed_types: frozenset[str],
    errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> None:
    """Validate that a ColumnRef has a non-empty name, exists in schema, and matches allowed types."""
    name_field = f"{field_path}.name"
    if not col_ref.name or not col_ref.name.strip():
        if not any(e.field in {field_path, name_field} for e in errors):
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=chart_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field=name_field,
                    value=col_ref.name,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        return

    if col_ref.name not in schema:
        errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="column_not_found",
                field=name_field,
                value=col_ref.name,
                valid_options=sorted(schema.keys()),
            )
        )
        return

    col_type = schema[col_ref.name]
    if col_type not in allowed_types:
        errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="type_mismatch",
                field=name_field,
                value=col_type,
                valid_options=sorted(allowed_types),
            )
        )


def validate_limit_value(
    chart_name: str,
    limit: int | None,
    max_limit: int,
    errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> None:
    """Validate an optional limit against minimum (1) and maximum bounds."""
    if limit is None:
        return

    if isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, int) or limit < 1:
        if not any(e.field == "limit" for e in errors):
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=chart_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="limit",
                    value=str(limit),
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        return

    if limit > max_limit:
        errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=chart_name,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field="limit",
                value=str(limit),
                valid_options=[str(max_limit)],
            )
        )


def validate_sort_specs(
    chart_name: str,
    sort_specs: Sequence[SortSpec] | None,
    allowed_by: Sequence[str] | frozenset[str],
    errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> None:
    """Validate sort rules against allowed target keys and sort directions."""
    if sort_specs is None:
        return

    allowed_set = set(allowed_by)
    valid_by_options = sorted(allowed_set)

    for idx, spec in enumerate(sort_specs):
        if spec.by not in allowed_set:
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=chart_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field=f"sort[{idx}].by",
                    value=spec.by,
                    valid_options=valid_by_options,
                )
            )
        if spec.dir not in VALID_SORT_DIRS:
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=chart_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field=f"sort[{idx}].dir",
                    value=spec.dir,
                    valid_options=sorted(VALID_SORT_DIRS),
                )
            )


def normalize_sort_value(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Normalize runtime values so ISO date strings and date objects sort consistently."""
    if (
        isinstance(value, str)
        and len(value) == 10
        and value[4] == "-"
        and value[7] == "-"
    ):
        try:
            return date.fromisoformat(value)
        except ValueError:
            return value
    return value


def apply_multi_level_sort(
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
    sort_specs: Sequence[SortSpec] | None,
    key_mapping: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Apply stable multi-level sort in reverse key order with NULL values placed last."""
    if not sort_specs:
        return list(rows)

    result = list(rows)
    for spec in reversed(sort_specs):
        col_name = key_mapping.get(spec.by, spec.by) if key_mapping is not None else spec.by
        reverse = spec.dir == "desc"
        non_null = [r for r in result if r.get(col_name) is not None]
        nulls = [r for r in result if r.get(col_name) is None]
        non_null.sort(
            key=lambda r, c=col_name: normalize_sort_value(r[c]),
            reverse=reverse,
        )
        result = non_null + nulls
    return result


def figure_to_png_bytes(fig: Figure) -> bytes:
    """Serialize a matplotlib Figure into PNG bytes and close the figure."""
    try:
        buf = BytesIO()
        fig.savefig(buf, format="png", bbox_inches="tight")
        return buf.getvalue()
    finally:
        plt.close(fig)


def render_empty_figure(title: str) -> bytes:
    """Render a placeholder PNG image with 'нет данных' when input data is empty."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGURE_SIZE, dpi=FIGURE_DPI)
    if title:
        ax.set_title(title)
    ax.axis("off")
    ax.text(
        0.5,
        0.5,
        "нет данных",
        ha="center",
        va="center",
        fontsize=16,
        transform=ax.transAxes,
    )
    return figure_to_png_bytes(fig)

'backend\app\chart_system\catalog.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from app.chart_system.base import ChartTypeSpec
from app.chart_system.charts import ALL_CHART_TYPES


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EngineSpec:
    """Description and structural rules of the chart engine."""

    description: str
    rules: list[str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Capabilities:
    """Supported and unsupported capabilities of the chart engine."""

    allowed: list[str]
    unsupported: list[str]


ENGINE: EngineSpec = EngineSpec(
    description=(
        "Движок графиков представляет собой минималистичный, линейный и предсказуемый "
        "рендерер визуализаций. Одна спецификация описывает ровно один график без "
        "вложенных серий, дашбордов или комбинаций и преобразует табличные данные в PNG."
    ),
    rules=[
        "Одна спека = один график.",
        "Поле title обязательно для всех типов графиков.",
        "Поля x.name, y.name, value.name и columns[].name должны совпадать с именами колонок в выходе query engine.",
        "Поле description внутри ColumnRef предназначено для LLM шага 2 и фронтенда, валидатором не проверяется.",
        "Если данные семантически не соответствуют типу графика (например, bar для временного ряда) — это ошибка LLM шага 1, chart engine её не ловит.",
        "Сортировка sort для графика bar задаётся по осям 'x' или 'y', а не по именам колонок.",
        "Сортировка sort для графика table задаётся по именам колонок из списка columns.",
    ],
)


CAPABILITIES: Capabilities = Capabilities(
    allowed=[
        "Столбчатая диаграмма (bar) для сравнения категорий по числовой метрике с поддержкой многоуровневой сортировки и лимита.",
        "Линейный график (line) для отображения динамики во времени или по упорядоченной числовой оси.",
        "Диаграмма рассеяния (scatter) для анализа связи двух числовых величин.",
        "Карточка метрики (card) для крупного отображения одного ключевого показателя.",
        "Табличное представление (table) для точных значений, малых выборок и универсального fallback-вывода.",
    ],
    unsupported=[
        "Несколько серий на одном графике (grouped bar, stacked bar, multi-line) — поддерживается строго одна серия.",
        "Круговые диаграммы (pie chart) — намеренно не поддерживаются.",
        "Гистограммы распределения, ящики с усами, тепловые карты и графики с заливкой (hist / box / heatmap / area) — вне MVP.",
        "Интерактивность, масштабирование (zoom) и всплывающие подсказки (tooltips) — зона ответственности frontend.",
        "Ручная расстановка границ и масштаба осей — отложена на вторую итерацию.",
    ],
)


_CHART_TYPE_MAP: dict[str, ChartTypeSpec] = {
    spec.name: spec for spec in ALL_CHART_TYPES
}


def get_engine() -> EngineSpec:
    """Return the chart engine specification and structural rules."""
    return ENGINE


def get_capabilities() -> Capabilities:
    """Return the supported and unsupported chart engine capabilities."""
    return CAPABILITIES


def get_chart_type(name: str) -> ChartTypeSpec | None:
    """Return the specification for a single chart type by name, or None if not found."""
    return _CHART_TYPE_MAP.get(name)


def get_chart_types() -> list[ChartTypeSpec]:
    """Return the list of all registered chart type specifications."""
    return list(ALL_CHART_TYPES)


def get_full(json_syntax: bool = False) -> str:
    """Render the complete chart catalog as a single Markdown document for an LLM prompt."""
    lines: list[str] = [
        "# Спецификация движка графиков",
        "",
        ENGINE.description,
        "",
        "## Правила построения спецификации графика",
    ]
    for rule in ENGINE.rules:
        lines.append(f"- {rule}")

    lines.extend(["", "## Поддерживаемые возможности (Allowed)"])
    for item in CAPABILITIES.allowed:
        lines.append(f"- {item}")

    lines.extend(["", "## Неподдерживаемые возможности (Unsupported)"])
    for item in CAPABILITIES.unsupported:
        lines.append(f"- {item}")

    lines.extend(["", "## Каталог типов графиков (Chart Types)"])
    for chart in ALL_CHART_TYPES:
        lines.extend(
            [
                "",
                f"### `{chart.name}`",
                chart.description,
                "",
                f"Когда использовать: {chart.when_to_use}",
                "",
                "Поля:",
            ]
        )
        for param in chart.fields:
            req_label = "обязательный" if param.required else "опциональный"
            lines.append(
                f"- `{param.name}` (`{param.type}`, {req_label}) — {param.description}"
            )
        lines.append("")
        if json_syntax:
            lines.append("Пример:")
            lines.append("```json")
            lines.append(json.dumps(chart.example, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
            lines.append("```")
        else:
            lines.append(f"Пример: `{_format_chart_pseudo(chart.example)}`")

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _format_chart_pseudo(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    chart_type = str(data.get("type", ""))
    parts: list[str] = []

    if "x" in data and isinstance(data["x"], Mapping):
        parts.append(f"x={data['x'].get('name', '')}")
    if "y" in data and isinstance(data["y"], Mapping):
        parts.append(f"y={data['y'].get('name', '')}")
    if "value" in data and isinstance(data["value"], Mapping):
        parts.append(f"value={data['value'].get('name', '')}")
    if "columns" in data and isinstance(data["columns"], list):
        cols = ", ".join(
            str(col.get("name", ""))
            for col in data["columns"]
            if isinstance(col, Mapping)
        )
        parts.append(f"columns=[{cols}]")
    if "sort" in data and isinstance(data["sort"], list) and data["sort"]:
        sort_str = ", ".join(
            f"{item.get('by', '')} {item.get('dir', '')}".strip()
            for item in data["sort"]
            if isinstance(item, Mapping)
        )
        parts.append(f"sort={sort_str}")
    if "limit" in data and data["limit"] is not None:
        parts.append(f"limit={data['limit']}")

    joined = ", ".join(parts)
    return f"{chart_type}: {joined}" if joined else chart_type

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from app.chart_system.base import (
    BAR_SORT_TARGETS,
    BAR_X_TYPES,
    FIGURE_DPI,
    FIGURE_SIZE,
    NUMERIC_TYPES,
    ChartBase,
    ChartTypeSpec,
    ColumnRef,
    ParamSpec,
    SortSpec,
    apply_multi_level_sort,
    figure_to_png_bytes,
    parse_column_ref,
    parse_limit,
    parse_sort_specs,
    parse_title,
    render_empty_figure,
    validate_column_ref,
    validate_limit_value,
    validate_sort_specs,
    validate_title_value,
)
from app.query_system.conditions import ValidationError

DEFAULT_LIMIT: int = 50
MAX_LIMIT: int = 200

BAR_TYPE_SPEC: ChartTypeSpec = ChartTypeSpec(
    name="bar",
    description="Столбчатая диаграмма для сравнения категорий по числовой метрике.",
    when_to_use="Выбирай, когда нужно сравнить дискретные категории, статусы или группы по одному числовому агрегату.",
    fields=[
        ParamSpec(
            name="type",
            type='"bar"',
            required=True,
            description="Идентификатор типа графика",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="title",
            type="str",
            required=True,
            description="Заголовок графика",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="x",
            type="ColumnRef (str|int|bool)",
            required=True,
            description="Категориальная колонка по оси X",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="y",
            type="ColumnRef (int|float)",
            required=True,
            description="Числовая метрика по оси Y",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="sort",
            type='list[{by: "x"|"y", dir: "asc"|"desc"}]',
            required=False,
            description="Правила сортировки столбцов по оси x или y",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="limit",
            type="int",
            required=False,
            description=f"Максимальное число категорий (по умолчанию {DEFAULT_LIMIT}, максимум {MAX_LIMIT})",
        ),
    ],
    example={
        "type": "bar",
        "title": "Заказы по статусам",
        "x": {"name": "status", "description": "статус заказа: paid, pending, cancelled"},
        "y": {"name": "orders_count", "description": "количество заказов в каждом статусе"},
        "sort": [{"by": "y", "dir": "desc"}],
        "limit": 50,
    },
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BarChart(ChartBase):
    """Bar chart implementation comparing discrete categories by a numeric metric."""

    name: str = field(default="bar", init=False)
    title: str
    x: ColumnRef
    y: ColumnRef
    sort: tuple[SortSpec, ...] | None = None
    limit: int | None = None
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> BarChart:
        title = parse_title("bar", data, parse_errors)
        x_ref = parse_column_ref("bar", data, "x", parse_errors)
        y_ref = parse_column_ref("bar", data, "y", parse_errors)
        sort_specs = parse_sort_specs("bar", data, parse_errors)
        limit_val = parse_limit("bar", data, parse_errors)

        return cls(
            title=title,
            x=x_ref,
            y=y_ref,
            sort=sort_specs,
            limit=limit_val,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize the bar chart specification to a dictionary."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "type": "bar",
            "title": self.title,
            "x": self.x.to_dict(),
            "y": self.y.to_dict(),
        }
        if self.sort is not None:
            payload["sort"] = [s.to_dict() for s in self.sort]
        if self.limit is not None:
            payload["limit"] = self.limit
        return payload

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        """Validate bar chart parameters, column types, sort keys, and limit."""
        errors: list[ValidationError] = list(self.parse_errors)

        validate_title_value("bar", self.title, errors)
        validate_column_ref("bar", self.x, "x", schema, BAR_X_TYPES, errors)
        validate_column_ref("bar", self.y, "y", schema, NUMERIC_TYPES, errors)
        validate_sort_specs("bar", self.sort, BAR_SORT_TARGETS, errors)
        validate_limit_value("bar", self.limit, MAX_LIMIT, errors)

        return errors

    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        """Render the bar chart into PNG bytes."""
        if not data:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        rows = [r for r in data if r.get(self.y.name) is not None]
        rows = apply_multi_level_sort(
            rows,
            self.sort,
            key_mapping={"x": self.x.name, "y": self.y.name},
        )
        effective_limit = self.limit if self.limit is not None else DEFAULT_LIMIT
        rows = rows[:effective_limit]

        if not rows:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        x_labels = [
            str(r[self.x.name]) if r.get(self.x.name) is not None else ""
            for r in rows
        ]
        y_values = [float(r[self.y.name]) for r in rows]

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGURE_SIZE, dpi=FIGURE_DPI)
        ax.bar(x_labels, y_values)
        ax.set_title(self.title)
        ax.set_xlabel(self.x.name)
        ax.set_ylabel(self.y.name)
        return figure_to_png_bytes(fig)

'backend\app\chart_system\charts\card.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from app.chart_system.base import (
    FIGURE_DPI,
    FIGURE_SIZE,
    NUMERIC_TYPES,
    ChartBase,
    ChartTypeSpec,
    ColumnRef,
    ParamSpec,
    check_unsupported_field,
    figure_to_png_bytes,
    parse_column_ref,
    parse_title,
    render_empty_figure,
    validate_column_ref,
    validate_title_value,
)
from app.query_system.conditions import ValidationError

CARD_TYPE_SPEC: ChartTypeSpec = ChartTypeSpec(
    name="card",
    description="Карточка с одним ключевым числовым показателем (KPI), отображаемым крупным шрифтом.",
    when_to_use="Выбирай, когда результатом запроса является ровно одно итоговое число (общая выручка, среднее, количество).",
    fields=[
        ParamSpec(
            name="type",
            type='"card"',
            required=True,
            description="Идентификатор типа графика",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="title",
            type="str",
            required=True,
            description="Подпись показателя на карточке",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="value",
            type="ColumnRef (int|float)",
            required=True,
            description="Колонка с единственным числовым значением метрики",
        ),
    ],
    example={
        "type": "card",
        "title": "Общая выручка",
        "value": {"name": "total_revenue", "description": "сумма всех оплаченных заказов"},
    },
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CardChart(ChartBase):
    """Single KPI metric card implementation."""
    name: str = field(default="card", init=False)
    title: str
    value: ColumnRef
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> CardChart:
        title = parse_title("card", data, parse_errors)
        value_ref = parse_column_ref("card", data, "value", parse_errors)
        check_unsupported_field("card", data, "sort", parse_errors)
        check_unsupported_field("card", data, "limit", parse_errors)

        return cls(
            title=title,
            value=value_ref,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "card",
            "title": self.title,
            "value": self.value.to_dict(),
        }

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        errors: list[ValidationError] = list(self.parse_errors)

        validate_title_value("card", self.title, errors)
        validate_column_ref("card", self.value, "value", schema, NUMERIC_TYPES, errors)

        return errors

    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        if not data:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        raw_val = data[0].get(self.value.name)
        if raw_val is None:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGURE_SIZE, dpi=FIGURE_DPI)
        ax.axis("off")
        ax.text(
            0.5,
            0.65,
            self.title,
            ha="center",
            va="center",
            fontsize=16,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
        )
        ax.text(
            0.5,
            0.40,
            str(raw_val),
            ha="center",
            va="center",
            fontsize=36,
            fontweight="bold",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
        )
        return figure_to_png_bytes(fig)

'backend\app\chart_system\charts\line.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from app.chart_system.base import (

    validate_title_value,

from app.query_system.conditions import ValidationError

DEFAULT_LIMIT: int = 1000
MAX_LIMIT: int = 5000

LINE_TYPE_SPEC: ChartTypeSpec = ChartTypeSpec(
    name="line",
    description="Линейный график для отображения динамики во времени или по упорядоченной оси.",
    when_to_use="Выбирай для временных рядов, трендов по датам или непрерывным упорядоченным числовым значениям.",
    fields=[
        ParamSpec(
            name="type",
            type='"line"',
            required=True,
            description="Идентификатор типа графика",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="title",
            type="str",
            required=True,
            description="Заголовок графика",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="x",
            type="ColumnRef (date|int|float)",
            required=True,
            description="Упорядоченная колонка времени или чисел по оси X",
        ),

        ),
    ],
    example={
        "type": "line",
        "title": "Выручка по дням",
        "x": {"name": "created_at", "description": "дата оформления заказа"},
        "y": {"name": "daily_revenue", "description": "суммарная выручка за день"},

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LineChart(ChartBase):
    """Line chart implementation for time series and ordered progressions."""

    name: str = field(default="line", init=False)
    title: str
    x: ColumnRef
    y: ColumnRef
    limit: int | None = None
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> LineChart:
        title = parse_title("line", data, parse_errors)
        x_ref = parse_column_ref("line", data, "x", parse_errors)
        y_ref = parse_column_ref("line", data, "y", parse_errors)
        check_unsupported_field("line", data, "sort", parse_errors)
        limit_val = parse_limit("line", data, parse_errors)

        return cls(
            title=title,
            x=x_ref,
            y=y_ref,
            limit=limit_val,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize the line chart specification to a dictionary."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "type": "line",
            "title": self.title,
            "x": self.x.to_dict(),
            "y": self.y.to_dict(),
        }
        if self.limit is not None:
            payload["limit"] = self.limit
        return payload

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        """Validate line chart parameters, column types, and limit."""
        errors: list[ValidationError] = list(self.parse_errors)

        validate_title_value("line", self.title, errors)
        validate_column_ref("line", self.x, "x", schema, LINE_X_TYPES, errors)
        validate_column_ref("line", self.y, "y", schema, NUMERIC_TYPES, errors)
        validate_limit_value("line", self.limit, MAX_LIMIT, errors)

        return errors

    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        """Render the line chart into PNG bytes."""
        if not data:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        rows = [
            r
            for r in data
            if r.get(self.x.name) is not None and r.get(self.y.name) is not None
        ]
        rows.sort(key=lambda r: r[self.x.name])

        effective_limit = self.limit if self.limit is not None else DEFAULT_LIMIT
        rows = rows[:effective_limit]

        if not rows:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        x_values = [
            str(r[self.x.name]) if not isinstance(r[self.x.name], (int, float)) else r[self.x.name]
            for r in rows
        ]
        y_values = [float(r[self.y.name]) for r in rows]

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGURE_SIZE, dpi=FIGURE_DPI)
        ax.plot(x_values, y_values, marker="o")
        ax.set_title(self.title)
        ax.set_xlabel(self.x.name)
        ax.set_ylabel(self.y.name)
        return figure_to_png_bytes(fig)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScatterChart(ChartBase):
    """Scatter plot implementation for comparing two numeric variables."""
    name: str = field(default="scatter", init=False)
    title: str
    x: ColumnRef
    y: ColumnRef
    limit: int | None = None
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> ScatterChart:
        title = parse_title("scatter", data, parse_errors)
        x_ref = parse_column_ref("scatter", data, "x", parse_errors)
        y_ref = parse_column_ref("scatter", data, "y", parse_errors)
        check_unsupported_field("scatter", data, "sort", parse_errors)
        limit_val = parse_limit("scatter", data, parse_errors)

        return cls(
            title=title,
            x=x_ref,
            y=y_ref,
            limit=limit_val,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "type": "scatter",
            "title": self.title,
            "x": self.x.to_dict(),
            "y": self.y.to_dict(),
        }
        if self.limit is not None:
            payload["limit"] = self.limit
        return payload

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        errors: list[ValidationError] = list(self.parse_errors)

        validate_title_value("scatter", self.title, errors)
        validate_column_ref("scatter", self.x, "x", schema, NUMERIC_TYPES, errors)
        validate_column_ref("scatter", self.y, "y", schema, NUMERIC_TYPES, errors)
        validate_limit_value("scatter", self.limit, MAX_LIMIT, errors)

        return errors

    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        if not data:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        rows = list(data)
        rows = [
            r
            for r in rows
            if r.get(self.x.name) is not None and r.get(self.y.name) is not None
        ]
        effective_limit = self.limit if self.limit is not None else DEFAULT_LIMIT
        rows = rows[:effective_limit]

        if not rows:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        x_values = [float(r[self.x.name]) for r in rows]
        y_values = [float(r[self.y.name]) for r in rows]

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGURE_SIZE, dpi=FIGURE_DPI)
        ax.scatter(x_values, y_values)
        ax.set_title(self.title)
        ax.set_xlabel(self.x.name)
        ax.set_ylabel(self.y.name)
        return figure_to_png_bytes(fig)

'backend\app\chart_system\charts\table.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from app.chart_system.base import (
    FIGURE_DPI,
    FIGURE_SIZE,
    VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES,
    ChartBase,
    ChartTypeSpec,
    ColumnRef,
    ParamSpec,
    SortSpec,
    apply_multi_level_sort,
    figure_to_png_bytes,
    parse_column_ref_item,
    parse_limit,
    parse_sort_specs,
    parse_title,
    render_empty_figure,
    validate_column_ref,
    validate_limit_value,
    validate_sort_specs,
    validate_title_value,
)
from app.query_system.conditions import ValidationError

DEFAULT_LIMIT: int = 100
MAX_LIMIT: int = 10000

TABLE_TYPE_SPEC: ChartTypeSpec = ChartTypeSpec(
    name="table",
    description="Табличное представление для вывода точных значений, детальных записей и выборок с несколькими колонками.",
    when_to_use="Выбирай, когда нужны точные значения нескольких полей, список объектов или когда другие типы графиков не подходят.",
    fields=[
        ParamSpec(
            name="type",
            type='"table"',
            required=True,
            description="Идентификатор типа графика",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="title",
            type="str",
            required=True,
            description="Заголовок таблицы",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="columns",
            type="list[ColumnRef]",
            required=True,
            description="Непустой список отображаемых колонок",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="sort",
            type='list[{by: str, dir: "asc"|"desc"}]',
            required=False,
            description="Правила сортировки по именам колонок из списка columns",
        ),
        ParamSpec(
            name="limit",
            type="int",
            required=False,
            description=f"Максимальное число строк (по умолчанию {DEFAULT_LIMIT}, максимум {MAX_LIMIT})",
        ),
    ],
    example={
        "type": "table",
        "title": "Последние крупные заказы",
        "columns": [
            {"name": "id", "description": "идентификатор заказа"},
            {"name": "status", "description": "статус заказа"},
            {"name": "amount", "description": "сумма заказа"},
        ],
        "sort": [{"by": "amount", "dir": "desc"}],
        "limit": 100,

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TableChart(ChartBase):
    """Tabular visualization implementation supporting multi-column sorting and limits."""
    name: str = field(default="table", init=False)
    title: str
    columns: tuple[ColumnRef, ...]
    sort: tuple[SortSpec, ...] | None = None
    limit: int | None = None
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> TableChart:
        title = parse_title("table", data, parse_errors)

        raw_cols = data.get("columns")
        col_refs: list[ColumnRef] = []
        if not isinstance(raw_cols, list) or len(raw_cols) == 0:
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var="table",
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="columns",
                    value=str(raw_cols) if raw_cols is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for idx, item in enumerate(raw_cols):
                col_refs.append(
                    parse_column_ref_item("table", item, f"columns[{idx}]", parse_errors)
                )

        sort_specs = parse_sort_specs("table", data, parse_errors)
        limit_val = parse_limit("table", data, parse_errors)

        return cls(
            title=title,
            columns=tuple(col_refs),
            sort=sort_specs,
            limit=limit_val,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "type": "table",
            "title": self.title,
            "columns": [col.to_dict() for col in self.columns],
        }
        if self.sort is not None:
            payload["sort"] = [s.to_dict() for s in self.sort]
        if self.limit is not None:
            payload["limit"] = self.limit
        return payload

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        errors: list[ValidationError] = list(self.parse_errors)

        validate_title_value("table", self.title, errors)

        if not self.columns and not any(e.field == "columns" for e in errors):
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var="table",
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="columns",
                    value="[]",
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        for idx, col_ref in enumerate(self.columns):
            validate_column_ref(
                "table",
                col_ref,
                f"columns[{idx}]",
                schema,
                VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES,
                errors,
            )

        allowed_sort_cols = [col.name for col in self.columns if col.name]
        validate_sort_specs("table", self.sort, allowed_sort_cols, errors)
        validate_limit_value("table", self.limit, MAX_LIMIT, errors)

        return errors

    def matplotlib_out(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
        if not data or not self.columns:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        rows = list(data)
        rows = apply_multi_level_sort(rows, self.sort)
        effective_limit = self.limit if self.limit is not None else DEFAULT_LIMIT
        rows = rows[:effective_limit]

        if not rows:
            return render_empty_figure(self.title)

        col_labels = [col.name for col in self.columns]
        cell_text = [
            [
                str(r.get(col.name, "")) if r.get(col.name) is not None else ""
                for col in self.columns
            ]
            for r in rows
        ]

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGURE_SIZE, dpi=FIGURE_DPI)
        ax.axis("off")
        ax.set_title(self.title)
        table_obj = ax.table(
            cellText=cell_text,
            colLabels=col_labels,
            loc="center",
        )
        table_obj.auto_set_font_size(False)
        table_obj.set_fontsize(10)
        table_obj.scale(1.0, 1.2)
        return figure_to_png_bytes(fig)

'backend\app\chart_system\engine.py':
"""Chart execution and validation engine.

Example:
    >>> from app.chart_system import ChartEngine
    >>> engine = ChartEngine()
    >>> raw_spec = {
    ...     "type": "bar",
    ...     "title": "Заказы по статусам",
    ...     "x": {"name": "status", "description": "статус заказа"},
    ...     "y": {"name": "orders_count", "description": "количество заказов"},
    ...     "sort": [{"by": "y", "dir": "desc"}],
    ...     "limit": 50,
    ... }
    >>> spec = engine.from_dict(raw_spec)
    >>> schema = {"status": "str", "orders_count": "int"}
    >>> data = [
    ...     {"status": "paid", "orders_count": 120},
    ...     {"status": "pending", "orders_count": 35},
    ... ]
    >>> validation = engine.validate(spec, schema, row_count=len(data))
    >>> if validation.ok:
    ...     png_bytes = engine.matplotlib_out(spec, data)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.chart_system.base import ChartBase
from app.query_system.conditions import VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES, ValidationError
from app.query_system.query_engine import ValidationResult


class ChartEngine:
    """Linear validator and matplotlib renderer for single-chart specifications."""

    def from_dict(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> ChartBase:
        """Parse a raw dictionary into a ChartBase instance without raising exceptions."""
        return ChartBase.from_dict(data)

    def to_dict(self, spec: ChartBase) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize a ChartBase instance back into a JSON-compatible dictionary."""
        return spec.to_dict()

    def validate(
        self,
        spec: ChartBase,
        schema: dict[str, str],
        row_count: int | None = None,
    ) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validate a chart specification against an output table schema and optional row count."""
        errors: list[ValidationError] = []

        for col_name, col_type in schema.items():
            if col_type not in VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES:
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=-1,
                        var=spec.name,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=f"schema.{col_name}",
                        value=col_type,
                        valid_options=sorted(VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES),
                    )
                )

        if row_count is not None and (isinstance(row_count, bool) or not isinstance(row_count, int) or row_count < 0):
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=-1,
                    var=spec.name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="row_count",
                    value=str(row_count),
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        errors.extend(spec.validate(schema=schema, row_count=row_count))

        return ValidationResult(
            ok=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            output_schema=None,
        )

    def matplotlib_out(
        self,
        spec: ChartBase,
        data: list[dict[str, Any]],
    ) -> bytes:
        """Render the validated chart specification and data rows into PNG bytes."""
        return spec.matplotlib_out(data)

'backend\app\chart_usage.py':
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any


def main() -> None:
    # БД + данные (например, полученные после выполнения QueryEngine)
    schema: dict[str, str] = {
        "country": "str",
        "revenue": "float",
        "orders_cnt": "int",
    }

    data: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"country": "SE", "revenue": 250.0, "orders_cnt": 3},
        {"country": "DE", "revenue": 180.0, "orders_cnt": 2},
        {"country": "FI", "revenue": 95.0, "orders_cnt": 1},
    ]

    # Каталог
    from app.chart_system import (
        get_capabilities,
        get_chart_type,
        get_chart_types,
        get_engine,
        get_full,
    )

    print("1. Работа с каталогом")
    bar_spec = get_chart_type("bar")
    table_spec = get_chart_type("table")
    caps = get_capabilities()

    if bar_spec and table_spec:
        print(f"График '{bar_spec.name}': {bar_spec.description}")
        print(f"График '{table_spec.name}': {table_spec.description}")
    print(
        f"Всего типов графиков: {len(get_chart_types())}, "
        f"поддерживаемых фич: {len(caps.allowed)}, ограничений: {len(caps.unsupported)}"
    )

    prompt_doc = get_full(json_syntax=False)
    print("Фрагмент документа для LLM (первые 12 строк):")
    print("\n".join(prompt_doc.splitlines()[:12]))

    # Движок графиков
    from app.chart_system import ChartEngine

    engine = ChartEngine()

    print("\n2. Рабочая спецификация графика и её валидация.")
    valid_payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "type": "bar",
        "title": "Выручка по странам",
        "x": {"name": "country", "description": "код страны клиента"},
        "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": "суммарная выручка по стране"},
        "sort": [{"by": "y", "dir": "desc"}, {"by": "x", "dir": "asc"}],
        "limit": 50,
    }

    chart = engine.from_dict(valid_payload)
    validation = engine.validate(chart, schema, row_count=len(data))
    print(f"Валидация успешна: {validation.ok}")
    print(f"Сериализация совпадает с исходником: {engine.to_dict(chart) == valid_payload}")

    if validation.ok:
        png_bytes = engine.matplotlib_out(chart, data)
        print(f"Сгенерирован PNG-файл, размер: {len(png_bytes)} байт")

    print("\n3. Спецификация с ошибками. Валидация.")
    invalid_payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "type": "bar",
        "title": "   ",
        "x": {"name": "unknown_col", "description": "несуществующая колонка"},
        "y": {"name": "country", "description": "строковая колонка вместо числовой"},
        "sort": [{"by": "country", "dir": "up"}],
        "limit": 500,
    }

    bad_chart = engine.from_dict(invalid_payload)
    bad_validation = engine.validate(bad_chart, schema, row_count=300)
    print(
        f"Валидация успешна: {bad_validation.ok} "
        f"(найдено ошибок: {len(bad_validation.errors)})"
    )
    for err in bad_validation.errors:
        print(
            f"  [step={err.step_index}, var='{err.var}'] "
            f"reason={err.reason}, field={err.field}, value={err.value}, "
            f"valid_options={err.valid_options}"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'backend\app\db.py':
import os
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv(
    "DATABASE_URL",
    "postgresql+psycopg://mrema:mrema_dev_password@localhost:5432/mrema",
)

engine = create_engine(
    DATABASE_URL,
    pool_pre_ping=True,
    pool_size=5,
    max_overflow=5,
    connect_args={"connect_timeout": 5},
)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False)


def init_db() -> None:
    # Schemas are separate: app metadata vs imported dataset tables.
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS app"))
        conn.execute(text("CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS data"))


'backend\app\importer.py':
import io
import re
import uuid
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

from app.db import SessionLocal
from app.models import Dataset


def safe_column_name(value: Any, used: set[str]) -> str:
    # Normalize headers before creating SQL columns. Original names remain in profile.
    name = str(value).strip().lower()
    name = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_]+", "_", name).strip("_")
    if not name:
        name = "column"
    if name[0].isdigit():
        name = f"col_{name}"
    name = name[:50]
    candidate = name
    suffix = 2
    while candidate in used:
        tail = f"_{suffix}"
        candidate = f"{name[:63-len(tail)]}{tail}"
        suffix += 1
    used.add(candidate)
    return candidate


def _profile_frame(df: pd.DataFrame, original_headers: list[str]) -> list[dict]:
    profile = []
    for i, column in enumerate(df.columns):
        series = df[column]
        dtype = str(series.dtype)
        if pd.api.types.is_bool_dtype(series):
            logical_type = "bool"
        elif pd.api.types.is_integer_dtype(series):
            logical_type = "int"
        elif pd.api.types.is_float_dtype(series):
            logical_type = "float"
        elif pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(series):
            logical_type = "date"
        else:
            logical_type = "str"

        non_null = series.dropna()
        examples = [None if pd.isna(v) else str(v)[:160] for v in non_null.head(5).tolist()]
        item = {
            "name": str(column),
            "original_name": str(original_headers[i]) if i < len(original_headers) else str(column),
            "type": logical_type,
            "pandas_dtype": dtype,
            "null_count": int(series.isna().sum()),
            "unique_count": int(series.nunique(dropna=True)),
            "examples": examples,
        }
        if logical_type in ("int", "float") and len(non_null):
            item["min"] = _json_scalar(non_null.min())
            item["max"] = _json_scalar(non_null.max())
        profile.append(item)
    return profile


def _json_scalar(value):
    if pd.isna(value):
        return None
    if hasattr(value, "item"):
        value = value.item()
    if hasattr(value, "isoformat"):
        return value.isoformat()
    return value


def import_csv_bytes(content: bytes, filename: str, engine: Engine) -> dict:
    # Keep a stable original-header mapping; pandas mangles duplicate headers, which is safer
    # than silently creating duplicate SQL column names.
    try:
        df = pd.read_csv(io.BytesIO(content), low_memory=False)
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        try:
            df = pd.read_csv(io.BytesIO(content), encoding="cp1251", low_memory=False)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"Не удалось прочитать CSV: {exc}") from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"Не удалось прочитать CSV: {exc}") from exc

    if len(df.columns) == 0:
        raise ValueError("CSV не содержит столбцов.")
    if len(df.columns) > 500:
        raise ValueError("Слишком много столбцов: максимум 500.")

    original_headers = [str(c) for c in df.columns]
    used: set[str] = set()
    new_headers = [safe_column_name(c, used) for c in original_headers]
    df.columns = new_headers

    table_name = f"dataset_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:20]}"
    try:
        # Explicit schema and generated table name; column names are normalized.
        df.to_sql(
            name=table_name,
            con=engine,
            schema="data",
            if_exists="fail",
            index=False,
            chunksize=2000,
            method="multi",
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Не удалось записать данные в PostgreSQL: {exc}") from exc

    profile = _profile_frame(df, original_headers)
    try:
        with SessionLocal() as session:
            dataset = Dataset(
                original_filename=filename[:255],
                table_name=table_name,
                row_count=int(len(df)),
                columns_profile=profile,
            )
            session.add(dataset)
            session.commit()
            session.refresh(dataset)
            return {
                "id": dataset.id,
                "original_filename": dataset.original_filename,
                "table_name": dataset.table_name,
                "row_count": dataset.row_count,
                "columns": profile,
            }
    except Exception:
        # Avoid leaving an orphaned imported table if metadata persistence fails.
        with engine.begin() as conn:
            conn.execute(text(f'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS data."{table_name}"'))
        raise


'backend\app\main.py':
import os
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, File, HTTPException, UploadFile
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from app.db import SessionLocal, engine, init_db
from app.importer import import_csv_bytes
from app.models import Base, Dataset


MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOAD_MB", "200")) * 1024 * 1024
ALLOWED_ORIGINS = [
    item.strip()
    for item in os.getenv(
        "CORS_ORIGINS", "http://localhost:5173,http://localhost:3000"
    ).split(",")
    if item.strip()
]


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    init_db()
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    yield


app = FastAPI(
    title="MREMA AI-Native BI API",
    version="0.1.0",
    description="Initial API for CSV import and dataset inspection.",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
    allow_credentials=False,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    try:
        with engine.connect() as conn:
            conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
        return {"status": "ok", "database": "connected"}
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Database unavailable")


@app.get("/api/datasets")
def list_datasets():
    with SessionLocal() as session:
        items = session.query(Dataset).order_by(Dataset.created_at.desc()).all()
        return [
            {
                "id": item.id,
                "original_filename": item.original_filename,
                "table_name": item.table_name,
                "row_count": item.row_count,
                "columns": item.columns_profile,
                "created_at": item.created_at.isoformat() if item.created_at else None,
            }
            for item in items
        ]


@app.post("/api/datasets/import", status_code=201)
async def upload_csv(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    filename = file.filename or "upload.csv"
    if Path(filename).suffix.lower() != ".csv":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=415, detail="Поддерживаются только CSV-файлы.")

    content = await file.read(MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1)
    if len(content) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=413,
            detail=f"Файл превышает лимит {MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES // (1024 * 1024)} MiB.",
        )
    if not content:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Файл пуст.")

    try:
        return import_csv_bytes(content, filename, engine)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        # In production, log full exception server-side; don't return SQL internals.
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Ошибка импорта CSV.") from exc


@app.get("/api/datasets/{dataset_id}")
def get_dataset(dataset_id: int):
    with SessionLocal() as session:
        item = session.get(Dataset, dataset_id)
        if item is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Датасет не найден.")
        return {
            "id": item.id,
            "original_filename": item.original_filename,
            "table_name": item.table_name,
            "row_count": item.row_count,
            "columns": item.columns_profile,
            "created_at": item.created_at.isoformat() if item.created_at else None,
        }


@app.get("/api/datasets/{dataset_id}/preview")
def preview_dataset(dataset_id: int, limit: int = 20, offset: int = 0):
    if limit < 1 or limit > 100:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="limit должен быть от 1 до 100.")
    if offset < 0:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="offset не может быть отрицательным.")

    with SessionLocal() as session:
        item = session.get(Dataset, dataset_id)
        if item is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Датасет не найден.")
        table_name = item.table_name

    # table_name is generated by this service (not user-provided).
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        columns = [
            col["name"]
            for col in inspect(engine).get_columns(table_name, schema="data")
        ]
        result = conn.execute(
            text(f'SELECT * FROM data."{table_name}" LIMIT :lim OFFSET :off'),
            {"lim": limit, "off": offset},
        )
        rows = [dict(row._mapping) for row in result.fetchall()]

    return {
        "dataset_id": dataset_id,
        "columns": columns,
        "rows": rows,
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset,
        "row_count": item.row_count,
    }


@app.get("/api/datasets/{dataset_id}/schema")
def get_dataset_schema(dataset_id: int):
    with SessionLocal() as session:
        item = session.get(Dataset, dataset_id)
        if item is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Датасет не найден.")
        return {
            "dataset_id": item.id,
            "table_name": item.table_name,
            "row_count": item.row_count,
            "columns": item.columns_profile,
        }


'backend\app\models.py':
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, Text, func
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


class Dataset(Base):
    __tablename__ = "datasets"
    __table_args__ = {"schema": "app"}

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    original_filename: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    table_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(63), unique=True, nullable=False)
    row_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    columns_profile: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, server_default=func.now()
    )

'backend\app\query_system\catalog.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParamSpec:
    name: str
    type: str
    required: bool
    description: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StepSpec:
    name: str
    description: str
    parameters: list[ParamSpec]
    example: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ConditionSpec:
    name: str
    description: str
    parameters: list[ParamSpec]
    example: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EngineSpec:
    description: str
    rules: list[str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Capabilities:
    allowed: list[str]
    unsupported: list[str]


ENGINE: EngineSpec = EngineSpec(
    description=(
        "Движок представляет собой линейный интерпретатор аналитических запросов без циклов, "
        "ветвлений и рекурсии. Вместо генерации SQL-запросов модель строит плоскую "
        "последовательность атомарных шагов, где каждый шаг создаёт новую именованную "
        "переменную, а финальный результат определяется обязательным указателем output."
    ),
    rules=[
        "Запрос состоит из непустого списка шагов steps и обязательного поля output.",
        "Каждый шаг обязан иметь уникальное имя переменной в поле var и имя операции в поле op.",
        "Порядок шагов в списке steps строго задаёт порядок их последовательного выполнения.",
        "Каждый шаг может ссылаться в полях input, left или right только на переменные, определённые на предыдущих шагах.",
        "Единственная операция с двумя входами — join (принимает left и right); после неё поток выполнения снова линейный.",
        "Вложенные подзапросы запрещены: любой промежуточный результат должен быть вынесен в отдельный именованный шаг.",
        "Поле output должно ссылаться на существующую переменную, созданную одним из шагов.",
    ],
)


CAPABILITIES: Capabilities = Capabilities(
    allowed=[
        "Чтение таблиц каталога (source) и проекция нужного подмножества колонок (select).",
        "Фильтрация строк (filter) с использованием простых предикатов и логических комбинаторов (and, or, not).",
        "Соединение двух таблиц или промежуточных переменных (join) по одному или нескольким ключам (inner, left, right).",
        "Группировка и агрегация данных (aggregate: count, sum, avg, min, max) с пост-фильтрацией агрегатов через having.",
        "Сортировка по одной или нескольким колонкам (sort), удаление дубликатов (distinct) и ограничение числа строк (limit).",
    ],
    unsupported=[
        "Подзапросы внутри filter (IN SELECT / EXISTS) — ломают линейность конвейера; вместо них используй отдельный шаг и join.",
        "Оконные функции (OVER, PARTITION BY, ROW_NUMBER, LAG/LEAD) — не поддерживаются табличным движком, вне рамок модели исполнения.",
        "Объединение множеств (UNION / INTERSECT / EXCEPT) — нарушает правило единственного двухвходового оператора join.",
        "Условные выражения и вычисляемые колонки (CASE WHEN, арифметика в select) — шаг select выполняет только чистую проекцию существующих колонок.",
        "Именованные табличные выражения (CTE / WITH) и рекурсивные запросы — заменены линейной системой переменных var.",
        "Модификация данных и схемы (INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, DROP, ALTER) — движок предназначен исключительно для аналитического чтения (read-only).",
    ],
)


STEPS: list[StepSpec] = [
    StepSpec(
        name="source",
        description="Загружает исходную таблицу из каталога данных в новую переменную. Выходная схема полностью повторяет схему таблицы.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="table",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя таблицы в каталоге источников",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "T1",
            "op": "source",
            "table": "orders",
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="select",
        description="Оставляет только указанные колонки из входной таблицы без изменения числа строк. Дублирование имён колонок не допускается.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="input",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="columns",
                type="list[str]",
                required=True,
                description="Непустой список имён колонок для выборки",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "C1",
            "op": "select",
            "input": "T1",
            "columns": ["status", "amount"],
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="filter",
        description="Оставляет строки, для которых условие истинно. Схема колонок не меняется.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="input",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="condition",
                type="Condition",
                required=True,
                description="Условие отбора строк",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "F1",
            "op": "filter",
            "input": "T1",
            "condition": {"column": "amount", "cmp": "gt", "value": 15},
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="sort",
        description="Упорядочивает строки по одной или нескольким колонкам (значения NULL всегда располагаются в конце). Схема колонок не меняется.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="input",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="by",
                type='list[{column: str, dir: "asc"|"desc"}]',
                required=True,
                description="Непустой список правил сортировки с указанием колонки и направления",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "S1",
            "op": "sort",
            "input": "F1",
            "by": [{"column": "amount", "dir": "asc"}],
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="limit",
        description="Оставляет первые n строк входной таблицы. Схема колонок не меняется.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="input",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="n",
                type="int",
                required=True,
                description="Максимальное количество возвращаемых строк (целое число >= 0)",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "L1",
            "op": "limit",
            "input": "S1",
            "n": 100,
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="aggregate",
        description="Группирует строки по group_by, вычисляет агрегатные функции и опционально фильтрует результат через having. Выходная схема состоит только из колонок group_by и псевдонимов alias.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="input",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="group_by",
                type="list[str]",
                required=True,
                description="Список колонок группировки (может быть пустым для агрегации по всей таблице)",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="aggregates",
                type='list[{func: "count"|"sum"|"avg"|"min"|"max", column: str, alias: str}]',
                required=True,
                description="Непустой список агрегатов; для count допустим column='*', sum/avg требуют числовых колонок, min/max — числовых или date",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="having",
                type="Condition",
                required=False,
                description="Условие фильтрации после агрегации, проверяемое по выходной схеме (group_by + alias)",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "A1",
            "op": "aggregate",
            "input": "T1",
            "group_by": ["status"],
            "aggregates": [
                {"func": "sum", "column": "amount", "alias": "total_amount"}
            ],
            "having": {"column": "total_amount", "cmp": "gt", "value": 100},
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="distinct",
        description="Удаляет полностью дублирующиеся строки с сохранением порядка первого вхождения. Схема колонок не меняется.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="input",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "D1",
            "op": "distinct",
            "input": "C1",
        },
    ),
    StepSpec(
        name="join",
        description="Соединяет две таблицы по равенству пар колонок. При совпадении имён колонок в левой и правой таблицах к ним добавляются суффиксы _left и _right.",
        parameters=[
            ParamSpec(
                name="left",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя левой переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="right",
                type="str",
                required=True,
                description="Имя правой переменной, определённой выше",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="on",
                type="list[{left_col: str, right_col: str}]",
                required=True,
                description="Непустой список пар совместимых по типу колонок для соединения",
            ),
            ParamSpec(
                name="how",
                type='"inner"|"left"|"right"',
                required=True,
                description="Тип соединения таблиц",
            ),
        ],
        example={
            "var": "J1",
            "op": "join",
            "left": "T1",
            "right": "T2",
            "on": [{"left_col": "user_id", "right_col": "id"}],
            "how": "inner",
        },
    ),
]


CONDITIONS: list[ConditionSpec] = [

_STEP_MAP: dict[str, StepSpec] = {spec.name: spec for spec in STEPS}
_CONDITION_MAP: dict[str, ConditionSpec] = {spec.name: spec for spec in CONDITIONS}


def get_engine() -> EngineSpec:
    """Return the query engine specification and structural rules."""
    return ENGINE


def get_capabilities() -> Capabilities:
    """Return the supported and unsupported engine capabilities."""
    return CAPABILITIES


def get_step(name: str) -> StepSpec | None:
    """Return the specification for a single step operation by name, or None if not found."""
    return _STEP_MAP.get(name)


def get_steps() -> list[StepSpec]:
    """Return the list of all registered step specifications."""
    return list(STEPS)


def get_condition(name: str) -> ConditionSpec | None:
    """Return the specification for a single condition or combinator by name, or None if not found."""
    return _CONDITION_MAP.get(name)


def get_conditions() -> list[ConditionSpec]:
    """Return the list of all registered condition specifications."""
    return list(CONDITIONS)


def get_full(json_syntax: bool = False) -> str:
    """Render the complete engine catalog as a single Markdown document for an LLM prompt."""
    lines: list[str] = [
        "# Спецификация движка запросов",
        "",
        ENGINE.description,
        "",
        "## Правила построения запроса",
    ]
    for rule in ENGINE.rules:
        lines.append(f"- {rule}")

    lines.extend(["", "## Поддерживаемые возможности (Allowed)"])
    for item in CAPABILITIES.allowed:
        lines.append(f"- {item}")
