    lines.extend(["", "## Неподдерживаемые возможности (Unsupported)"])
    for item in CAPABILITIES.unsupported:
        lines.append(f"- {item}")

    lines.extend(["", "## Каталог операций (Steps)"])
    for step in STEPS:
        lines.extend(["", f"### `{step.name}`", step.description, "", "Параметры:"])
        for param in step.parameters:
            req_label = "обязательный" if param.required else "опциональный"
            lines.append(
                f"- `{param.name}` (`{param.type}`, {req_label}) — {param.description}"
            )
        lines.append("")
        if json_syntax:
            lines.append("Пример:")
            lines.append("```json")
            lines.append(json.dumps(step.example, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
            lines.append("```")
        else:
            lines.append(f"Пример: `{_format_step_pseudo(step.example)}`")

    lines.extend(["", "## Каталог условий (Conditions)"])
    for cond in CONDITIONS:
        lines.extend(["", f"### `{cond.name}`", cond.description, "", "Параметры:"])
        for param in cond.parameters:
            req_label = "обязательный" if param.required else "опциональный"
            lines.append(
                f"- `{param.name}` (`{param.type}`, {req_label}) — {param.description}"
            )
        lines.append("")
        if json_syntax:
            lines.append("Пример:")
            lines.append("```json")
            lines.append(json.dumps(cond.example, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
            lines.append("```")
        else:
            lines.append(f"Пример: `{_format_condition_pseudo(cond.example)}`")

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _format_scalar(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return f'"{value}"'
    return str(value)


def _format_leaf_condition_pseudo(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    col = str(data.get("column", ""))
    cmp_op = str(data.get("cmp", ""))
    symbol_map = {
        "eq": "==",
        "ne": "!=",
        "gt": ">",
        "lt": "<",
        "ge": ">=",
        "le": "<=",
    }
    if cmp_op in symbol_map:
        return f"{col} {symbol_map[cmp_op]} {_format_scalar(data.get('value'))}"
    if cmp_op == "in":
        vals = ", ".join(_format_scalar(v) for v in data.get("values", []))
        return f"{col} in [{vals}]"
    if cmp_op == "not_in":
        vals = ", ".join(_format_scalar(v) for v in data.get("values", []))
        return f"{col} not in [{vals}]"
    if cmp_op == "between":
        low = _format_scalar(data.get("min"))
        high = _format_scalar(data.get("max"))
        return f"{col} between {low} and {high}"
    if cmp_op == "like":
        return f"{col} like {_format_scalar(data.get('pattern'))}"
    if cmp_op == "is_null":
        return f"{col} is null"
    if cmp_op == "is_not_null":
        return f"{col} is not null"
    return f"{col} {cmp_op}"


def _format_condition_pseudo(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    work_stack: list[tuple[Mapping[str, Any], bool]] = [(data, False)]
    result_stack: list[str] = []

    while work_stack:
        current, visited = work_stack.pop()
        if "and" in current or "or" in current:
            op = "and" if "and" in current else "or"
            children = current[op]
            if not visited:
                work_stack.append((current, True))
                for child in children:
                    work_stack.append((child, False))
            else:
                parts = [f"({result_stack.pop()})" for _ in range(len(children))]
                result_stack.append(f" {op} ".join(parts))
        elif "not" in current:
            if not visited:
                work_stack.append((current, True))
                work_stack.append((current["not"], False))
            else:
                result_stack.append(f"not ({result_stack.pop()})")
        else:
            result_stack.append(_format_leaf_condition_pseudo(current))

    return result_stack[0]


def _format_step_pseudo(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    var = data.get("var", "")
    op = data.get("op", "")
    if op == "source":
        return f'{var} = source("{data.get("table", "")}")'
    if op == "select":
        cols = ", ".join(data.get("columns", []))
        return f"{var} = select({data.get('input', '')}, [{cols}])"
    if op == "filter":
        cond_str = _format_condition_pseudo(data.get("condition", {}))
        return f"{var} = filter({data.get('input', '')}, {cond_str})"
    if op == "sort":
        by_parts = [f"{item['column']} {item['dir']}" for item in data.get("by", [])]
        return f"{var} = sort({data.get('input', '')}, [{', '.join(by_parts)}])"
    if op == "limit":
        return f"{var} = limit({data.get('input', '')}, {data.get('n', 0)})"
    if op == "aggregate":
        gb = ", ".join(data.get("group_by", []))
        aggs = ", ".join(
            f"{a['func']}({a['column']}) as {a['alias']}"
            for a in data.get("aggregates", [])
        )
        base = f"{var} = aggregate({data.get('input', '')}, group_by=[{gb}], [{aggs}]"
        if "having" in data and data["having"] is not None:
            having_str = _format_condition_pseudo(data["having"])
            return f"{base}, having=({having_str}))"
        return f"{base})"
    if op == "distinct":
        return f"{var} = distinct({data.get('input', '')})"
    if op == "join":
        on_parts = [
            f"{pair['left_col']} == {pair['right_col']}"
            for pair in data.get("on", [])
        ]
        return (
            f"{var} = join({data.get('left', '')}, {data.get('right', '')}, "
            f'on=[{", ".join(on_parts)}], how="{data.get("how", "inner")}")'
        )
    return f"{var} = {op}()"

'backend\app\query_system\conditions.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from abc import ABC
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any


VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"int", "float", "str", "date", "bool"})
NUMERIC_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"int", "float"})
ORDERABLE_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"int", "float", "date", "str"})

SCALAR_COMPARISONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"eq", "ne", "gt", "lt", "ge", "le"})
ORDERING_COMPARISONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"gt", "lt", "ge", "le", "between"})
SET_COMPARISONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"in", "not_in"})
NULL_COMPARISONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"is_null", "is_not_null"})
ALL_COMPARISONS: frozenset[str] = (
    SCALAR_COMPARISONS
    | SET_COMPARISONS
    | NULL_COMPARISONS
    | frozenset({"between", "like"})
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ValidationError:
    """Structured schema or syntax validation error."""

    step_index: int
    var: str
    reason: str
    field: str | None = None
    value: str | None = None
    valid_options: list[str] | None = None

    def with_context(self, step_index: int, var: str) -> ValidationError:
        return ValidationError(
            step_index=step_index,
            var=var,
            reason=self.reason,
            field=self.field,
            value=self.value,
            valid_options=self.valid_options,
        )


class Condition(ABC):
    """Base condition representation supporting iterative parsing, validation, and evaluation."""

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Any) -> Condition:
        """Iteratively parse a raw dictionary into a Condition instance without recursion."""
        work_stack: list[tuple[Any, bool]] = [(data, False)]
        result_stack: list[Condition] = []

        while work_stack:
            current, visited = work_stack.pop()

            if not isinstance(current, Mapping):
                result_stack.append(
                    InvalidCondition(
                        raw_data=current,
                        message="Condition must be a mapping",
                        field_suffix=None,
                        value=str(current),
                    )
                )
                continue

            has_and = "and" in current
            has_or = "or" in current
            has_not = "not" in current
            combinator_count = int(has_and) + int(has_or) + int(has_not)

            if combinator_count > 1 or (combinator_count == 1 and len(current) > 1):
                result_stack.append(
                    InvalidCondition(
                        raw_data=dict(current),
                        message="Ambiguous combinator keys",
                        field_suffix=None,
                        value=str(dict(current)),
                    )
                )
                continue

            if has_and or has_or:
                op = "and" if has_and else "or"
                raw_children = current[op]
                if not isinstance(raw_children, list) or len(raw_children) == 0:
                    result_stack.append(
                        InvalidCondition(
                            raw_data=dict(current),
                            message=f"Combinator '{op}' requires a non-empty list",
                            field_suffix=op,
                            value=str(raw_children),
                        )
                    )
                    continue

                if not visited:
                    work_stack.append((current, True))
                    for child in raw_children:
                        work_stack.append((child, False))
                else:
                    children = [result_stack.pop() for _ in range(len(raw_children))]
                    result_stack.append(
                        LogicalCondition(op=op, conditions=tuple(children))
                    )
                continue

            if has_not:
                raw_child = current["not"]
                if not visited:
                    work_stack.append((current, True))
                    work_stack.append((raw_child, False))
                else:
                    child_cond = result_stack.pop()
                    result_stack.append(NotCondition(condition=child_cond))
                continue

            result_stack.append(_parse_leaf_condition(current))

        return result_stack[0]

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Iteratively serialize the condition back to its dictionary representation."""
        work_stack: list[tuple[Condition, bool]] = [(self, False)]
        result_stack: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        while work_stack:
            node, visited = work_stack.pop()

            if isinstance(node, LogicalCondition):
                if not visited:
                    work_stack.append((node, True))
                    for child in node.conditions:
                        work_stack.append((child, False))
                else:
                    serialized = [
                        result_stack.pop() for _ in range(len(node.conditions))
                    ]
                    result_stack.append({node.op: serialized})
            elif isinstance(node, NotCondition):
                if not visited:
                    work_stack.append((node, True))
                    work_stack.append((node.condition, False))
                else:
                    result_stack.append({"not": result_stack.pop()})
            elif isinstance(node, ComparisonCondition):
                result_stack.append(node._leaf_to_dict())
            elif isinstance(node, InvalidCondition):
                if isinstance(node.raw_data, Mapping):
                    result_stack.append(dict(node.raw_data))
                else:
                    result_stack.append({"invalid": node.raw_data})

        return result_stack[0]

    def validate(
        self,
        schema: Mapping[str, str],
        step_index: int = 0,
        var: str = "",
        field_prefix: str = "condition",
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        """Iteratively validate the condition against a column schema."""
        errors: list[ValidationError] = []
        work_stack: list[tuple[Condition, str]] = [(self, field_prefix)]

        while work_stack:
            node, path = work_stack.pop()

            if isinstance(node, InvalidCondition):
                field_path = (
                    f"{path}.{node.field_suffix}" if node.field_suffix else path
                )
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="invalid_condition",
                        field=field_path,
                        value=node.value,
                        valid_options=node.valid_options,
                    )
                )
            elif isinstance(node, LogicalCondition):
                if not node.conditions:
                    errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_condition",
                            field=f"{path}.{node.op}",
                            value="[]",
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    for idx in range(len(node.conditions) - 1, -1, -1):
                        work_stack.append(
                            (node.conditions[idx], f"{path}.{node.op}[{idx}]")
                        )
            elif isinstance(node, NotCondition):
                work_stack.append((node.condition, f"{path}.not"))
            elif isinstance(node, ComparisonCondition):
                errors.extend(
                    node._validate_leaf(
                        schema=schema,
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        path=path,
                    )
                )

        return errors

    def evaluate(self, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Iteratively evaluate the condition for a single row."""
        work_stack: list[tuple[Condition, bool]] = [(self, False)]
        result_stack: list[bool] = []

        while work_stack:
            node, visited = work_stack.pop()

            if isinstance(node, LogicalCondition):
                if not visited:
                    work_stack.append((node, True))
                    for child in node.conditions:
                        work_stack.append((child, False))
                else:
                    child_results = [
                        result_stack.pop() for _ in range(len(node.conditions))
                    ]
                    if node.op == "and":
                        result_stack.append(all(child_results))
                    else:
                        result_stack.append(any(child_results))
            elif isinstance(node, NotCondition):
                if not visited:
                    work_stack.append((node, True))
                    work_stack.append((node.condition, False))
                else:
                    result_stack.append(not result_stack.pop())
            elif isinstance(node, ComparisonCondition):
                result_stack.append(node._evaluate_leaf(row))
            else:
                raise RuntimeError("Cannot evaluate an invalid condition.")

        return result_stack[0]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LogicalCondition(Condition):
    op: str
    conditions: tuple[Condition, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NotCondition(Condition):
    condition: Condition


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InvalidCondition(Condition):
    raw_data: Any
    message: str
    field_suffix: str | None = None
    value: str | None = None
    valid_options: list[str] | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ComparisonCondition(Condition):
    column: str
    cmp: str
    value: Any = None
    values: tuple[Any, ...] | None = None
    min_value: Any = None
    max_value: Any = None
    pattern: Any = None
    raw_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    def _leaf_to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if self.cmp in SCALAR_COMPARISONS:
            return {
                "column": self.column,
                "cmp": self.cmp,
                "value": self.value,
            }
        if self.cmp in SET_COMPARISONS:
            return {
                "column": self.column,
                "cmp": self.cmp,
                "values": list(self.values) if self.values is not None else [],
            }
        if self.cmp == "between":
            return {
                "column": self.column,
                "cmp": "between",
                "min": self.min_value,
                "max": self.max_value,
            }
        if self.cmp == "like":
            return {
                "column": self.column,
                "cmp": "like",
                "pattern": self.pattern,
            }
        return {
            "column": self.column,
            "cmp": self.cmp,
        }

    def _validate_leaf(
        self,
        schema: Mapping[str, str],
        step_index: int,
        var: str,
        path: str,
    ) -> list[ValidationError]:
        errors: list[ValidationError] = []

        if self.column not in schema:
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=var,
                    reason="column_not_found",
                    field=f"{path}.column",
                    value=self.column,
                    valid_options=sorted(schema.keys()),
                )
            )
            return errors

        col_type = schema[self.column]

        if self.cmp in NULL_COMPARISONS:
            return errors

        if self.cmp in ORDERING_COMPARISONS and col_type not in ORDERABLE_TYPES:
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=var,
                    reason="type_mismatch",
                    field=f"{path}.column",
                    value=col_type,
                    valid_options=sorted(ORDERABLE_TYPES),
                )
            )
            return errors

        if self.cmp == "like":
            if col_type != "str":
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="type_mismatch",
                        field=f"{path}.column",
                        value=col_type,
                        valid_options=["str"],
                    )
                )
            if not isinstance(self.pattern, str):
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="type_mismatch",
                        field=f"{path}.pattern",
                        value=str(self.pattern),
                        valid_options=["str"],
                    )
                )
            return errors

        if self.cmp in SCALAR_COMPARISONS:
            if not is_value_compatible(self.value, col_type):
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="type_mismatch",
                        field=f"{path}.value",
                        value=str(self.value),
                        valid_options=[col_type],
                    )
                )
            return errors

        if self.cmp in SET_COMPARISONS:
            if self.values is None or len(self.values) == 0:
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="invalid_condition",
                        field=f"{path}.values",
                        value=str(self.values),
                        valid_options=None,
                    )
                )
                return errors

            for idx, item in enumerate(self.values):
                if not is_value_compatible(item, col_type):
                    errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=var,
                            reason="type_mismatch",
                            field=f"{path}.values[{idx}]",
                            value=str(item),
                            valid_options=[col_type],
                        )
                    )
            return errors

        if self.cmp == "between":
            if not is_value_compatible(self.min_value, col_type):
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="type_mismatch",
                        field=f"{path}.min",
                        value=str(self.min_value),
                        valid_options=[col_type],
                    )
                )
            if not is_value_compatible(self.max_value, col_type):
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=var,
                        reason="type_mismatch",
                        field=f"{path}.max",
                        value=str(self.max_value),
                        valid_options=[col_type],
                    )
                )
            return errors

        return errors

    def _evaluate_leaf(self, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
        raw_cell = row.get(self.column)

        if self.cmp == "is_null":
            return raw_cell is None
        if self.cmp == "is_not_null":
            return raw_cell is not None

        if raw_cell is None:
            return False

        cell = normalize_runtime_value(raw_cell)

        if self.cmp == "eq":
            return cell == normalize_runtime_value(self.value)
        if self.cmp == "ne":
            return cell != normalize_runtime_value(self.value)
        if self.cmp == "gt":
            return cell > normalize_runtime_value(self.value)
        if self.cmp == "lt":
            return cell < normalize_runtime_value(self.value)
        if self.cmp == "ge":
            return cell >= normalize_runtime_value(self.value)
        if self.cmp == "le":
            return cell <= normalize_runtime_value(self.value)
        if self.cmp == "in":
            targets = [
                normalize_runtime_value(v) for v in (self.values or ())
            ]
            return cell in targets
        if self.cmp == "not_in":
            targets = [
                normalize_runtime_value(v) for v in (self.values or ())
            ]
            return cell not in targets
        if self.cmp == "between":
            low = normalize_runtime_value(self.min_value)
            high = normalize_runtime_value(self.max_value)
            return low <= cell <= high
        if self.cmp == "like":
            if not isinstance(raw_cell, str) or not isinstance(self.pattern, str):
                return False
            regex = _like_to_regex(self.pattern)
            return regex.fullmatch(raw_cell) is not None

        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported comparison operator: {self.cmp}")


def _parse_leaf_condition(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Condition:
    raw_col = data.get("column")
    raw_cmp = data.get("cmp")

    if not isinstance(raw_col, str) or not raw_col.strip():
        return InvalidCondition(
            raw_data=dict(data),
            message="Missing or invalid 'column'",
            field_suffix="column",
            value=str(raw_col) if raw_col is not None else None,
        )

    if not isinstance(raw_cmp, str) or raw_cmp not in ALL_COMPARISONS:
        return InvalidCondition(
            raw_data=dict(data),
            message="Unsupported 'cmp' operator",
            field_suffix="cmp",
            value=str(raw_cmp) if raw_cmp is not None else None,
            valid_options=sorted(ALL_COMPARISONS),
        )

    if raw_cmp in SCALAR_COMPARISONS:
        if "value" not in data or data["value"] is None:
            return InvalidCondition(
                raw_data=dict(data),
                message=f"Operator '{raw_cmp}' requires 'value'",
                field_suffix="value",
                value=None,
            )
        return ComparisonCondition(
            column=raw_col,
            cmp=raw_cmp,
            value=data["value"],
            raw_data=dict(data),
        )

    if raw_cmp in SET_COMPARISONS:
        raw_values = data.get("values")
        if not isinstance(raw_values, list) or len(raw_values) == 0:
            return InvalidCondition(
                raw_data=dict(data),
                message=f"Operator '{raw_cmp}' requires non-empty 'values' list",
                field_suffix="values",
                value=str(raw_values) if raw_values is not None else None,
            )
        return ComparisonCondition(
            column=raw_col,
            cmp=raw_cmp,
            values=tuple(raw_values),
            raw_data=dict(data),
        )

    if raw_cmp == "between":
        if "min" not in data or data["min"] is None:
            return InvalidCondition(
                raw_data=dict(data),
                message="Operator 'between' requires 'min'",
                field_suffix="min",
                value=None,
            )
        if "max" not in data or data["max"] is None:
            return InvalidCondition(
                raw_data=dict(data),
                message="Operator 'between' requires 'max'",
                field_suffix="max",
                value=None,
            )
        return ComparisonCondition(
            column=raw_col,
            cmp="between",
            min_value=data["min"],
            max_value=data["max"],
            raw_data=dict(data),
        )

    if raw_cmp == "like":
        if "pattern" not in data or data["pattern"] is None:
            return InvalidCondition(
                raw_data=dict(data),
                message="Operator 'like' requires 'pattern'",
                field_suffix="pattern",
                value=None,
            )
        return ComparisonCondition(
            column=raw_col,
            cmp="like",
            pattern=data["pattern"],
            raw_data=dict(data),
        )

    return ComparisonCondition(
        column=raw_col,
        cmp=raw_cmp,
        raw_data=dict(data),
    )


def is_value_compatible(value: Any, schema_type: str) -> bool:
    """Check whether a literal value conforms to a declared schema type."""
    if value is None:
        return False

    if schema_type == "bool":
        return isinstance(value, bool)

    if schema_type == "int":
        return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)

    if schema_type == "float":
        return isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool)

    if schema_type == "str":
        return isinstance(value, str)

    if schema_type == "date":
        if isinstance(value, date) and not isinstance(value, datetime):
            return True
        if isinstance(value, str):
            try:
                date.fromisoformat(value)
                return True
            except ValueError:
                return False
        return False

    return False


def normalize_runtime_value(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Normalize runtime values so ISO date strings and date objects compare consistently."""
    if isinstance(value, str) and len(value) == 10 and value[4] == "-" and value[7] == "-":
        try:
            return date.fromisoformat(value)
        except ValueError:
            return value
    return value


def _like_to_regex(pattern: str) -> re.Pattern[str]:
    parts: list[str] = ["^"]
    for ch in pattern:
        if ch == "%":
            parts.append(".*")
        elif ch == "_":
            parts.append(".")
        else:
            parts.append(re.escape(ch))
    parts.append("$")
    return re.compile("".join(parts), re.DOTALL)

'backend\app\query_system\query_engine.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from app.query_system.conditions import VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES, ValidationError
from app.query_system.steps import Step, Table


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ValidationResult:
    """Result of static schema validation for a Query."""

    ok: bool
    errors: list[ValidationError]
    output_schema: dict[str, str] | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Query:
    """Linear sequence of named query steps and a designated output variable."""

    steps: tuple[Step, ...]
    output: str
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)
    raw_data: dict[str, Any] | None = field(default=None, repr=False)

    @property
    def ok(self) -> bool:
        return len(self.parse_errors) == 0


class QueryEngine:
    def from_dict(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Query:
        """Parse a raw dictionary into a Query object without raising exceptions."""
        if not isinstance(data, Mapping):
            return Query(
                steps=(),
                output="",
                parse_errors=(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=-1,
                        var="",
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=None,
                        value=str(data),
                        valid_options=None,
                    ),
                ),
                raw_data=None,
            )

        parse_errors: list[ValidationError] = []
        raw_steps = data.get("steps")
        parsed_steps: list[Step] = []

        if not isinstance(raw_steps, list) or len(raw_steps) == 0:
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=-1,
                    var="",
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="steps",
                    value=str(raw_steps) if raw_steps is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for step_dict in raw_steps:
                parsed_steps.append(Step.from_dict(step_dict))

        raw_output = data.get("output")
        if not isinstance(raw_output, str) or not raw_output.strip():
            output_str = ""
        else:
            output_str = raw_output

        return Query(
            steps=tuple(parsed_steps),
            output=output_str,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
            raw_data=dict(data),
        )

    def to_dict(self, query: Query) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize a Query back into an identical JSON-compatible dictionary."""
        return {
            "steps": [step.to_dict() for step in query.steps],
            "output": query.output,
        }

    def validate(
        self,
        query: Query,
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
    ) -> ValidationResult:
        """Linearly validate all steps in a Query against a catalog schema."""
        errors: list[ValidationError] = list(query.parse_errors)
        env_schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {}
        defined_vars: set[str] = set()

        for table_name, table_cols in schema.items():
            for col_name, col_type in table_cols.items():
                if col_type not in VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES:
                    errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=-1,
                            var="",
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"schema.{table_name}.{col_name}",
                            value=col_type,
                            valid_options=sorted(VALID_SCHEMA_TYPES),
                        )
                    )

        for idx, step in enumerate(query.steps):
            var_name = step.var
            has_duplicate_var = False

            if var_name:
                if var_name in defined_vars:
                    has_duplicate_var = True
                    errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=idx,
                            var=var_name,
                            reason="duplicate_var",
                            field="var",
                            value=var_name,
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )

            step_input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {}
            for field_name, ref_var in step.input_refs:
                if not ref_var:
                    continue
                if ref_var not in defined_vars:
                    errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=idx,
                            var=var_name,
                            reason="undefined_reference",
                            field=field_name,
                            value=ref_var,
                            valid_options=sorted(defined_vars),
                        )
                    )
                elif ref_var in env_schema:
                    step_input_schemas[ref_var] = env_schema[ref_var]

            step_errors: list[ValidationError] = []
            out_schema = step.validate(
                input_schemas=step_input_schemas,
                schema=schema,
                errors=step_errors,
                step_index=idx,
            )
            errors.extend(step_errors)

            if var_name:
                defined_vars.add(var_name)
                if not has_duplicate_var and not step_errors and out_schema:
                    env_schema[var_name] = out_schema

        if not query.output or query.output not in defined_vars:
            errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=-1,
                    var=query.output,
                    reason="output_not_found",
                    field="output",
                    value=query.output if query.output else None,
                    valid_options=sorted(defined_vars),
                )
            )

        ok = len(errors) == 0
        final_schema = env_schema.get(query.output) if ok else None
        return ValidationResult(
            ok=ok,
            errors=errors,
            output_schema=final_schema,
        )

    def execute(
        self,
        query: Query,
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        """Execute a validated Query linearly and return the output variable's Table."""
        env: dict[str, Table] = {}
        for step in query.steps:
            env[step.var] = step.execute(env, sources)
        return env[query.output]

'backend\app\query_system\steps.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from app.query_system.conditions import (
    NUMERIC_TYPES,
    ORDERABLE_TYPES,
    Condition,
    ValidationError,
    normalize_runtime_value,
)


class Table:
    """Immutable tabular relation used during query execution."""

    __slots__ = ("_columns", "_rows")

    def __init__(
        self,
        columns: Sequence[str],
        rows: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
    ) -> None:
        col_tuple = tuple(columns)
        self._columns: tuple[str, ...] = col_tuple
        self._rows: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...] = tuple(
            {col: row.get(col) for col in col_tuple} for row in rows
        )

    @property
    def columns(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        return self._columns

    @property
    def rows(self) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
        return self._rows

    @classmethod
    def from_source(
        cls,
        raw: Any,
        expected_columns: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    ) -> Table:
        if isinstance(raw, Table):
            if expected_columns is None:
                return raw
            return cls(expected_columns, raw.rows)

        if hasattr(raw, "to_dict") and callable(raw.to_dict):
            records = raw.to_dict(orient="records")
            cols = (
                 list(expected_columns)
                if expected_columns is not None
                else list(getattr(raw, "columns", ()))
            )
            if not cols and records:
                cols = list(records[0].keys())
            return cls(cols, records)

        if isinstance(raw, Sequence):
            records_list = list(raw)
            cols = list(expected_columns) if expected_columns is not None else []
            if not cols and records_list and isinstance(records_list[0], Mapping):
                cols = list(records_list[0].keys())
            return cls(cols, records_list)

        raise TypeError(f"Unsupported table source type: {type(raw).__name__}")

    def to_dicts(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [dict(row) for row in self._rows]

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._rows)

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(self._rows)

    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(other, Table):
            return NotImplemented
        return self._columns == other._columns and self._rows == other._rows


STEP_REGISTRY: dict[str, type[Step]] = {}

VALID_SORT_DIRS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"asc", "desc"})
VALID_JOIN_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"inner", "left", "right"})
VALID_AGG_FUNCS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"count", "sum", "avg", "min", "max"})


def register_step(op_name: str) -> Callable[[type[Step]], type[Step]]:
    """Register a Step implementation in the global operation catalog."""

    def decorator(cls: type[Step]) -> type[Step]:
        cls.op = op_name
        STEP_REGISTRY[op_name] = cls
        return cls

    return decorator


class Step(ABC):
    """Abstract base class for a linear query pipeline operation."""

    op: str = ""
    var: str

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return ()

    @abstractmethod
    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        """Validate step parameters against input schemas and return output schema."""

    @abstractmethod
    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        """Execute the operation against the runtime environment and table sources."""

    @abstractmethod
    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize the step back to its dictionary representation."""

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Step:
        """Deserialize a dictionary into a concrete Step instance without raising."""
        if not isinstance(data, Mapping):
            return InvalidStep(
                var="",
                raw_data={},
                parse_errors=(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=0,
                        var="",
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=None,
                        value=str(data),
                        valid_options=None,
                    ),
                ),
            )

        raw_var = data.get("var")
        var_name = raw_var if isinstance(raw_var, str) else ""
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError] = []

        if not isinstance(raw_var, str) or not raw_var.strip():
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var_name,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="var",
                    value=str(raw_var) if raw_var is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        raw_op = data.get("op")
        if cls is Step:
            if not isinstance(raw_op, str) or raw_op not in STEP_REGISTRY:
                parse_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=0,
                        var=var_name,
                        reason="unsupported_operation",
                        field="op",
                        value=str(raw_op) if raw_op is not None else None,
                        valid_options=sorted(STEP_REGISTRY.keys()),
                    )
                )
                return InvalidStep(
                    var=var_name,
                    raw_data=dict(data),
                    parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
                )
            target_cls = STEP_REGISTRY[raw_op]
        else:
            if cls.op and raw_op != cls.op:
                parse_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=0,
                        var=var_name,
                        reason="unsupported_operation",
                        field="op",
                        value=str(raw_op) if raw_op is not None else None,
                        valid_options=[cls.op],
                    )
                )
                return InvalidStep(
                    var=var_name,
                    raw_data=dict(data),
                    parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
                )
            target_cls = cls

        step = target_cls._parse(var_name, data, parse_errors)
        return step

    @classmethod
    @abstractmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        """Build a step instance while recording any structural parse errors."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InvalidStep(Step):
    var: str
    raw_data: dict[str, Any]
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = ()
    input_ref_pairs: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return self.input_ref_pairs

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        if errors is not None:
            for err in self.parse_errors:
                errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason=err.reason,
                        field=err.field,
                        value=err.value,
                        valid_options=err.valid_options,
                    )
                )
        return {}

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Cannot execute invalid step '{self.var}'.")

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return dict(self.raw_data)

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        return cls(
            var=var,
            raw_data=dict(data),
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@register_step("source")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceStep(Step):
    var: str
    table: str
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = []
        for err in self.parse_errors:
            local_errors.append(err.with_context(step_index, self.var))

        if not self.table:
            if not any(e.field == "table" for e in local_errors):
                local_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field="table",
                        value=self.table,
                        valid_options=sorted(schema.keys()),
                    )
                )
        elif self.table not in schema:
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="table_not_found",
                    field="table",
                    value=self.table,
                    valid_options=sorted(schema.keys()),
                )
            )

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors:
            return {}
        return dict(schema[self.table])

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        return Table.from_source(sources[self.table])

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "source",
            "table": self.table,
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        raw_table = data.get("table")
        if not isinstance(raw_table, str) or not raw_table.strip():
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="table",
                    value=str(raw_table) if raw_table is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
            table_val = ""
        else:
            table_val = raw_table

        return cls(
            var=var,
            table=table_val,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@register_step("select")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SelectStep(Step):
    var: str
    input: str
    columns: tuple[str, ...]
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("input", self.input),)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]

        if not self.columns and not any(e.field == "columns" for e in local_errors):
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="columns",
                    value="[]",
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        in_schema = input_schemas.get(self.input)
        out_schema: dict[str, str] = {}
        seen_cols: set[str] = set()

        for idx, col in enumerate(self.columns):
            if col in seen_cols:
                local_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=f"columns[{idx}]",
                        value=col,
                        valid_options=None,
                    )
                )
                continue
            seen_cols.add(col)

            if in_schema is not None:
                if col not in in_schema:
                    local_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=self.var,
                            reason="column_not_found",
                            field=f"columns[{idx}]",
                            value=col,
                            valid_options=sorted(in_schema.keys()),
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    out_schema[col] = in_schema[col]

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or in_schema is None:
            return {}
        return out_schema

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        source_table = env[self.input]
        projected = (
            {col: row.get(col) for col in self.columns} for row in source_table.rows
        )
        return Table(self.columns, projected)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "select",
            "input": self.input,
            "columns": list(self.columns),
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        input_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "input", parse_errors)
        raw_cols = data.get("columns")
        cols: list[str] = []
        if (
            not isinstance(raw_cols, list)
            or len(raw_cols) == 0
        ):
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="columns",
                    value=str(raw_cols) if raw_cols is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for idx, item in enumerate(raw_cols):
                if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"columns[{idx}]",
                            value=str(item),
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    cols.append(item)

        return cls(
            var=var,
            input=input_var,
            columns=tuple(cols),
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@register_step("filter")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FilterStep(Step):
    var: str
    input: str
    condition: Condition
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("input", self.input),)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]

        in_schema = input_schemas.get(self.input)
        if in_schema is not None:
            cond_errors = self.condition.validate(
                schema=in_schema,
                step_index=step_index,
                var=self.var,
                field_prefix="condition",
            )
            local_errors.extend(cond_errors)

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or in_schema is None:
            return {}
        return dict(in_schema)

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        source_table = env[self.input]
        filtered = (
            row for row in source_table.rows if self.condition.evaluate(row)
        )
        return Table(source_table.columns, filtered)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "filter",
            "input": self.input,
            "condition": self.condition.to_dict(),
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        input_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "input", parse_errors)
        raw_cond = data.get("condition")
        condition = Condition.from_dict(raw_cond)
        return cls(
            var=var,
            input=input_var,
            condition=condition,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SortKey:
    column: str
    dir: str

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"column": self.column, "dir": self.dir}


@register_step("sort")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SortStep(Step):
    var: str
    input: str
    by: tuple[SortKey, ...]
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("input", self.input),)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]

        if not self.by and not any(e.field == "by" for e in local_errors):
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="by",
                    value="[]",
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        in_schema = input_schemas.get(self.input)
        for idx, item in enumerate(self.by):
            if in_schema is not None:
                if item.column not in in_schema:
                    local_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=self.var,
                            reason="column_not_found",
                            field=f"by[{idx}].column",
                            value=item.column,
                            valid_options=sorted(in_schema.keys()),
                        )
                    )
            if item.dir not in VALID_SORT_DIRS:
                local_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=f"by[{idx}].dir",
                        value=item.dir,
                        valid_options=sorted(VALID_SORT_DIRS),
                    )
                )

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or in_schema is None:
            return {}
        return dict(in_schema)

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        source_table = env[self.input]
        rows = list(source_table.rows)
        for spec in reversed(self.by):
            reverse = spec.dir == "desc"
            non_null = [r for r in rows if r.get(spec.column) is not None]
            nulls = [r for r in rows if r.get(spec.column) is None]
            non_null.sort(
                key=lambda r, col=spec.column: normalize_runtime_value(r[col]),
                reverse=reverse,
            )
            rows = non_null + nulls
        return Table(source_table.columns, rows)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "sort",
            "input": self.input,
            "by": [item.to_dict() for item in self.by],
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        input_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "input", parse_errors)
        raw_by = data.get("by")
        sort_keys: list[SortKey] = []

        if not isinstance(raw_by, list) or len(raw_by) == 0:
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="by",
                    value=str(raw_by) if raw_by is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for idx, entry in enumerate(raw_by):
                if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"by[{idx}]",
                            value=str(entry),
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
                raw_col = entry.get("column")
                raw_dir = entry.get("dir")
                col_str = raw_col if isinstance(raw_col, str) else ""
                dir_str = raw_dir if isinstance(raw_dir, str) else str(raw_dir)
                if not isinstance(raw_col, str) or not raw_col.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"by[{idx}].column",
                            value=str(raw_col) if raw_col is not None else None,
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                sort_keys.append(SortKey(column=col_str, dir=dir_str))

        return cls(
            var=var,
            input=input_var,
            by=tuple(sort_keys),
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@register_step("limit")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LimitStep(Step):
    var: str
    input: str
    n: int
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("input", self.input),)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]

        if (isinstance(self.n, bool) or not isinstance(self.n, int) or self.n < 0) and not any(
            e.field == "n" for e in local_errors
        ):
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="n",
                    value=str(self.n),
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        in_schema = input_schemas.get(self.input)
        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or in_schema is None:
            return {}
        return dict(in_schema)

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        source_table = env[self.input]
        return Table(source_table.columns, source_table.rows[: self.n])

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "limit",
            "input": self.input,
            "n": self.n,
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        input_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "input", parse_errors)
        raw_n = data.get("n")
        if isinstance(raw_n, bool) or not isinstance(raw_n, int) or raw_n < 0:
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="n",
                    value=str(raw_n) if raw_n is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
            n_val = -1
        else:
            n_val = raw_n

        return cls(
            var=var,
            input=input_var,
            n=n_val,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AggregateSpec:
    func: str
    column: str
    alias: str

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "func": self.func,
            "column": self.column,
            "alias": self.alias,
        }


@register_step("aggregate")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AggregateStep(Step):
    var: str
    input: str
    group_by: tuple[str, ...]
    aggregates: tuple[AggregateSpec, ...]
    having: Condition | None = None
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("input", self.input),)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]

        in_schema = input_schemas.get(self.input)
        out_schema: dict[str, str] = {}
        seen_out_cols: set[str] = set()

        for idx, col in enumerate(self.group_by):
            if col in seen_out_cols:
                local_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=f"group_by[{idx}]",
                        value=col,
                        valid_options=None,
                    )
                )
                continue
            seen_out_cols.add(col)

            if in_schema is not None:
                if col not in in_schema:
                    local_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=self.var,
                            reason="column_not_found",
                            field=f"group_by[{idx}]",
                            value=col,
                            valid_options=sorted(in_schema.keys()),
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    out_schema[col] = in_schema[col]

        if not self.aggregates and not any(
            e.field == "aggregates" for e in local_errors
        ):
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="aggregates",
                    value="[]",
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        for idx, agg in enumerate(self.aggregates):
            func_valid = agg.func in VALID_AGG_FUNCS
            if not func_valid:
                local_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=f"aggregates[{idx}].func",
                        value=agg.func,
                        valid_options=sorted(VALID_AGG_FUNCS),
                    )
                )

            if not agg.alias or agg.alias in seen_out_cols:
                local_errors.append(
                    ValidationError(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        var=self.var,
                        reason="invalid_parameter",
                        field=f"aggregates[{idx}].alias",
                        value=agg.alias,
                        valid_options=None,
                    )
                )
            else:
                seen_out_cols.add(agg.alias)

            if in_schema is not None and func_valid:
                if agg.func == "count":
                    if agg.column != "*" and agg.column not in in_schema:
                        local_errors.append(
                            ValidationError(
                                step_index=step_index,
                                var=self.var,
                                reason="column_not_found",
                                field=f"aggregates[{idx}].column",
                                value=agg.column,
                                valid_options=["*", *sorted(in_schema.keys())],
                            )
                        )
                    elif agg.alias:
                        out_schema[agg.alias] = "int"
                else:
                    if agg.column not in in_schema:
                        local_errors.append(
                            ValidationError(
                                step_index=step_index,
                                var=self.var,
                                reason="column_not_found",
                                field=f"aggregates[{idx}].column",
                                value=agg.column,
                                valid_options=sorted(in_schema.keys()),
                            )
                        )
                    else:
                        col_type = in_schema[agg.column]
                        if agg.func in {"sum", "avg"}:
                            if col_type not in NUMERIC_TYPES:
                                local_errors.append(
                                    ValidationError(
                                        step_index=step_index,
                                        var=self.var,
                                        reason="type_mismatch",
                                        field=f"aggregates[{idx}].column",
                                        value=col_type,
                                        valid_options=sorted(NUMERIC_TYPES),
                                    )
                                )
                            elif agg.alias:
                                out_schema[agg.alias] = (
                                    "float" if agg.func == "avg" else col_type
                                )
                        elif agg.func in {"min", "max"}:
                            allowed = NUMERIC_TYPES | {"date"}
                            if col_type not in allowed:
                                local_errors.append(
                                    ValidationError(
                                        step_index=step_index,
                                        var=self.var,
                                        reason="type_mismatch",
                                        field=f"aggregates[{idx}].column",
                                        value=col_type,
                                        valid_options=sorted(allowed),
                                    )
                                )
                            elif agg.alias:
                                out_schema[agg.alias] = col_type

        if self.having is not None and in_schema is not None and out_schema:
            having_errors = self.having.validate(
                schema=out_schema,
                step_index=step_index,
                var=self.var,
                field_prefix="having",
            )
            local_errors.extend(having_errors)

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or in_schema is None:
            return {}
        return out_schema

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        source_table = env[self.input]
        groups: dict[tuple[Any, ...], list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}

        if not self.group_by:
            groups[()] = list(source_table.rows)
        else:
            for row in source_table.rows:
                key = tuple(row.get(col) for col in self.group_by)
                groups.setdefault(key, []).append(row)

        out_columns = [
            *self.group_by,
            *(agg.alias for agg in self.aggregates),
        ]
        result_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for key, group_rows in groups.items():
            out_row: dict[str, Any] = {}
            for col_idx, col_name in enumerate(self.group_by):
                out_row[col_name] = key[col_idx]

            for agg in self.aggregates:
                out_row[agg.alias] = _compute_aggregate(agg, group_rows)

            if self.having is None or self.having.evaluate(out_row):
                result_rows.append(out_row)

        return Table(out_columns, result_rows)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "aggregate",
            "input": self.input,
            "group_by": list(self.group_by),
            "aggregates": [agg.to_dict() for agg in self.aggregates],
        }
        if self.having is not None:
            payload["having"] = self.having.to_dict()
        return payload

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        input_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "input", parse_errors)
        raw_group_by = data.get("group_by")
        group_cols: list[str] = []

        if not isinstance(raw_group_by, list):
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="group_by",
                    value=str(raw_group_by) if raw_group_by is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for idx, col in enumerate(raw_group_by):
                if not isinstance(col, str) or not col.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"group_by[{idx}]",
                            value=str(col),
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    group_cols.append(col)

        raw_aggs = data.get("aggregates")
        aggs: list[AggregateSpec] = []
        if not isinstance(raw_aggs, list) or len(raw_aggs) == 0:
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="aggregates",
                    value=str(raw_aggs) if raw_aggs is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for idx, item in enumerate(raw_aggs):
                if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"aggregates[{idx}]",
                            value=str(item),
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
                raw_func = item.get("func")
                raw_col = item.get("column")
                raw_alias = item.get("alias")

                func_str = raw_func if isinstance(raw_func, str) else str(raw_func)
                col_str = raw_col if isinstance(raw_col, str) else ""
                alias_str = raw_alias if isinstance(raw_alias, str) else ""

                if not isinstance(raw_col, str) or not raw_col.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"aggregates[{idx}].column",
                            value=str(raw_col) if raw_col is not None else None,
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                if not isinstance(raw_alias, str) or not raw_alias.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"aggregates[{idx}].alias",
                            value=str(raw_alias) if raw_alias is not None else None,
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )

                aggs.append(
                    AggregateSpec(
                        func=func_str,
                        column=col_str,
                        alias=alias_str,
                    )
                )

        having_cond: Condition | None = None
        if "having" in data and data["having"] is not None:
            having_cond = Condition.from_dict(data["having"])

        return cls(
            var=var,
            input=input_var,
            group_by=tuple(group_cols),
            aggregates=tuple(aggs),
            having=having_cond,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@register_step("distinct")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DistinctStep(Step):
    var: str
    input: str
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("input", self.input),)

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]
        in_schema = input_schemas.get(self.input)

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or in_schema is None:
            return {}
        return dict(in_schema)

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        source_table = env[self.input]
        cols = source_table.columns
        seen: set[tuple[Any, ...]] = set()
        unique_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for row in source_table.rows:
            signature = tuple(row.get(col) for col in cols)
            if signature not in seen:
                seen.add(signature)
                unique_rows.append(row)

        return Table(cols, unique_rows)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "distinct",
            "input": self.input,
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        input_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "input", parse_errors)
        return cls(
            var=var,
            input=input_var,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JoinKey:
    left_col: str
    right_col: str

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "left_col": self.left_col,
            "right_col": self.right_col,
        }


@register_step("join")
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JoinStep(Step):
    var: str
    left: str
    right: str
    on: tuple[JoinKey, ...]
    how: str
    parse_errors: tuple[ValidationError, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False)

    @property
    def input_refs(self) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
        return (("left", self.left), ("right", self.right))

    def validate(
        self,
        input_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]],
        errors: list[ValidationError] | None = None,
        step_index: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        local_errors: list[ValidationError] = [
