            err.with_context(step_index, self.var) for err in self.parse_errors
        ]

        if self.how not in VALID_JOIN_TYPES:
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="how",
                    value=self.how,
                    valid_options=sorted(VALID_JOIN_TYPES),
                )
            )

        if not self.on and not any(e.field == "on" for e in local_errors):
            local_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=step_index,
                    var=self.var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="on",
                    value="[]",
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )

        left_schema = input_schemas.get(self.left)
        right_schema = input_schemas.get(self.right)

        for idx, pair in enumerate(self.on):
            left_ok = False
            right_ok = False
            if left_schema is not None:
                if pair.left_col not in left_schema:
                    local_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=self.var,
                            reason="column_not_found",
                            field=f"on[{idx}].left_col",
                            value=pair.left_col,
                            valid_options=sorted(left_schema.keys()),
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    left_ok = True

            if right_schema is not None:
                if pair.right_col not in right_schema:
                    local_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=self.var,
                            reason="column_not_found",
                            field=f"on[{idx}].right_col",
                            value=pair.right_col,
                            valid_options=sorted(right_schema.keys()),
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    right_ok = True

            if left_ok and right_ok and left_schema is not None and right_schema is not None:
                l_type = left_schema[pair.left_col]
                r_type = right_schema[pair.right_col]
                if not _are_join_types_compatible(l_type, r_type):
                    local_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            var=self.var,
                            reason="type_mismatch",
                            field=f"on[{idx}]",
                            value=f"{l_type} != {r_type}",
                            valid_options=[l_type],
                        )
                    )

        if errors is not None:
            errors.extend(local_errors)

        if local_errors or left_schema is None or right_schema is None:
            return {}

        return _build_join_schema(left_schema, right_schema)

    def execute(
        self,
        env: dict[str, Table],
        sources: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> Table:
        left_table = env[self.left]
        right_table = env[self.right]

        overlap = set(left_table.columns) & set(right_table.columns)
        left_map = {
            col: (f"{col}_left" if col in overlap else col)
            for col in left_table.columns
        }
        right_map = {
            col: (f"{col}_right" if col in overlap else col)
            for col in right_table.columns
        }
        out_columns = [*left_map.values(), *right_map.values()]

        right_index: dict[tuple[Any, ...], list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]]] = {}
        for r_idx, r_row in enumerate(right_table.rows):
            key = tuple(
                normalize_runtime_value(r_row.get(k.right_col)) for k in self.on
            )
            if all(v is not None for v in key):
                right_index.setdefault(key, []).append((r_idx, r_row))

        matched_right_indices: set[int] = set()
        out_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for l_row in left_table.rows:
            l_key = tuple(
                normalize_runtime_value(l_row.get(k.left_col)) for k in self.on
            )
            matches = (
                right_index.get(l_key, [])
                if all(v is not None for v in l_key)
                else []
            )

            if matches:
                for r_idx, r_row in matches:
                    matched_right_indices.add(r_idx)
                    merged: dict[str, Any] = {}
                    for col, out_col in left_map.items():
                        merged[out_col] = l_row.get(col)
                    for col, out_col in right_map.items():
                        merged[out_col] = r_row.get(col)
                    out_rows.append(merged)
            elif self.how == "left":
                merged = {}
                for col, out_col in left_map.items():
                    merged[out_col] = l_row.get(col)
                for out_col in right_map.values():
                    merged[out_col] = None
                out_rows.append(merged)

        if self.how == "right":
            for r_idx, r_row in enumerate(right_table.rows):
                if r_idx not in matched_right_indices:
                    merged = {}
                    for out_col in left_map.values():
                        merged[out_col] = None
                    for col, out_col in right_map.items():
                        merged[out_col] = r_row.get(col)
                    out_rows.append(merged)

        return Table(out_columns, out_rows)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "var": self.var,
            "op": "join",
            "left": self.left,
            "right": self.right,
            "on": [pair.to_dict() for pair in self.on],
            "how": self.how,
        }

    @classmethod
    def _parse(
        cls,
        var: str,
        data: Mapping[str, Any],
        parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
    ) -> Step:
        left_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "left", parse_errors)
        right_var = _parse_input_field(var, data, "right", parse_errors)

        raw_how = data.get("how")
        how_str = raw_how if isinstance(raw_how, str) else str(raw_how)

        raw_on = data.get("on")
        join_keys: list[JoinKey] = []
        if not isinstance(raw_on, list) or len(raw_on) == 0:
            parse_errors.append(
                ValidationError(
                    step_index=0,
                    var=var,
                    reason="invalid_parameter",
                    field="on",
                    value=str(raw_on) if raw_on is not None else None,
                    valid_options=None,
                )
            )
        else:
            for idx, entry in enumerate(raw_on):
                if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"on[{idx}]",
                            value=str(entry),
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
                raw_l = entry.get("left_col")
                raw_r = entry.get("right_col")
                l_str = raw_l if isinstance(raw_l, str) else ""
                r_str = raw_r if isinstance(raw_r, str) else ""
                if not isinstance(raw_l, str) or not raw_l.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"on[{idx}].left_col",
                            value=str(raw_l) if raw_l is not None else None,
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                if not isinstance(raw_r, str) or not raw_r.strip():
                    parse_errors.append(
                        ValidationError(
                            step_index=0,
                            var=var,
                            reason="invalid_parameter",
                            field=f"on[{idx}].right_col",
                            value=str(raw_r) if raw_r is not None else None,
                            valid_options=None,
                        )
                    )
                join_keys.append(JoinKey(left_col=l_str, right_col=r_str))

        return cls(
            var=var,
            left=left_var,
            right=right_var,
            on=tuple(join_keys),
            how=how_str,
            parse_errors=tuple(parse_errors),
        )


def _parse_input_field(
    var: str,
    data: Mapping[str, Any],
    field_name: str,
    parse_errors: list[ValidationError],
) -> str:
    raw_val = data.get(field_name)
    if not isinstance(raw_val, str) or not raw_val.strip():
        parse_errors.append(
            ValidationError(
                step_index=0,
                var=var,
                reason="invalid_parameter",
                field=field_name,
                value=str(raw_val) if raw_val is not None else None,
                valid_options=None,
            )
        )
        return ""
    return raw_val


def _are_join_types_compatible(left_type: str, right_type: str) -> bool:
    if left_type == right_type:
        return True
    return left_type in NUMERIC_TYPES and right_type in NUMERIC_TYPES


def _build_join_schema(
    left_schema: Mapping[str, str],
    right_schema: Mapping[str, str],
) -> dict[str, str]:
    overlap = set(left_schema.keys()) & set(right_schema.keys())
    out_schema: dict[str, str] = {}
    for col, col_type in left_schema.items():
        out_name = f"{col}_left" if col in overlap else col
        out_schema[out_name] = col_type
    for col, col_type in right_schema.items():
        out_name = f"{col}_right" if col in overlap else col
        out_schema[out_name] = col_type
    return out_schema


def _compute_aggregate(
    agg: AggregateSpec,
    rows: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> Any:
    if agg.func == "count":
        if agg.column == "*":
            return len(rows)
        return sum(1 for r in rows if r.get(agg.column) is not None)

    values = [
        normalize_runtime_value(r[agg.column])
        for r in rows
        if r.get(agg.column) is not None
    ]
    if not values:
        return None

    if agg.func == "sum":
        return sum(values)
    if agg.func == "avg":
        return float(sum(values)) / len(values)
    if agg.func == "min":
        return min(values)
    if agg.func == "max":
        return max(values)
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported aggregate function: {agg.func}")

'backend\app\query_usage.py':
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any


def main() -> None:
    # БД
    schema: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {
        "users": {"id": "int", "name": "str", "country": "str"},
        "orders": {"id": "int", "user_id": "int", "status": "str", "amount": "float"},
    }

    sources: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {
        "users": [
            {"id": 1, "name": "Alice", "country": "SE"},
            {"id": 2, "name": "Bob", "country": "DE"},
            {"id": 3, "name": "Clara", "country": "SE"},
        ],
        "orders": [
            {"id": 101, "user_id": 1, "status": "paid", "amount": 120.0},
            {"id": 102, "user_id": 1, "status": "paid", "amount": 80.0},
            {"id": 103, "user_id": 2, "status": "cancelled", "amount": 300.0},
            {"id": 104, "user_id": 3, "status": "paid", "amount": 50.0},
        ],
    }

    #КАТАЛОГ
    from app.query_system import (
        get_capabilities,
        get_condition,
        get_full,
        get_step,
        get_conditions,
        get_steps,
        get_engine
    )
    print("1. Работа с каталогом")
    agg_spec = get_step("aggregate")
    between_spec = get_condition("between")
    caps = get_capabilities()

    if agg_spec and between_spec:
        print(f"Шаг '{agg_spec.name}': {agg_spec.description}")
        print(f"Условие '{between_spec.name}': {between_spec.description}")
    print(f"Поддерживаемых фич: {len(caps.allowed)}, ограничений: {len(caps.unsupported)}")

    prompt_doc = get_full(json_syntax=False)
    print("Фрагмент документа для LLM (первые 12 строк):")
    print("\n".join(prompt_doc.splitlines()[:12]))

    # Движок запросов
    from app.query_system import QueryEngine

    engine = QueryEngine()

    print("2. Рабочий запрос и его валидация.")
    valid_payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "U", "op": "source", "table": "users"},
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {
                "var": "F_ORD",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "O",
                "condition": {
                    "and": [
                        {"column": "status", "cmp": "eq", "value": "paid"},
                        {"column": "amount", "cmp": "ge", "value": 50.0},
                    ]
                },
            },
            {
                "var": "J",
                "op": "join",
                "left": "U",
                "right": "F_ORD",
                "on": [{"left_col": "id", "right_col": "user_id"}],
                "how": "inner",
            },
            {
                "var": "AGG",
                "op": "aggregate",
                "input": "J",
                "group_by": ["country"],
                "aggregates": [
                    {"func": "sum", "column": "amount", "alias": "revenue"},
                    {"func": "count", "column": "*", "alias": "orders_cnt"},
                ],
                "having": {"column": "revenue", "cmp": "gt", "value": 100.0},
            },
            {
                "var": "RES",
                "op": "sort",
                "input": "AGG",
                "by": [{"column": "revenue", "dir": "desc"}],
            },
        ],
        "output": "RES",
    }

    query = engine.from_dict(valid_payload)
    validation = engine.validate(query, schema)
    print(f"Валидация успешна: {validation.ok}")
    print(f"Выходная схема: {validation.output_schema}")

    if validation.ok:
        table = engine.execute(query, sources)
        print(f"Результат выполнения: {table.to_dicts()}")

    print("3. Запрос с ошибкой. Валидация.")
    invalid_payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {
                "var": "F_BAD",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "O",
                "condition": {
                    "and": [
                        {"column": "unknown_col", "cmp": "eq", "value": 1},
                        {"column": "amount", "cmp": "gt", "value": "not_a_float"},
                    ]
                },
            },
            {"var": "S_BAD", "op": "select", "input": "MISSING_VAR", "columns": ["id"]},
        ],
        "output": "UNKNOWN_OUT",
    }

    bad_query = engine.from_dict(invalid_payload)
    bad_validation = engine.validate(bad_query, schema)
    print(f"Валидация успешна: {bad_validation.ok} (найдено ошибок: {len(bad_validation.errors)})")
    for err in bad_validation.errors:
        print(
            f"  [step={err.step_index}, var='{err.var}'] "
            f"reason={err.reason}, field={err.field}, value={err.value}, "
            f"valid_options={err.valid_options}"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'backend\scripts\eval_router.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import sys
import time
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from app.agent.config import LLMConfig  # noqa: E402
from app.agent.intents import Intent  # noqa: E402
from app.agent.llm import OpenAICompatibleClient, StructuredOutputError  # noqa: E402
from app.agent.memory import SessionState  # noqa: E402
from app.agent.router import IntentRouter  # noqa: E402
from tests.agent.fakes import make_catalog  # noqa: E402

CASES: list[tuple[str, Intent]] = [
    ("Что это вообще за данные?", Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW),
    ("Сколько здесь записей и за какие даты?", Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW),
    ("Опиши структуру таблицы продаж", Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW),
    ("Что значит колонка «Канал продажи»?", Intent.DESCRIBE_COLUMN),
    ("Есть ли пропуски в поле Город?", Intent.DESCRIBE_COLUMN),
    ("Какие города встречаются чаще всего?", Intent.DISTRIBUTION),
    ("Как распределены суммы заказов?", Intent.DISTRIBUTION),
    ("Какая общая сумма продаж в Перми?", Intent.DATA_QUESTION),
    ("Топ-5 городов по выручке", Intent.DATA_QUESTION),
    ("Сколько заказов пришло через интернет-магазин в январе?", Intent.DATA_QUESTION),
    ("Как связаны заказы и покупатели?", Intent.RELATIONSHIPS),
    ("Есть ли дубли заказов?", Intent.ANOMALIES),
    ("Найди странные значения в суммах", Intent.ANOMALIES),
    ("Как менялись продажи по месяцам?", Intent.TIME_PATTERNS),
    ("Есть ли сезонные пики зимой?", Intent.TIME_PATTERNS),
    ("Какой паттерн повторяется каждые 12 месяцев?", Intent.TIME_PATTERNS),
    ("Чем отличаются продажи в рознице и в приложении?", Intent.SEGMENT_COMPARE),
    ("Как группируются покупатели по типу и каналу?", Intent.SEGMENT_COMPARE),
    ("Изменения скидки предшествуют росту продаж?", Intent.CORRELATION),
    ("Сколько продаж ожидать в следующем квартале?", Intent.FORECAST),
    ("Что будет с выручкой, если поднять цены на 10%?", Intent.FORECAST),
    ("Собери панель с ключевыми метриками продаж по кварталам", Intent.CREATE_DASHBOARD),
    ("Какая погода будет завтра?", Intent.OUT_OF_SCOPE),
    ("Напиши стихотворение про осень", Intent.OUT_OF_SCOPE),
    ("ну это самое", Intent.UNCLEAR),
]


async def main() -> None:
    config = LLMConfig.from_env()
    client = OpenAICompatibleClient(config)
    router = IntentRouter(client)
    catalog = make_catalog()
    print(f"model={config.model} mode={config.structured_mode} url={config.base_url}\n")
    ok, latencies, confusion = 0, [], Counter()
    for question, expected in CASES:
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            decision = await router.classify(question, SessionState(session_id="eval"), catalog)
            got = decision.intent
        except StructuredOutputError as e:
            got = None
            print(f"  !! JSON не получен: {e.errors}")
        latencies.append(time.monotonic() - started)
        mark = "OK " if got is expected else "ERR"
        ok += got is expected
        if got is not expected:
            confusion[(expected, got)] += 1
        print(f"{mark} {latencies[-1]:5.1f}s  {expected.value:>17} -> {getattr(got, 'value', None)!s:<17} {question}")
    await client.aclose()
    latencies.sort()
    print(f"\nТочность: {ok}/{len(CASES)} = {ok / len(CASES):.0%}")
    print(f"Задержка: медиана {latencies[len(latencies) // 2]:.1f}s, максимум {latencies[-1]:.1f}s")
    print(f"Итоговый режим structured output: {client.structured_mode}")
    for (exp, got), n in confusion.most_common():
        print(f"  {exp.value} -> {getattr(got, 'value', None)}: {n}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

'backend\tests\agent\fakes.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.agent.contracts import (
    BaseTool,
    Catalog,
    ColumnInfo,
    QueryResult,
    ResultTable,
    SemanticType,
    TableColumn,
    TableField,
    TableInfo,
    ToolContext,
    ToolResult,
    ToolStatus,
    ValueCount,
)
from app.agent.contracts.tools import ChartHint
from app.agent.intents import Intent
from app.agent.llm.client import ChatMessage, LLMReply
from app.agent.prompts import SQL_GUIDE

Reply = str | dict[str, Any] | Callable[[Sequence[ChatMessage]], str]


class ScriptedLLM:
    def __init__(self, script: dict[str, list[Reply]] | None = None):
        self.script = {k: list(v) for k, v in (script or {}).items()}
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, list[ChatMessage]]] = []

    def add(self, schema_name: str, *replies: Reply) -> None:
        self.script.setdefault(schema_name, []).extend(replies)

    async def chat(self, messages, *, json_schema=None, schema_name="response", temperature=None, max_tokens=None):
        self.calls.append((schema_name, list(messages)))
        queue = self.script.get(schema_name)
        if not queue:
            raise AssertionError(f"неожиданный вызов LLM: {schema_name}")
        item = queue.pop(0)
        if callable(item):
            item = item(messages)
        content = json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) if isinstance(item, dict) else item
        return LLMReply(content=content, model="fake", latency_ms=1)

    def names(self) -> list[str]:
        return [name for name, _ in self.calls]


def make_catalog() -> Catalog:
    return Catalog(
        version="1",
        tables=[
            TableInfo(
                name="sales",
                display_name="Продажи",
                row_count=1000,
                time_column="Дата операции",
                period_start="2024-10-01",
                period_end="2025-09-30",
                columns=[
                    ColumnInfo(name="Код заказа", db_type="text", semantic_type=SemanticType.ID),
                    ColumnInfo(name="Дата операции", db_type="timestamp", semantic_type=SemanticType.DATETIME),
                    ColumnInfo(
                        name="Город",
                        db_type="text",
                        semantic_type=SemanticType.CATEGORY,
                        top_values=[ValueCount(value="Пермь", count=120), ValueCount(value="Казань", count=80)],
                    ),
                    ColumnInfo(name="Канал продажи", db_type="text", semantic_type=SemanticType.CATEGORY),
                    ColumnInfo(name="Сумма", db_type="numeric", semantic_type=SemanticType.NUMERIC, unit="руб."),
                ],
            )
        ],
    )


class FakeCatalogProvider:
    def __init__(self, catalog: Catalog | None = None):
        self.catalog = catalog or make_catalog()

    async def get_catalog(self) -> Catalog:
        return self.catalog


class FakeExecutor:
    async def execute(self, sql, *, params=None, max_rows=10_000, timeout_s=30.0) -> QueryResult:
        return QueryResult(columns=[], rows=[], row_count=0)


class EmptyArgs(BaseModel):
    pass


class OverviewTool(BaseTool):
    name = "dataset_overview"
    description = "Обзор всех таблиц: объём, период, состав колонок."
    intents = frozenset({Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW})
    args_model = EmptyArgs

    async def run(self, args, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        t = ctx.catalog.tables[0]
        return ToolResult(
            facts={"tables": len(ctx.catalog.tables), "rows": t.row_count, "period": f"{t.period_start} — {t.period_end}"},
            summary_text=f"Загружена 1 таблица «{t.label}» на {t.row_count} строк.",
        )


class SqlArgs(BaseModel):
    tables: list[str] = TableField("Таблицы, которые использует запрос")
    sql: str


CITY_TABLE = ResultTable(
    columns=[
        TableColumn(name="Город", semantic_type=SemanticType.CATEGORY),
        TableColumn(name="Заказы", semantic_type=SemanticType.NUMERIC),
    ],
    rows=[["Пермь", 120], ["Казань", 80], ["Москва", 40]],
)


class SqlTool(BaseTool):
    name = "sql_query"
    description = "Произвольный запрос SELECT к данным."
    intents = frozenset({Intent.DATA_QUESTION, Intent.DISTRIBUTION, Intent.TIME_PATTERNS})
    args_model = SqlArgs
    args_hint = SQL_GUIDE
    llm_role = "sql"

    def __init__(self, table: ResultTable = CITY_TABLE):
        self.table = table
        self.seen: list[SqlArgs] = []

    async def run(self, args, ctx: ToolContext) -> ToolResult:
        self.seen.append(args)
        if '"Город2"' in args.sql:
            return ToolResult(status=ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR, error="колонки «Город2» нет, похожие: «Город»")
        top = self.table.rows[0]
        return ToolResult(
            facts={"top": top[0], "top_value": top[1], "groups": len(self.table.rows)},
            table=self.table,
            chart_hint=ChartHint(kind="comparison"),
            sql=args.sql,
            summary_text=f"Больше всего у «{top[0]}»: {top[1]}.",
        )


def intent(intent: str, question: str = "вопрос", confidence: float = 0.9, **extra: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"standalone_question": question, "intent": intent, "confidence": confidence, **extra}


def narrative(summary: str, points: list[str] | None = None, follow_ups: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"summary": summary, "points": points or [], "follow_ups": follow_ups or []}


'backend\tests\agent\test_agent.py':
import asyncio

from app.agent import Agent, AgentConfig
from app.agent.contracts import (
    Catalog,
    ChartBlock,
    ChartType,
    ClarificationBlock,
    DashboardBlock,
    ErrorBlock,
    InMemoryDashboardStore,
    TableBlock,
    TextBlock,
    ToolRegistry,
)
from app.agent.intents import Intent
from app.agent.llm import LLMUnavailableError
from tests.agent.fakes import (
    FakeCatalogProvider,
    FakeExecutor,
    OverviewTool,
    ScriptedLLM,
    SqlTool,
    intent,
    narrative,
)

SQL_OK = {"tables": ["sales"], "sql": 'SELECT "Город", count(*) AS "Заказы" FROM "sales" GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 50'}


def make_agent(llm, *, sql_tool=None, catalog=None, dashboards=None, **cfg):
    tools = ToolRegistry([OverviewTool(), sql_tool or SqlTool()])
    return Agent(
        llm=llm,
        catalog_provider=FakeCatalogProvider(catalog),
        executor=FakeExecutor(),
        tools=tools,
        dashboards=dashboards,
        config=AgentConfig(**cfg),
    )


def blocks_of(response, kind):
    return [b for b in response.blocks if isinstance(b, kind)]


def test_overview_needs_no_args_call():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("dataset_overview", "Что есть в этих данных?")],
        "narrative": [narrative("Это продажи: 1000 строк с 2024-10-01 по 2025-09-30.", ["Одна таблица"])],
    })
    agent = make_agent(llm)
    r = asyncio.run(agent.handle("s1", "Что есть в этих данных?"))
    assert r.status == "ok"
    assert llm.names() == ["intent_decision", "narrative"]
    assert blocks_of(r, TextBlock)[0].markdown.startswith("Это продажи")
    assert r.trace.intent is Intent.DATASET_OVERVIEW and r.trace.routed_by == "llm"
    assert [c.purpose for c in r.trace.llm_calls] == ["intent_decision", "narrative"]


def test_sql_repair_loop_chart_and_memory_then_change_view():
    tool = SqlTool()
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("data_question", "Сколько заказов в каждом городе?")],
        "args_sql_query": [
            {"tables": ["sales"], "sql": 'SELECT "Город2", count(*) FROM "sales" GROUP BY 1'},
            SQL_OK,
        ],
        "narrative": [narrative("Больше всего заказов в Перми: 120.", ["Пермь: 120", "Казань: 80"], ["А по каналам?"])],
    })
    agent = make_agent(llm, sql_tool=tool)
    r = asyncio.run(agent.handle("s1", "Сколько заказов в каждом городе?"))
    assert r.status == "ok", r
    assert [c.status for c in r.trace.tool_calls] == ["retryable_error", "ok"]
    repair_prompt = llm.calls[2][1][-1].content
    assert "Город2" in repair_prompt and "не сработали" in repair_prompt
    chart = blocks_of(r, ChartBlock)[0]
    assert chart.spec.chart_type is ChartType.BAR
    assert blocks_of(r, TableBlock)
    assert r.follow_ups == ["А по каналам?"]

    calls_before = len(llm.calls)
    r2 = asyncio.run(agent.handle("s1", "А теперь круговой диаграммой"))
    assert r2.trace.routed_by == "rules" and r2.trace.intent is Intent.CHANGE_VIEW
    assert blocks_of(r2, ChartBlock)[0].spec.chart_type is ChartType.PIE
    assert len(llm.calls) == calls_before and len(tool.seen) == 2


def test_follow_up_reclassifies_point_as_new_question():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [
            intent("data_question", "Сколько заказов в каждом городе?"),
            intent("data_question", "Почему в Перми больше всего заказов?"),
        ],
        "args_sql_query": [SQL_OK, SQL_OK],
        "narrative": [
            narrative("Лидирует Пермь.", ["Пермь: 120", "Казань: 80"]),
            narrative("В Перми 120 заказов."),
        ],
    })
    agent = make_agent(llm)
    asyncio.run(agent.handle("s1", "Сколько заказов в каждом городе?"))
    r = asyncio.run(agent.handle("s1", "Расскажи подробнее про первый пункт"))
    assert r.trace.intent is Intent.FOLLOW_UP_DETAIL
    router_prompt = [m for name, m in llm.calls if name == "intent_decision"][1][-1].content
    assert "Пермь: 120" in router_prompt
    assert r.trace.standalone_question == "Почему в Перми больше всего заказов?"


def test_low_confidence_asks_to_clarify():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({"intent_decision": [intent("data_question", "Покажи всё про всё", confidence=0.2)]})
    r = asyncio.run(make_agent(llm).handle("s1", "покажи всё"))
    assert r.status == "clarification"
    block = blocks_of(r, ClarificationBlock)[0]
    assert block.options[0] == "Покажи всё про всё"


def test_refusal_without_llm_call():
    llm = ScriptedLLM()
    r = asyncio.run(make_agent(llm).handle("s1", "Удали таблицу продаж"))
    assert r.status == "refused" and llm.calls == []


def test_llm_down_gives_retryable_error():
    class DownLLM:
        async def chat(self, *a, **k):
            raise LLMUnavailableError("connection refused")

    r = asyncio.run(make_agent(DownLLM()).handle("s1", "Что есть в данных?"))
    assert r.status == "error" and blocks_of(r, ErrorBlock)[0].retryable


def test_garbage_from_model_turns_into_rephrase_request():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({"intent_decision": ["ой", "ой", "ой"]})
    r = asyncio.run(make_agent(llm).handle("s1", "Что есть в данных?"))
    assert r.status == "clarification"
    assert blocks_of(r, ClarificationBlock)[0].options[0] == "Что есть в этих данных?"


def test_invented_numbers_fall_back_to_tool_text():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("data_question", "Заказы по городам")],
        "args_sql_query": [SQL_OK],
        "narrative": [narrative("В Перми 777 заказов."), narrative("В Перми 778 заказов.")],
    })
    r = asyncio.run(make_agent(llm).handle("s1", "Заказы по городам"))
    assert blocks_of(r, TextBlock)[0].markdown == "Больше всего у «Пермь»: 120."
    assert any("шаблонный" in w for w in r.trace.warnings)


def test_empty_catalog_says_load_data():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({"intent_decision": [intent("dataset_overview")]})
    r = asyncio.run(make_agent(llm, catalog=Catalog()).handle("s1", "Что тут?"))
    assert "не загружены" in blocks_of(r, TextBlock)[0].markdown


def test_dashboard_is_built_saved_and_refreshable():
    store = InMemoryDashboardStore()
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("create_dashboard", "Дашборд по заказам")],
        "dashboard_plan": [{
            "title": "Заказы",
            "widgets": [
                {"title": "По городам", "question": "Заказы по городам", "intent": "data_question"},
                {"title": "Доля городов", "question": "Доля заказов по городам", "intent": "distribution", "chart_type": "pie"},
            ],
        }],
        "args_sql_query": [SQL_OK, SQL_OK],
    })
    agent = make_agent(llm, dashboards=store)
    r = asyncio.run(agent.handle("s1", "Создай дашборд по заказам"))
    assert r.status == "ok"
    dash_block = blocks_of(r, DashboardBlock)[0]
    assert dash_block.widget_count == 2
    charts = [b.spec.chart_type for b in blocks_of(r, ChartBlock)]
    assert charts == [ChartType.BAR, ChartType.PIE]
    saved = asyncio.run(store.get(dash_block.dashboard_id))
    assert saved.widgets[0].args == SQL_OK
    calls_before = len(llm.calls)
    refreshed = asyncio.run(agent.refresh_dashboard(saved.id))
    assert refreshed.refreshed_at is not None and len(llm.calls) == calls_before


def test_status_events_are_streamed():
    events = []

    async def sink(e):
        events.append(e.type if e.type == "done" else e.stage)

    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("dataset_overview")],
        "narrative": [narrative("Одна таблица на 1000 строк.")],
    })
    asyncio.run(make_agent(llm).handle("s1", "Что тут?", emit=sink))
    assert events[0] == "routing" and events[-1] == "done" and "writing" in events


'backend\tests\agent\test_api.py':
import json

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.agent import AgentConfig
from app.agent.factory import build_agent
from app.api.server import create_app
from tests.agent.fakes import ScriptedLLM, intent, narrative
from tests.agent.test_integrations import SALES

TOP_CITY_ARGS = {"column": "Продажи.Город", "limit": 10}


def make_client(llm):
    bundle = build_agent(config=AgentConfig(), llm=llm, data_dir="/nonexistent")
    return TestClient(create_app(bundle)), bundle


def test_upload_catalog_chat_history_and_png():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("distribution", "Какие города встречаются чаще всего?", wants_chart=True)],
        "args_top_values": [TOP_CITY_ARGS],
        "narrative": [narrative("Чаще всего встречается Пермь: 3 раза, это 60%.")],
    })
    client, _ = make_client(llm)
    assert client.get("/api/health").json()["tables"] == 0

    r = client.post("/api/datasets", files={"file": ("Продажи.csv", SALES, "text/csv")})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["row_count"] == 5
    assert client.get("/api/catalog").json()["tables"][0]["name"] == "Продажи"

    r = client.post("/api/chat", json={"message": "Какие города встречаются чаще всего?"})
    body = r.json()
    assert r.status_code == 200 and body["status"] == "ok", body
    chart = next(b for b in body["blocks"] if b["type"] == "chart")
    assert chart["engine_spec"]["type"] == "bar"

    history = client.get(f"/api/sessions/{body['session_id']}").json()
    assert history["turns"][0]["summary"].startswith("Чаще всего")

    png = client.post("/api/charts/png", json={"engine_spec": chart["engine_spec"], "data": chart["data"]})
    assert png.status_code == 200 and png.headers["content-type"] == "image/png" and png.content[:4] == b"\x89PNG"


def test_stream_sends_status_then_done():
    llm = ScriptedLLM()
    client, _ = make_client(llm)
    with client.stream("POST", "/api/chat/stream", json={"message": "Привет!", "session_id": "s1"}) as r:
        text = "".join(r.iter_text())
    events = [line.removeprefix("event: ") for line in text.splitlines() if line.startswith("event: ")]
    assert events[0] == "status" and events[-1] == "done"
    done = json.loads(text.strip().split("data: ")[-1])
    assert done["response"]["status"] == "ok" and done["response"]["session_id"] == "s1"


def test_dashboard_endpoints():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("create_dashboard", "Дашборд по городам")],
        "dashboard_plan": [{"title": "Города", "widgets": [
            {"title": "Частые города", "question": "Какие города чаще всего?", "intent": "distribution"},
        ]}],
        "args_top_values": [TOP_CITY_ARGS],
    })
    client, bundle = make_client(llm)
    bundle.store.add_csv(SALES, "Продажи.csv")
    body = client.post("/api/chat", json={"message": "Создай дашборд по городам", "session_id": "s1"}).json()
    dash_id = next(b for b in body["blocks"] if b["type"] == "dashboard")["dashboard_id"]
    listed = client.get("/api/dashboards", params={"session_id": "s1"}).json()
    assert listed[0]["id"] == dash_id and listed[0]["widget_count"] == 1
    assert client.get(f"/api/dashboards/{dash_id}").json()["widgets"][0]["tool"] == "top_values"
    assert client.post(f"/api/dashboards/{dash_id}/refresh").json()["refreshed_at"] is not None
    assert client.get("/api/dashboards/nope").status_code == 404


'backend\tests\agent\test_components.py':
from app.agent.catalog_refs import build_args_schema, catalog_fields, check_catalog_refs, fix_catalog_refs
from app.agent.contracts import ChartType, ResultTable, SemanticType, TableColumn
from app.agent.intents import Intent
from app.agent.memory import Artifact, SessionState, Turn
from app.agent.narrator import ungrounded_numbers
from app.agent.router import detect_chart_type, route_by_rules
from app.agent.contracts.tools import ToolResult
from app.agent.viz import alternative_chart, choose_chart
from tests.agent.fakes import CITY_TABLE, SqlArgs, make_catalog


def _table(*cols: tuple[str, SemanticType], rows: list[list]) -> ResultTable:
    return ResultTable(columns=[TableColumn(name=n, semantic_type=t) for n, t in cols], rows=rows)


def test_time_series_becomes_line():
    t = _table(("Месяц", SemanticType.DATE), ("Выручка", SemanticType.NUMERIC), rows=[["2024-10", 1], ["2024-11", 2]])
    spec, note = choose_chart(t)
    assert spec.chart_type is ChartType.LINE and spec.x == "Месяц" and note is None


def test_categories_become_bar_and_pie_is_allowed_for_few():
    spec, _ = choose_chart(CITY_TABLE)
    assert spec.chart_type is ChartType.BAR
    pie, note = choose_chart(CITY_TABLE, requested=ChartType.PIE)
    assert pie.chart_type is ChartType.PIE and note is None


def test_pie_with_many_categories_falls_back_with_note():
    t = _table(("Город", SemanticType.CATEGORY), ("N", SemanticType.NUMERIC), rows=[[f"г{i}", i] for i in range(20)])
    spec, note = choose_chart(t, requested=ChartType.PIE)
    assert spec.chart_type is ChartType.BAR
    assert "Круговая диаграмма не подходит" in note


def test_single_value_is_kpi_and_alternative_cycles():
    t = _table(("Всего", SemanticType.NUMERIC), rows=[[42]])
    assert choose_chart(t)[0].chart_type is ChartType.KPI
    assert alternative_chart(CITY_TABLE, ChartType.BAR).chart_type is ChartType.PIE


def test_grounding_accepts_rounding_percent_and_millions():
    source = {"facts": {"share": 0.2834, "revenue": 1_234_567, "rows": 512340}}
    assert ungrounded_numbers("Доля 28,3%, выручка 1,2 млн, строк 512 340, топ-5 городов", source) == []


def test_grounding_flags_invented_numbers():
    source = {"facts": {"revenue": 1_234_567}}
    assert ungrounded_numbers("Выручка выросла на 37% и составила 999", source) == ["37", "999"]


def _state_with_artifact(points: list[str] | None = None) -> SessionState:
    state = SessionState(session_id="s")
    state.add_artifact(
        Artifact(id="a1", question="Заказы по городам", intent=Intent.DATA_QUESTION, tool="sql_query", args={},
                 result=ToolResult(table=CITY_TABLE))
    )
    state.add_turn(Turn(id="t1", user_message="Заказы по городам", points=points or []))
    return state


def test_rules_change_view_and_follow_up():
    state = _state_with_artifact(points=["Пермь лидирует", "Москва отстаёт"])
    d = route_by_rules("А теперь покажи то же самое в другом виде", state)
    assert d.intent is Intent.CHANGE_VIEW and d.chart_type is None and d.standalone_question == "Заказы по городам"
    d = route_by_rules("А круговой диаграммой?", state)
    assert d.intent is Intent.CHANGE_VIEW and d.chart_type is ChartType.PIE
    d = route_by_rules("Расскажи подробнее про второй пункт", state)
    assert d.intent is Intent.FOLLOW_UP_DETAIL and d.point_number == 2


def test_rules_leave_real_questions_to_llm():
    state = _state_with_artifact()
    assert route_by_rules("А теперь то же самое для Москвы", state) is None
    assert route_by_rules("Покажи продажи по Московской области за год", state) is None
    assert route_by_rules("Какие столбцы есть в таблице?", state) is None
    assert route_by_rules("Что есть в этих данных?", SessionState(session_id="s")) is None


def test_rules_refuse_writes_and_answer_help():
    state = SessionState(session_id="s")
    assert route_by_rules("Удали таблицу продаж", state).intent is Intent.OUT_OF_SCOPE
    assert route_by_rules("Привет! Что ты умеешь?", state).intent is Intent.HELP
    assert detect_chart_type("Покажи в виде таблицы") is ChartType.TABLE


def test_args_schema_gets_catalog_enums_and_fuzzy_fix():
    catalog = make_catalog()
    fields = catalog_fields(SqlArgs)
    assert fields == {"tables": "table"}
    schema = build_args_schema(SqlArgs, ["sales"], catalog.column_refs())
    assert schema["properties"]["tables"]["items"]["enum"] == ["sales"]
    assert "x-catalog" not in str(schema)
    fixed = fix_catalog_refs({"tables": ["Sales", "salse"], "sql": "select 1"}, fields, ["sales"], [])
    assert fixed["tables"] == ["sales", "sales"]
    bad = SqlArgs(tables=["orders"], sql="select 1")
    assert "«orders» нет в данных" in check_catalog_refs(bad, fields, catalog)[0]


def test_allowed_chart_types_restrict_choice_with_note():
    allowed = {ChartType.BAR, ChartType.LINE, ChartType.SCATTER, ChartType.KPI}
    spec, note = choose_chart(CITY_TABLE, requested=ChartType.PIE, allowed=allowed)
    assert spec.chart_type is ChartType.BAR and "пока не поддерживается" in note
    assert alternative_chart(CITY_TABLE, ChartType.BAR, allowed=allowed).chart_type is ChartType.LINE

import asyncio
from datetime import date

import pytest

from app.agent import AgentConfig
from app.agent.contracts import ChartBlock, ChartType, TableBlock, TextBlock, ToolStatus
from app.agent.contracts.catalog import SemanticType
from app.agent.contracts.tools import ToolContext
from app.agent.factory import build_agent
from app.agent.integrations.basic_tools import TopValuesTool
from app.agent.integrations.charts import export_chart, render_png
from app.agent.integrations.csv_store import InMemoryCsvStore, parse_csv
from app.agent.integrations.query_dsl import QueryArgs, QueryDslTool
from tests.agent.fakes import ScriptedLLM, intent, narrative

SALES = """Код позиции;Код заказа;Дата операции (МСК);Код покупателя;Город;Канал продажи;Количество, шт.;Цена, руб.;Код магазина
ПР-1;ЗК-1;2024-10-01 02:27:58;КЛ-1;Пермь;Интернет-магазин;9;298,70;007
ПР-2;ЗК-1;2024-10-01 02:27:58;КЛ-1;Пермь;Интернет-магазин;1;100,00;007
ПР-3;ЗК-2;2024-11-15 10:00:00;КЛ-2;Казань;Розничный магазин;2;50,50;012
ПР-4;ЗК-3;2024-12-20 12:30:00;КЛ-3;Пермь;Мобильное приложение;3;10,00;007
ПР-5;ЗК-4;2025-01-05 09:00:00;КЛ-2;Москва;Интернет-магазин;5;20,00;
""".encode("cp1251")

CUSTOMERS = """Код покупателя,Тип покупателя
КЛ-1,Физическое лицо
КЛ-2,Организация
КЛ-3,Физическое лицо

@pytest.fixture
def store():
    s = InMemoryCsvStore()
    s.add_csv(SALES, "Продажи.csv")
    s.add_csv(CUSTOMERS, "Покупатели.csv")
