def ctx(store, question="?"):
    return ToolContext(catalog=store.catalog(), executor=None, session_id="s", question=question)


def test_csv_types_russian_headers_and_derived_periods():
    t = parse_csv(SALES, "Продажи.csv")
    assert t.schema["Количество, шт."] == "int"
    assert t.schema["Цена, руб."] == "float" and t.rows[0]["Цена, руб."] == 298.70
    assert t.schema["Дата операции (МСК)"] == "date" and t.rows[0]["Дата операции (МСК)"] == date(2024, 10, 1)
    assert t.schema["Код магазина"] == "str" and t.rows[0]["Код магазина"] == "007"
    assert t.rows[4]["Код магазина"] is None
    assert t.rows[2]["Дата операции (МСК) (месяц)"] == date(2024, 11, 1)
    assert t.rows[3]["Дата операции (МСК) (год)"] == 2024


def test_catalog_profile_and_relations(store):
    catalog = store.catalog()
    sales = catalog.table("Продажи")
    assert sales.row_count == 5 and sales.period_start == "2024-10-01" and sales.period_end == "2025-01-05"
    assert sales.column("Город").semantic_type is SemanticType.CATEGORY
    assert sales.column("Город").top_values[0].value == "Пермь"
    assert sales.column("Код заказа").semantic_type is SemanticType.ID
    assert sales.column("Дата операции (МСК)").semantic_type is SemanticType.DATETIME
    rel = [(r.left_table, r.left_column, r.right_table) for r in catalog.relations]
    assert ("Продажи", "Код покупателя", "Покупатели") in rel


def test_query_tool_runs_and_reports_engine_errors(store):
    tool = QueryDslTool(store.schema, store.sources)
    bad = QueryArgs.model_validate({
        "steps": [
            {"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": "Продажи"},
            {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "T", "group_by": ["Горд"],
             "aggregates": [{"func": "count", "column": "*", "alias": "Заказы"}]},
        ],
        "output": "A",
    })
    result = asyncio.run(tool.run(bad, ctx(store)))
    assert result.status is ToolStatus.RETRYABLE_ERROR
    assert "Горд" in result.error and "Город" in result.error

    good = QueryArgs.model_validate({
        "steps": [
            {"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": "Продажи"},
            {"var": "F", "op": "filter", "input": "T", "condition": {"and": [
                {"column": "Количество, шт.", "cmp": "ge", "value": 2},
                {"column": "Город", "cmp": "ne", "value": "Москва"},
            ]}},
            {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "F", "group_by": ["Город"],
             "aggregates": [{"func": "sum", "column": "Количество, шт.", "alias": "Штук"}]},
            {"var": "S", "op": "sort", "input": "A", "by": [{"column": "Штук", "dir": "desc"}]},
        ],
        "output": "S",
    })
    result = asyncio.run(tool.run(good, ctx(store)))
    assert result.status is ToolStatus.OK
    assert result.table.rows == [["Пермь", 12], ["Казань", 2]]
    assert result.chart_hint.x == "Город" and result.chart_hint.y == ["Штук"]


def test_top_values_with_shares(store):
    tool = TopValuesTool(store.schema, store.sources)
    args = tool.args_model(column="Продажи.Город")
    result = asyncio.run(tool.run(args, ctx(store)))
    assert result.table.rows[0] == ["Пермь", 3, 60.0]
    assert result.facts["самое_частое"] == "Пермь"


def test_chart_export_and_png(store):
    tool = QueryDslTool(store.schema, store.sources)
    args = QueryArgs.model_validate({
        "steps": [
            {"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": "Продажи"},
            {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "T", "group_by": ["Дата операции (МСК) (месяц)"],
             "aggregates": [{"func": "count", "column": "*", "alias": "Позиций"}]},
            {"var": "S", "op": "sort", "input": "A", "by": [{"column": "Дата операции (МСК) (месяц)", "dir": "asc"}]},
        ],
        "output": "S",
    })
    result = asyncio.run(tool.run(args, ctx(store)))
    from app.agent.viz import choose_chart

    spec, _ = choose_chart(result.table, hint=result.chart_hint, title="Позиции по месяцам")
    assert spec.chart_type is ChartType.LINE
    engine_spec = export_chart(spec, result.table)
    assert engine_spec["type"] == "line" and engine_spec["x"]["name"] == "Дата операции (МСК) (месяц)"
    png = render_png(engine_spec, result.table)
    assert png[:8] == b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"


def test_end_to_end_with_real_engines(store, tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "Продажи.csv").write_bytes(SALES)
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("data_question", "Сколько штук продано в каждом городе?")],
        "args_data_query": [
            {"steps": [{"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": "Продажи"},
                       {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "T", "group_by": ["Горд"],
                        "aggregates": [{"func": "sum", "column": "Количество, шт.", "alias": "Штук"}]}],
             "output": "A"},
            {"steps": [{"var": "T", "op": "source", "table": "Продажи"},
                       {"var": "A", "op": "aggregate", "input": "T", "group_by": ["Город"],
                        "aggregates": [{"func": "sum", "column": "Количество, шт.", "alias": "Штук"}]},
                       {"var": "S", "op": "sort", "input": "A", "by": [{"column": "Штук", "dir": "desc"}]}],
             "output": "S"},
        ],
        "narrative": [narrative("Больше всего штук продано в Перми: 13.", ["Пермь: 13", "Москва: 5", "Казань: 2"])],
    })
    bundle = build_agent(config=AgentConfig(), llm=llm, data_dir=tmp_path)
    r = asyncio.run(bundle.agent.handle("s1", "Сколько штук продано в каждом городе?"))
    assert r.status == "ok", r.blocks
    assert [c.status for c in r.trace.tool_calls] == ["retryable_error", "ok"]
    args_prompt = llm.calls[1][1][0].content
    assert '"Количество, шт."' in args_prompt and "aggregate" in args_prompt
    chart = next(b for b in r.blocks if isinstance(b, ChartBlock))
    assert chart.spec.chart_type is ChartType.BAR and chart.engine_spec["type"] == "bar"
    assert next(b for b in r.blocks if isinstance(b, TableBlock)).data.rows[0] == ["Пермь", 13]

    r2 = asyncio.run(bundle.agent.handle("s1", "А круговой диаграммой?"))
    chart2 = next(b for b in r2.blocks if isinstance(b, ChartBlock))
    assert chart2.spec.chart_type is ChartType.BAR and "пока не поддерживается" in chart2.note


def test_overview_end_to_end(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "Продажи.csv").write_bytes(SALES)
    (tmp_path / "Покупатели.csv").write_bytes(CUSTOMERS)
    llm = ScriptedLLM({
        "intent_decision": [intent("dataset_overview", "Что есть в этих данных?")],
        "narrative": [narrative("Загружено 2 таблицы: продажи за 2024-10-01 — 2025-01-05 и покупатели.")],
    })
    bundle = build_agent(config=AgentConfig(), llm=llm, data_dir=tmp_path)
    r = asyncio.run(bundle.agent.handle("s1", "Что есть в этих данных?"))
    assert r.status == "ok"
    text = next(b for b in r.blocks if isinstance(b, TextBlock))
    assert "2 таблицы" in text.markdown
    narrative_prompt = llm.calls[-1][1][-1].content
    assert "Код покупателя" in narrative_prompt


'backend\tests\agent\test_structured.py':
import asyncio

import httpx
import pytest
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.agent.config import LLMConfig, StructuredMode
from app.agent.llm import (
    LLMUnavailableError,
    OpenAICompatibleClient,
    StructuredOutputError,
    extract_json,
    generate_structured,
    model_schema,
    user_msg,
)
from tests.agent.fakes import ScriptedLLM


class Point(BaseModel):
    x: int
    label: str


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "text",
    [
        '{"x": 1, "label": "a"}',
        'Вот ответ:\n```json\n{"x": 1, "label": "a"}\n```',
        '<think>{"x": 2}?</think>{"x": 1, "label": "a"}',
        '{"x": 1, "label": "a",}',
        'Конечно! {"x": 1, "label": "a {скобка}"} Надеюсь, помог.',
    ],
)
def test_extract_json_variants(text):
    assert extract_json(text) == {"x": 1, "label": "a {скобка}" if "скобка" in text else "a"}


def test_extract_json_fails_without_json():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        extract_json("я не знаю")


def test_compact_schema_inlines_refs_and_keeps_title_property():
    class Inner(BaseModel):
        title: str

    class Outer(BaseModel):
        inner: Inner

    schema = model_schema(Outer)
    assert "$defs" not in schema
    assert schema["properties"]["inner"]["properties"]["title"] == {"type": "string"}
    assert "title" not in schema


def test_generate_structured_repairs_invalid_output():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({"Point": ["это не json", '{"x": "много", "label": "a"}', '{"x": 3, "label": "a"}']})
    result = asyncio.run(generate_structured(llm, [user_msg("дай точку")], Point))
    assert result == Point(x=3, label="a")
    assert len(llm.calls) == 3
    repair = llm.calls[2][1][-1].content
    assert "x" in repair and "исправленный JSON" in repair


def test_generate_structured_semantic_validation():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({"Point": ['{"x": -1, "label": "a"}', '{"x": 1, "label": "a"}']})
    result = asyncio.run(
        generate_structured(llm, [user_msg("?")], Point, validate=lambda p: ["x должен быть > 0"] if p.x < 0 else [])
    )
    assert result.x == 1


def test_generate_structured_gives_up():
    llm = ScriptedLLM({"Point": ["нет", "нет", "нет"]})
    with pytest.raises(StructuredOutputError) as e:
        asyncio.run(generate_structured(llm, [user_msg("?")], Point, max_attempts=3))
    assert e.value.raw == "нет"


def _completion(content: str) -> dict:
    return {"model": "m", "choices": [{"message": {"content": content}, "finish_reason": "stop"}], "usage": {}}


def test_client_downgrades_structured_mode_on_400():
    formats = []

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        body = __import__("json").loads(request.content)
        rf = body.get("response_format")
        formats.append(rf["type"] if rf else None)
        if rf and rf["type"] == "json_schema":
            return httpx.Response(400, json={"error": "response_format not supported"})
        return httpx.Response(200, json=_completion('{"ok": true}'))

    config = LLMConfig(base_url="http://llm/v1", backoff_base_s=0)
    http = httpx.AsyncClient(base_url="http://llm/v1/", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    client = OpenAICompatibleClient(config, http=http)
    reply = asyncio.run(client.chat([user_msg("?")], json_schema={"type": "object"}))
    assert reply.content == '{"ok": true}'
    assert formats == ["json_schema", "json_object"]
    assert client.structured_mode is StructuredMode.JSON_OBJECT


def test_client_retries_then_raises_unavailable():
    calls = []

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        calls.append(request.url.path)
        return httpx.Response(503, text="overloaded")

    config = LLMConfig(base_url="http://llm/v1", max_retries=2, backoff_base_s=0)
    http = httpx.AsyncClient(base_url="http://llm/v1/", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    client = OpenAICompatibleClient(config, http=http)
    with pytest.raises(LLMUnavailableError):
        asyncio.run(client.chat([user_msg("?")]))
    assert calls == ["/v1/chat/completions"] * 3


def test_client_recovers_after_transient_error():
    responses = iter([httpx.Response(502), httpx.Response(200, json=_completion("привет"))])
    http = httpx.AsyncClient(base_url="http://llm/v1/", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r: next(responses)))
    client = OpenAICompatibleClient(LLMConfig(backoff_base_s=0), http=http)
    assert asyncio.run(client.chat([user_msg("?")])).content == "привет"


'backend\tests\chart_tests.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from app.chart_system import (
    ChartEngine,
    get_capabilities,
    get_chart_type,
    get_chart_types,
    get_engine,
    get_full,
)
from app.chart_system.base import SortSpec, apply_multi_level_sort


SCHEMA: dict[str, str] = {
    "id": "int",
    "status": "str",
    "orders_count": "int",
    "revenue": "float",
    "created_at": "date",
    "active": "bool",
}


def test_all_charts_roundtrip_and_valid_types() -> bool:
    engine = ChartEngine()
    payloads: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {
            "type": "bar",
            "title": "Заказы по статусам",
            "x": {"name": "status", "description": "статус"},
            "y": {"name": "orders_count", "description": "количество"},
            "sort": [{"by": "y", "dir": "desc"}, {"by": "x", "dir": "asc"}],
            "limit": 50,
        },
        {
            "type": "bar",
            "title": "Заказы по активности (bool по оси X, без sort/limit)",
            "x": {"name": "active", "description": "флаг активности"},
            "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": "выручка"},
        },
        {
            "type": "line",
            "title": "Динамика по датам",
            "x": {"name": "created_at", "description": "дата"},
            "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": "выручка"},
            "limit": 1000,
        },
        {
            "type": "line",
            "title": "Динамика по числовой оси X",
            "x": {"name": "id", "description": "номер шага"},
            "y": {"name": "orders_count", "description": "заказы"},
        },
        {
            "type": "scatter",
            "title": "Связь заказов и выручки",
            "x": {"name": "orders_count", "description": "заказы"},
            "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": "выручка"},
            "limit": 500,
        },
        {
            "type": "card",
            "title": "Общая выручка",
            "value": {"name": "revenue", "description": "итого"},
        },
        {
            "type": "table",
            "title": "Полная выгрузка",
            "columns": [
                {"name": "id", "description": "ID"},
                {"name": "status", "description": "Статус"},
                {"name": "revenue", "description": "Выручка"},
                {"name": "created_at", "description": "Дата"},
                {"name": "active", "description": "Активен"},
            ],
            "sort": [{"by": "revenue", "dir": "desc"}, {"by": "id", "dir": "asc"}],
            "limit": 100,
        },
    ]

    for raw in payloads:
        spec = engine.from_dict(raw)
        if engine.to_dict(spec) != raw:
            return False
        val = engine.validate(spec, SCHEMA, row_count=40)
        if not val.ok or val.errors or val.output_schema is not None:
            return False

    return True


def test_multi_level_sort_stability_dates_and_nulls() -> bool:
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"group": "B", "score": 10, "dt": "2026-01-05"},
        {"group": "A", "score": None, "dt": "2026-01-01"},
        {"group": "A", "score": 20, "dt": date(2026, 1, 3)},
        {"group": "A", "score": 20, "dt": "2026-01-02"},
        {"group": "B", "score": 20, "dt": "2026-01-01"},
    ]

    sort_specs = (
        SortSpec(by="score", dir="desc"),
        SortSpec(by="group", dir="asc"),
        SortSpec(by="dt", dir="asc"),
    )
    sorted_rows = apply_multi_level_sort(rows, sort_specs)

    expected = [
        {"group": "A", "score": 20, "dt": "2026-01-02"},
        {"group": "A", "score": 20, "dt": date(2026, 1, 3)},
        {"group": "B", "score": 20, "dt": "2026-01-01"},
        {"group": "B", "score": 10, "dt": "2026-01-05"},
        {"group": "A", "score": None, "dt": "2026-01-01"},
    ]
    if sorted_rows != expected:
        return False

    mapped_specs = (
        SortSpec(by="y", dir="asc"),
        SortSpec(by="x", dir="desc"),
    )
    mapped_sorted = apply_multi_level_sort(
        rows,
        mapped_specs,
        key_mapping={"x": "group", "y": "score"},
    )
    return (
        mapped_sorted[0]["score"] == 10
        and mapped_sorted[1]["group"] == "B"
        and mapped_sorted[-1]["score"] is None
    )


def test_catalog_completeness_and_prompt_formats() -> bool:
    eng = get_engine()
    caps = get_capabilities()
    chart_types = get_chart_types()

    if not eng.description or len(eng.rules) < 5:
        return False
    if not caps.allowed or not caps.unsupported:
        return False

    expected_names = ["bar", "line", "scatter", "card", "table"]
    if [c.name for c in chart_types] != expected_names:
        return False

    for name in expected_names:
        spec = get_chart_type(name)
        if spec is None or spec.name != name or not spec.fields or not spec.when_to_use:
            return False

    if get_chart_type("pie") is not None:
        return False

    doc_pseudo = get_full(json_syntax=False)
    doc_json = get_full(json_syntax=True)

    return (
        "bar: x=status, y=orders_count, sort=y desc, limit=50" in doc_pseudo
        and "card: value=total_revenue" in doc_pseudo
        and "```json" in doc_json
        and '"type": "scatter"' in doc_json
    )


def test_bar_validation_boundaries_and_cardinality() -> bool:
    engine = ChartEngine()
    base_bar: dict[str, Any] = {
        "type": "bar",
        "title": "Тест границ bar",
        "x": {"name": "status", "description": ""},
        "y": {"name": "orders_count", "description": ""},
        "limit": 200,
    }

    if not engine.validate(engine.from_dict(base_bar), SCHEMA, row_count=200).ok:
        return False

    if not engine.validate(engine.from_dict(base_bar), SCHEMA, row_count=201).ok:
        return False

    if not engine.validate(engine.from_dict(base_bar), SCHEMA, row_count=5000).ok:
        return False

    bad_rc = engine.validate(engine.from_dict(base_bar), SCHEMA, row_count=-5)
    if bad_rc.ok or not any(
        e.field == "row_count" and e.reason == "invalid_parameter"
        for e in bad_rc.errors
    ):
        return False

    bad_bar = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "bar",
            "title": "Ошибки параметров bar",
            "x": {"name": "created_at", "description": "date запрещён"},
            "y": {"name": "status", "description": "str запрещён"},
            "sort": [
                {"by": "status", "dir": "asc"},
                {"by": "y", "dir": "sideways"},
            ],
            "limit": 201,
        }
    )
    val = engine.validate(bad_bar, SCHEMA)
    if val.ok:
        return False

    err_set = {(e.field, e.reason) for e in val.errors}
    expected = {
        ("x.name", "type_mismatch"),
        ("y.name", "type_mismatch"),
        ("sort[0].by", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("sort[1].dir", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("limit", "invalid_parameter"),
    }
    return expected.issubset(err_set)


def test_line_scatter_card_specific_constraints() -> bool:
    engine = ChartEngine()

    bad_line = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "line",
            "title": "Невалидный line",
            "x": {"name": "status", "description": "str запрещён для line.x"},
            "y": {"name": "active", "description": "bool запрещён для line.y"},
            "sort": [{"by": "x", "dir": "asc"}],
            "limit": 5001,
        }
    )
    line_val = engine.validate(bad_line, SCHEMA)
    line_errs = {(e.field, e.reason) for e in line_val.errors}
    if not {
        ("x.name", "type_mismatch"),
        ("y.name", "type_mismatch"),
        ("sort", "unsupported_operation"),
        ("limit", "invalid_parameter"),
    }.issubset(line_errs):
        return False

    bad_scatter = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "scatter",
            "title": "Невалидный scatter",
            "x": {"name": "created_at", "description": "date запрещён для scatter.x"},
            "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": ""},
            "sort": [],
            "limit": 0,
        }
    )
    scatter_val = engine.validate(bad_scatter, SCHEMA)
    scatter_errs = {(e.field, e.reason) for e in scatter_val.errors}
    if not {
        ("x.name", "type_mismatch"),
        ("sort", "unsupported_operation"),
        ("limit", "invalid_parameter"),
    }.issubset(scatter_errs):
        return False

    bad_card = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "card",
            "title": "Невалидный card",
            "value": {"name": "created_at", "description": "date запрещён для card"},
            "sort": [{"by": "value", "dir": "desc"}],
            "limit": 1,
        }
    )
    card_val = engine.validate(bad_card, SCHEMA)
    card_errs = {(e.field, e.reason) for e in card_val.errors}
    return {
        ("value.name", "type_mismatch"),
        ("sort", "unsupported_operation"),
        ("limit", "unsupported_operation"),
    }.issubset(card_errs)


def test_table_columns_and_sort_scope_validation() -> bool:
    engine = ChartEngine()

    empty_cols_table = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "table",
            "title": "Пустые колонки",
            "columns": [],
        }
    )
    if engine.validate(empty_cols_table, SCHEMA).ok:
        return False

    scoped_table = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "table",
            "title": "Сортировка по колонке вне списка columns и по оси x",
            "columns": [
                {"name": "id", "description": ""},
                {"name": "missing_col", "description": ""},
            ],
            "sort": [
                {"by": "revenue", "dir": "desc"},
                {"by": "x", "dir": "asc"},
            ],
            "limit": 10001,
        }
    )
    val = engine.validate(scoped_table, SCHEMA)
    if val.ok:
        return False

    err_set = {(e.field, e.reason) for e in val.errors}
    expected = {
        ("columns[1].name", "column_not_found"),
        ("sort[0].by", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("sort[1].by", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("limit", "invalid_parameter"),
    }
    return expected.issubset(err_set)


def test_structural_parse_errors_and_schema_validation() -> bool:
    engine = ChartEngine()

    non_dict = engine.from_dict(["not", "a", "dict"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    if engine.validate(non_dict, SCHEMA).ok:
        return False

    unknown_type = engine.from_dict({"type": "pie", "title": "Круговая диаграмма"})
    unknown_val = engine.validate(unknown_type, SCHEMA)
    if unknown_val.ok or not any(
        e.field == "type"
        and e.reason == "unsupported_operation"
        and e.valid_options == ["bar", "card", "line", "scatter", "table"]
        for e in unknown_val.errors
    ):
        return False

    malformed_bar = engine.from_dict(
        {
            "type": "bar",
            "title": "   ",
            "x": "status_as_string_instead_of_object",
            "y": {"name": "   ", "description": ""},
            "sort": "not_a_list",
            "limit": True,
        }
    )
    malformed_val = engine.validate(
        malformed_bar,
        {"status": "str", "bad_col": "decimal"},
        row_count=-5,
    )
    if malformed_val.ok:
        return False

    err_set = {(e.field, e.reason) for e in malformed_val.errors}
    expected = {
        ("schema.bad_col", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("row_count", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("title", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("x", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("y.name", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("sort", "invalid_parameter"),
        ("limit", "invalid_parameter"),
    }
    return expected.issubset(err_set)


def main() -> None:
    cnt = 0
    tests = [
        [test_all_charts_roundtrip_and_valid_types, True],
        [test_multi_level_sort_stability_dates_and_nulls, True],
        [test_catalog_completeness_and_prompt_formats, True],
        [test_bar_validation_boundaries_and_cardinality, True],
        [test_line_scatter_card_specific_constraints, True],
        [test_table_columns_and_sort_scope_validation, True],
        [test_structural_parse_errors_and_schema_validation, True],
    ]

    for idx, (test_fn, expected) in enumerate(tests, start=1):
        actual = test_fn()
        passed = actual == expected
        if passed:
            cnt += 1
        status = "OK" if passed else "FAIL"
        print(
            f"[{idx}/{len(tests)}] {test_fn.__name__}: {status} (got {actual}, expected {expected})"
        )

    print(f"\nPassed: {cnt}/{len(tests)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'backend\tests\query_tests.py':
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from app.query_system import QueryEngine, Table


SCHEMA: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {
    "orders": {
        "id": "int",
        "user_id": "int",
        "status": "str",
        "amount": "float",
        "created_at": "date",
    },
    "users": {
        "id": "int",
        "name": "str",
        "country": "str",
        "active": "bool",
    },
}


SOURCES: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {
    "orders": [
        {
            "id": 1,
            "user_id": 10,
            "status": "paid",
            "amount": 100.0,
            "created_at": "2026-01-01",
        },
        {
            "id": 2,
            "user_id": 10,
            "status": "paid",
            "amount": 50.0,
            "created_at": "2026-01-05",
        },
        {
            "id": 3,
            "user_id": 20,
            "status": "pending",
            "amount": 15.0,
            "created_at": "2026-01-03",
        },
        {
            "id": 4,
            "user_id": 30,
            "status": "cancelled",
            "amount": None,
            "created_at": "2026-01-02",
        },
    ],
    "users": [
        {"id": 10, "name": "Alice", "country": "SE", "active": True},
        {"id": 20, "name": "Bob", "country": "DE", "active": False},
        {"id": 40, "name": "Charlie", "country": "SE", "active": True},
    ],
}


def test_linear_pipeline_and_roundtrip() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "T1", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {"var": "C1", "op": "select", "input": "T1", "columns": ["status", "amount"]},
            {
                "var": "F1",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "C1",
                "condition": {"column": "amount", "cmp": "gt", "value": 20},
            },
            {
                "var": "S1",
                "op": "sort",
                "input": "F1",
                "by": [{"column": "amount", "dir": "asc"}],
            },
            {"var": "L1", "op": "limit", "input": "S1", "n": 1},
        ],
        "output": "L1",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    if engine.to_dict(query) != raw:
        return False

    val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
    if not val.ok or val.output_schema != {"status": "str", "amount": "float"}:
        return False

    res = engine.execute(query, SOURCES).to_dicts()
    return res == [{"status": "paid", "amount": 50.0}]


def test_leaf_conditions() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {
                "var": "F1",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "O",
                "condition": {
                    "and": [
                        {"column": "amount", "cmp": "is_not_null"},
                        {"column": "amount", "cmp": "between", "min": 15.0, "max": 100.0},
                        {"column": "status", "cmp": "in", "values": ["paid", "pending"]},
                        {"column": "status", "cmp": "not_in", "values": ["cancelled"]},
                        {"column": "status", "cmp": "like", "pattern": "p%"},
                        {"column": "created_at", "cmp": "ge", "value": "2026-01-03"},
                    ]
                },
            },
            {"var": "S", "op": "select", "input": "F1", "columns": ["id"]},
            {"var": "O_SORT", "op": "sort", "input": "S", "by": [{"column": "id", "dir": "asc"}]},
        ],
        "output": "O_SORT",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    if engine.to_dict(query) != raw:
        return False

    val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
    if not val.ok:
        return False

    res = engine.execute(query, SOURCES).to_dicts()
    return res == [{"id": 2}, {"id": 3}]


def test_logical_combinators_not_or_is_null() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {
                "var": "F",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "O",
                "condition": {
                    "or": [
                        {"column": "amount", "cmp": "is_null"},
                        {
                            "not": {
                                "and": [
                                    {"column": "status", "cmp": "eq", "value": "paid"},
                                    {"column": "amount", "cmp": "le", "value": 100.0},
                                ]
                            }
                        },
                    ]
                },
            },
            {"var": "S", "op": "select", "input": "F", "columns": ["id"]},
            {"var": "SRT", "op": "sort", "input": "S", "by": [{"column": "id", "dir": "asc"}]},
        ],
        "output": "SRT",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
    if not val.ok:
        return False

    res = engine.execute(query, SOURCES).to_dicts()
    return res == [{"id": 3}, {"id": 4}]


def test_distinct_and_null_sorting() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {"var": "S", "op": "select", "input": "O", "columns": ["status", "amount"]},
            {
                "var": "SRT",
                "op": "sort",
                "input": "S",
                "by": [{"column": "amount", "dir": "desc"}],
            },
            {"var": "ONLY_STATUS", "op": "select", "input": "SRT", "columns": ["status"]},
            {"var": "D", "op": "distinct", "input": "ONLY_STATUS"},
        ],
        "output": "D",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
    if not val.ok:
        return False

    sorted_with_null = engine.execute(
        engine.from_dict({**raw, "output": "SRT"}), SOURCES
    ).to_dicts()
    if sorted_with_null[-1]["amount"] is not None:
        return False

    distinct_res = engine.execute(query, SOURCES).to_dicts()
    return distinct_res == [
        {"status": "paid"},
        {"status": "pending"},
        {"status": "cancelled"},
    ]


def test_aggregate_and_having() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {
                "var": "A",
                "op": "aggregate",
                "input": "O",
                "group_by": ["status"],
                "aggregates": [
                    {"func": "count", "column": "*", "alias": "row_cnt"},
                    {"func": "count", "column": "amount", "alias": "non_null_cnt"},
                    {"func": "sum", "column": "amount", "alias": "total_amount"},
                    {"func": "avg", "column": "amount", "alias": "avg_amount"},
                    {"func": "min", "column": "created_at", "alias": "first_date"},
                    {"func": "max", "column": "created_at", "alias": "last_date"},
                ],
                "having": {"column": "row_cnt", "cmp": "ge", "value": 1},
            },
            {
                "var": "S",
                "op": "sort",
                "input": "A",
                "by": [{"column": "row_cnt", "dir": "desc"}, {"column": "status", "dir": "asc"}],
            },
        ],
        "output": "S",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    if engine.to_dict(query) != raw:
        return False

    val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
    expected_schema = {
        "status": "str",
        "row_cnt": "int",
        "non_null_cnt": "int",
        "total_amount": "float",
        "avg_amount": "float",
        "first_date": "date",
        "last_date": "date",
    }
    if not val.ok or val.output_schema != expected_schema:
        return False

    res = engine.execute(query, SOURCES).to_dicts()
    return res == [
        {
            "status": "paid",
            "row_cnt": 2,
            "non_null_cnt": 2,
            "total_amount": 150.0,
            "avg_amount": 75.0,
            "first_date": date(2026, 1, 1),
            "last_date": date(2026, 1, 5),
        },
        {
            "status": "cancelled",
            "row_cnt": 1,
            "non_null_cnt": 0,
            "total_amount": None,
            "avg_amount": None,
            "first_date": date(2026, 1, 2),
            "last_date": date(2026, 1, 2),
        },
        {
            "status": "pending",
            "row_cnt": 1,
            "non_null_cnt": 1,
            "total_amount": 15.0,
            "avg_amount": 15.0,
            "first_date": date(2026, 1, 3),
            "last_date": date(2026, 1, 3),
        },
    ]


def test_joins_inner_left_right_and_collisions() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    for how, expected_len in (("inner", 3), ("left", 4), ("right", 4)):
        raw = {
            "steps": [
                {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
                {"var": "U", "op": "source", "table": "users"},
                {
                    "var": "J",
                    "op": "join",
                    "left": "O",
                    "right": "U",
                    "on": [{"left_col": "user_id", "right_col": "id"}],
                    "how": how,
                },
            ],
            "output": "J",
        }
        query = engine.from_dict(raw)
        val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
        if not val.ok or val.output_schema is None:
            return False
        if "id_left" not in val.output_schema or "id_right" not in val.output_schema:
            return False

        rows = engine.execute(query, SOURCES).to_dicts()
        if len(rows) != expected_len:
            return False

    return True


def test_dataframe_and_table_sources() -> bool:
    class DummyDataFrame:
        def __init__(self, columns: list[str], data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
            self.columns = columns
            self._data = data

        def to_dict(self, orient: str = "records") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
            if orient != "records":
                raise ValueError(orient)
            return list(self._data)

    df_like = DummyDataFrame(
        columns=["id", "name", "country", "active"],
        data=SOURCES["users"],
    )
    table_obj = Table.from_source(SOURCES["orders"])

    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "U", "op": "source", "table": "users"},
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {
                "var": "F",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "U",
                "condition": {"column": "active", "cmp": "eq", "value": True},
            },
            {"var": "S", "op": "select", "input": "F", "columns": ["name"]},
        ],
        "output": "S",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    if not engine.validate(query, SCHEMA).ok:
        return False

    res = engine.execute(query, {"users": df_like, "orders": table_obj}).to_dicts()
    return res == [{"name": "Alice"}, {"name": "Charlie"}]


def test_validation_error_collection_and_condition_index() -> bool:
    engine = QueryEngine()
    raw = {
        "steps": [
            {"var": "O", "op": "source", "table": "orders"},
            {"var": "O", "op": "select", "input": "O", "columns": ["non_existent"]},
            {"var": "BAD_REF", "op": "distinct", "input": "MISSING_VAR"},
            {
                "var": "F",
                "op": "filter",
                "input": "O",
                "condition": {
                    "and": [
                        {"column": "id", "cmp": "eq", "value": 1},
                        {"column": "amount", "cmp": "gt", "value": "wrong_type"},
                        {"column": "status", "cmp": "eq", "value": "paid"},
                    ]
                },
            },
            {"var": "U_OP", "op": "pivot", "input": "O"},
        ],
        "output": "UNKNOWN_OUT",
    }
    query = engine.from_dict(raw)
    val = engine.validate(query, SCHEMA)
    if val.ok or val.output_schema is not None:
        return False

    reasons = {err.reason for err in val.errors}
    expected_reasons = {
        "duplicate_var",
        "column_not_found",
        "undefined_reference",
        "type_mismatch",
        "unsupported_operation",
        "output_not_found",
    }
    if not expected_reasons.issubset(reasons):
        return False

    return any(
        err.reason == "type_mismatch" and err.field == "condition.and[1].value"
        for err in val.errors
    )


def main() -> None:
    cnt = 0
    tests = [
        [test_linear_pipeline_and_roundtrip, True],
        [test_leaf_conditions, True],
        [test_logical_combinators_not_or_is_null, True],
        [test_distinct_and_null_sorting, True],
        [test_aggregate_and_having, True],
        [test_joins_inner_left_right_and_collisions, True],
        [test_dataframe_and_table_sources, True],
        [test_validation_error_collection_and_condition_index, True],
    ]

    for idx, (test_fn, expected) in enumerate(tests, start=1):
        actual = test_fn()
        passed = actual == expected
        if passed:
            cnt += 1
        status = "OK" if passed else "FAIL"
        print(f"[{idx}/{len(tests)}] {test_fn.__name__}: {status} (got {actual}, expected {expected})")

    print(f"\nPassed: {cnt}/{len(tests)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'backend\tests\visual_charts_tests.py':
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.chart_system import ChartEngine
from app.chart_system.base import apply_multi_level_sort, parse_sort_specs


OUT_DIR = Path("chart_plots")

SCHEMA: dict[str, str] = {
    "country": "str",
    "revenue": "float",
    "orders_cnt": "int",
    "order_date": "date",
    "customer_id": "int",
    "total_spent": "float",
    "orders_count": "int",
    "order_id": "int",
    "status": "str",
    "amount": "float",
}


BAR_DATA: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
    {"country": "SE", "revenue": 250.0, "orders_cnt": 3},
    {"country": "DE", "revenue": 180.0, "orders_cnt": 2},
    {"country": "FI", "revenue": 95.0, "orders_cnt": 1},
    {"country": "NO", "revenue": 310.0, "orders_cnt": 4},
    {"country": "DK", "revenue": 140.0, "orders_cnt": 2},
    {"country": "EE", "revenue": 60.0, "orders_cnt": 1},
]

LINE_DATA: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
    {"order_date": "2026-01-03", "revenue": 120.0},
    {"order_date": "2026-01-01", "revenue": 80.0},
    {"order_date": "2026-01-05", "revenue": 260.0},
    {"order_date": "2026-01-02", "revenue": 95.0},
    {"order_date": "2026-01-04", "revenue": 210.0},
]

SCATTER_DATA: list[dict[str, Any]] = [

    {"customer_id": 3, "orders_count": 1, "total_spent": 50.0},
    {"customer_id": 4, "orders_count": 8, "total_spent": 1120.0},

def _print_table(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], title: str) -> None:
    print(f"  {title}:")
    if not rows:
        print("    <no rows>")
        return
    cols = list(rows[0].keys())
    widths = {c: max(len(str(c)), *(len(str(r.get(c, ""))) for r in rows)) for c in cols}
    
    header = "    " + " | ".join(str(c).ljust(widths[c]) for c in cols)
    print(header)
    print("    " + "-+-".join("-" * widths[c] for c in cols))
    for r in rows:
        line = "    " + " | ".join(str(r.get(c, "")).ljust(widths[c]) for c in cols)
        print(line)
    print()


def _run_case(
    engine: ChartEngine,
    index: int,
    total: int,
    label: str,
    payload: dict[str, Any],
    data: list[dict[str, Any]],
    filename: str,
    expect_ok: bool = True,
) -> bool:
    print("\n")
    print(f"[{index}/{total}] {label.upper()}")
    print()
    print(f"  Spec: {payload}\n")

    _print_table(data, "Input Data (rows)")

    spec = engine.from_dict(payload)
    
    if spec.name in ("bar", "table") and hasattr(spec, "sort") and spec.sort:
        key_mapping = None
        if spec.name == "bar":
            key_mapping = {"x": spec.x.name, "y": spec.y.name}
        
        sorted_data = apply_multi_level_sort(data, spec.sort, key_mapping=key_mapping)

        eff_limit = getattr(spec, "limit", None) or (50 if spec.name == "bar" else 100)
        sorted_data = sorted_data[:eff_limit]
        
        if sorted_data != data:
            _print_table(sorted_data, "Data AFTER Engine Sort (what will be plotted)")

    validation = engine.validate(spec, SCHEMA, row_count=len(data))
    
    if not validation.ok:
        print(f"  Validation status: FAILED ({len(validation.errors)} errors)")
        for err in validation.errors:
            print(
                f"    [step={err.step_index}, var='{err.var}'] reason={err.reason}, "
                f"field={err.field}, value={err.value}, valid_options={err.valid_options}"
            )
        if expect_ok:
            print("\n  -> UNEXPECTED validation failure")
            return False
        print("\n  -> Expected failure. Render skipped.")
        return True

    print("  Validation status: OK")
    png = engine.matplotlib_out(spec, data)
    out_path = OUT_DIR / filename
    out_path.write_bytes(png)
    print(f"\n  -> PNG saved: {out_path.resolve()} ({len(png)} bytes)\n")
    return True


def main() -> None:
    if OUT_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(OUT_DIR)
    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True)

    engine = ChartEngine()

    cases = [
        (
            "Bar / No sort (Original order)", 
            {
                "type": "bar",
                "title": "Выручка по странам (без сортировки)",
                "x": {"name": "country", "description": ""},
                "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": ""},
                "limit": 50,
            },
            BAR_DATA,
            "01_bar_no_sort.png",
            True,
        ),
        (
            "Bar / Sort y Ascending",

            BAR_DATA,

            "Table / Sort amount Ascending",
            {

                "columns": [
                    {"name": "order_id", "description": "ID"},
                    {"name": "status", "description": "статус"},
                    {"name": "amount", "description": "сумма"},
                    {"name": "country", "description": "страна"},
                ],

                "limit": 100,

            True,
        ),
        (
            "Bar / Empty data -> placeholder",
            {
                "type": "bar",
                "title": "Пустой bar",
                "x": {"name": "country", "description": ""},
                "y": {"name": "revenue", "description": ""},
            },
            [],
            "10_bar_empty.png",
            True,
        ),
        (
            "Bar / Invalid spec (str in y) -> validation error, no PNG",
            {
                "type": "bar",
                "title": "Некорректный bar",
                "x": {"name": "country", "description": ""},
                "y": {"name": "status", "description": "str вместо числа"},
                "sort": [{"by": "country", "dir": "asc"}],
            },
            BAR_DATA,
            "11_bar_invalid.png",
            False,
        ),
    ]

    passed = 0
    for idx, (label, payload, data, filename, expect_ok) in enumerate(cases, start=1):
        ok = _run_case(
            engine=engine,
            index=idx,
            total=len(cases),
            label=label,
            payload=payload,
            data=data,
            filename=filename,
            expect_ok=expect_ok,
        )
        if ok:
            passed += 1

    print("\n\n")
    print(f"SUMMARY: Passed {passed}/{len(cases)} tests")
    print(f"PNGs written to: {OUT_DIR.resolve()}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'compose.yaml':
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:18-alpine
    container_name: mrema-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-mrema}
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-mrema}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?Set POSTGRES_PASSWORD in .env}
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:5433:5432"
    volumes:
      - mrema_postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $${POSTGRES_USER} -d $${POSTGRES_DB}"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 20
    restart: unless-stopped

  backend:
    build:
      context: ./backend
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql+psycopg://${POSTGRES_USER:-mrema}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?Set POSTGRES_PASSWORD in .env}@postgres:5432/${POSTGRES_DB:-mrema}
      MAX_UPLOAD_MB: ${MAX_UPLOAD_MB:-200}
      CORS_ORIGINS: ${CORS_ORIGINS:-http://localhost:5173,http://localhost:3000}
    ports:
      - "${BACKEND_PORT:-8000}:8000"
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  mrema_postgres_data:
